000001 | #!/bin/bash
000002 | set -euo pipefail
000003 | project_root="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../../.." && pwd)"
000004 | cd "$project_root"
000005 | [[ "$(uname -s)" == Darwin && "$EUID" != 0 ]] || { echo 'Build on macOS as the normal user' >&2; exit 64; }
000006 | case "$(uname -m)" in arm64) native_target=aarch64-apple-darwin;; x86_64) native_target=x86_64-apple-darwin;; *) exit 64;; esac
000007 | component_directory="$project_root/apps/desktop/src-tauri/components"
000008 | mkdir -p "$component_directory"
000009 | signing_identity="${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY:--}"
000010 | stage="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/switcher-native.XXXXXX")"
000011 | trap 'rm -rf "$stage"' EXIT
000012 | python3 - "$stage" <<'PY'
000013 | import json, pathlib, plistlib, sys
000014 | version = json.loads(pathlib.Path('apps/desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json').read_text())['version']
000015 | for role in ['helper', 'guardian']:
000016 |     (pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1])/(role+'.plist')).write_bytes(plistlib.dumps(dict(
000017 |         CFBundleIdentifier='local.claudeswitcher.'+role,
000018 |         CFBundleShortVersionString=version, CFBundleVersion=version)))
000019 | PY
000020 | for component in helper guardian; do
000021 |   package=switcher-helper
000022 |   if [[ "$component" == guardian ]]; then package=switcher-core; fi
000023 |   cargo rustc --locked --release --target "$native_target" -p "$package" --bin "switcher-$component" -- \
000024 |     -C "link-arg=-Wl,-sectcreate,__TEXT,__info_plist,$stage/$component.plist"
000025 |   install -m 755 "$project_root/target/$native_target/release/switcher-$component" "$component_directory/switcher-$component"
000026 |   args=(--force --options runtime --sign "$signing_identity" --identifier "local.claudeswitcher.$component")
000027 |   if [[ "$signing_identity" != "-" ]]; then args+=(--timestamp); fi
000028 |   /usr/bin/codesign "${args[@]}" "$component_directory/switcher-$component"
000029 |   /usr/bin/codesign --verify --strict "$component_directory/switcher-$component"
000030 | done
000031 | 
000032 | # The compatibility entrypoint executes this Rust CLI from the installed App.
000033 | cargo build --locked --release --target "$native_target" -p switcher-core --bin claude-switcher
000034 | install -m 755 "$project_root/target/$native_target/release/claude-switcher" "$component_directory/claude-switcher"
000035 | /usr/bin/codesign --force --options runtime --sign "$signing_identity" --identifier local.claudeswitcher.cli "$component_directory/claude-switcher"
000036 | /usr/bin/codesign --verify --strict "$component_directory/claude-switcher"

000001 | //! Desktop coordination. Remote content never supplies operations or filesystem paths.
000002 | use base64::Engine;
000003 | use std::{path::PathBuf, sync::Mutex};
000004 | use switcher_core::{
000005 |     accounts::{AccountId, AccountStore},
000006 |     ad_runtime::{AdRuntime, ClickDecision},
000007 |     checks, config, diagnostics, regions, storage, time,

000010 | 
000011 | pub struct DesktopState {
000012 |     pub root: PathBuf,
000013 |     pub edition: Edition,
000014 |     sessions: CheckSessions,
000015 |     repair_plan: Mutex<RepairPlanState>,
000016 | }
000017 | 
000018 | /// One pending UI confirmation. The request ID is not an authorization credential.
000019 | #[derive(Default)]
000020 | pub struct RepairPlanState {
000021 |     request_id: Option<String>,
000022 |     prepared: Option<regions::PreparedRepair>,
000023 | }
000024 | impl RepairPlanState {
000025 |     pub fn begin(&mut self, request_id: String) {
000026 |         self.request_id = Some(request_id);
000027 |         self.prepared = None;
000028 |     }
000029 |     pub fn matches(&self, request_id: &str) -> bool {
000030 |         self.request_id.as_deref() == Some(request_id)
000031 |     }
000032 |     pub fn cancel(&mut self, request_id: &str) {
000033 |         if self.matches(request_id) {
000034 |             *self = Self::default();
000035 |         }
000036 |     }
000037 |     pub fn complete(
000038 |         &mut self,
000039 |         request_id: &str,
000040 |         prepared: Option<regions::PreparedRepair>,
000041 |     ) -> bool {
000042 |         if !self.matches(request_id) {
000043 |             return false;
000044 |         }
000045 |         self.prepared = prepared;
000046 |         true
000047 |     }
000048 |     pub(crate) fn take(&mut self, request_id: &str) -> Option<regions::PreparedRepair> {
000049 |         if !self.matches(request_id) {
000050 |             return None;
000051 |         }
000052 |         self.request_id = None;
000053 |         self.prepared.take()
000054 |     }
000055 | }
000056 | 
000057 | pub fn completed(message: impl Into<String>) -> OperationOutcome {
000058 |     OperationOutcome {
000059 |         completed: true,
000060 |         message: message.into(),
000061 |         recovery: None,
000062 |     }
000063 | }
000064 | pub fn waiting(message: impl Into<String>, recovery: impl Into<String>) -> OperationOutcome {
000065 |     OperationOutcome {
000066 |         completed: false,
000067 |         message: message.into(),
000068 |         recovery: Some(recovery.into()),
000069 |     }
000070 | }
000071 | 
000072 | /// Recovery guidance follows the persisted transaction, not new-operation dependencies.
000073 | pub fn account_action_failure(action: AccountAction, error: impl Into<String>) -> OperationOutcome {
000074 |     waiting(
000075 |         error,
000076 |         match action {
000077 |             AccountAction::Recover => "保留账号资料，核对 Claude 退出状态和中断记录后重试恢复",
000078 |             _ => "保留账号资料，按错误提示处理后重试",
000079 |         },
000080 |     )
000081 | }
000082 | 
000083 | impl DesktopState {
000084 |     pub fn new(edition: Edition) -> Result<Self, String> {
000085 |         let root = config::home_dir()
000086 |             .map_err(|_| "无法确认本机用户目录")?
000087 |             .join(".claude-switcher");
000088 |         Ok(Self {
000089 |             root,
000090 |             edition,
000091 |             sessions: CheckSessions::default(),
000092 |             repair_plan: Mutex::new(RepairPlanState::default()),
000093 |         })
000094 |     }
000095 | 
000096 |     pub fn directory(&self) -> PathBuf {
000097 |         self.root.join("v2").join(edition_name(self.edition))
000098 |     }
000099 |     fn home(&self) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
000100 |         config::home_dir().map_err(|_| "无法确认本机用户目录".into())
000101 |     }
000102 |     fn owner(&self) -> u32 {
000103 |         unsafe { libc::geteuid() }
000104 |     }
000105 |     pub fn prepare_directory(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
000106 |         if !self.root.exists() {
000107 |             storage::create_private_directory(&self.root).map_err(|_| "无法创建私有配置目录")?;
000108 |         }
000109 |         storage::validate_no_symlinks(&self.root).map_err(|_| "配置目录包含不受支持的链接")?;
000110 |         storage::create_private_directory(&self.root.join("v2"))
000111 |             .map_err(|_| "无法创建 v2 私有目录")?;
000112 |         storage::create_private_directory(&self.directory()).map_err(|_| "无法创建发行配置目录")?;
000113 |         Ok(())
000114 |     }
000115 |     pub fn preferences(&self) -> Result<Preferences, String> {
000116 |         Ok(read_optional(&self.directory().join("preferences.json"))?.unwrap_or_default())
000117 |     }
000118 |     pub fn save_preferences(&self, preferences: Preferences) -> Result<Preferences, String> {
000119 |         if preferences.ads.slots_enabled.iter().any(|v| *v)
000120 |             && !preferences.ads.request_purpose_acknowledged
000121 |         {
000122 |             return Err("请先确认广告请求用途，再开启广告位".into());
000123 |         }
000124 |         self.prepare_directory()?;
000125 |         storage::atomic_json(&self.directory().join("preferences.json"), &preferences)
000126 |             .map_err(|_| "设置保存失败")?;
000127 |         self.log("info", "已保存本机设置；全部广告关闭时不发送广告请求")?;
000128 |         Ok(preferences)
000129 |     }
000130 |     pub fn accounts(&self) -> Result<AccountOverview, String> {
000131 |         let active = self.home()?.join("Library/Application Support/Claude");
000132 |         let status = AccountStore::inspect(&self.root, &active, self.owner())
000133 |             .map_err(|_| "账号元数据无法安全读取；请检查恢复状态")?;
000134 |         let current = status.current.map(|id| match id {
000135 |             AccountId::A => "A".into(),
000136 |             AccountId::B => "B".into(),
000137 |         });
000138 |         let profiles = [("A", status.a_saved), ("B", status.b_saved)]
000139 |             .into_iter()
000140 |             .map(|(id, saved)| AccountProfile {
000141 |                 id: id.into(),
000142 |                 state: if status.recovery_pending {
000143 |                     ProfileState::RecoveryPending
000144 |                 } else if current.as_deref() == Some(id) {
000145 |                     ProfileState::Current
000146 |                 } else if saved {
000147 |                     ProfileState::Saved
000148 |                 } else {
000149 |                     ProfileState::Uninitialized
000150 |                 },
000151 |                 login_evidence: "登录状态尚未通过真实登录验收".into(),
000152 |             })
000153 |             .collect();
000154 |         Ok(AccountOverview {
000155 |             current,
000156 |             profiles,
000157 |             claude_running: switcher_core::accounts::ensure_claude_stopped(self.owner()).is_err(),
000158 |             action_blocker: if status.recovery_pending {
000159 |                 Some("存在待恢复的账号事务".into())
000160 |             } else {
000161 |                 None
000162 |             },
000163 |         })
000164 |     }
000165 |     pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<WorkspaceSnapshot, String> {
000166 |         let mut materials = Vec::new();
000167 |         if !switcher_core::ad_runtime::local::configured(&self.root) {
000168 |             materials.push("本机广告尚未完成首次签名发布；远程广告上线未配置".into());
000169 |         }
000170 |         let preferences = self.preferences()?;
000171 |         let mut runtime = switcher_core::runtime_info(self.edition);
000172 |         runtime.ads_enabled = preferences.ads.slots_enabled.iter().any(|v| *v);
000173 |         let ads_manifest =
000174 |             match AdRuntime::new(self.root.clone(), self.edition).snapshot(&preferences.ads) {
000175 |                 Ok(manifest) => manifest,
000176 |                 Err(reason) => {
000177 |                     materials.push(format!(
000178 |                         "广告已收起：{reason}。请检查 Personal 本机运营内容。"
000179 |                     ));
000180 |                     None
000181 |                 }
000182 |             };
000183 |         Ok(WorkspaceSnapshot {
000184 |             runtime,
000185 |             accounts: self.accounts()?,
000186 |             ads_manifest,
000187 |             preferences,
000188 |             check_report: self.sessions.report()?,
000189 |             update: crate::update_runtime::UpdateRuntime::new(self.root.clone(), self.edition)
000190 |                 .snapshot(),
000191 |             pending_materials: materials,
000192 |         })
000193 |     }
000194 |     pub async fn check(&self) -> Result<CheckReport, String> {
000195 |         let session = self.sessions.begin()?;
000196 |         let config =
000197 |             config::load_read_only(&self.home()?).map_err(|_| "地区目标配置无效，请先修复配置")?;
000198 |         let report = tokio::select! {
000199 |             biased;
000200 |             () = session.wait_cancelled() => return Err("已取消本次环境检查；后台守护继续运行".into()),
000201 |             report = checks::run_checks(config) => report,
000202 |         };
000203 |         self.log(
000204 |             "info",
000205 |             &format!(
000206 |                 "环境检查完成：{} 项通过，{} 项提示，{} 项阻塞",
000207 |                 report.summary.passed, report.summary.advisory, report.summary.blocking
000208 |             ),
000209 |         )?;
000210 |         session.publish(report.clone())?;
000211 |         Ok(report)
000212 |     }
000213 |     pub fn cancel_check(&self) -> OperationOutcome {
000214 |         match self.sessions.cancel() {
000215 |             Ok(true) => completed("已取消本次检查；后台守护继续运行"),
000216 |             Ok(false) => completed("当前没有进行中的检查"),
000217 |             Err(error) => waiting(error, "检查状态不可用，请重新连接桌面核心"),
000218 |         }
000219 |     }
000220 |     pub fn migration_preparation(&self) -> Result<AccountPreparation, String> {
000221 |         let coordinator =
000222 |             switcher_core::migration::MigrationCoordinator::for_current_user(self.edition)
000223 |                 .map_err(|_| "无法确认本机迁移上下文，请核对系统用户目录")?;
000224 |         let readiness = coordinator
000225 |             .readiness()
000226 |             .map_err(|_| "无法安全核对迁移准备状态，请保留资料并检查恢复入口")?;
000227 |         let active = self.home()?.join("Library/Application Support/Claude");
000228 |         let status = AccountStore::inspect(&self.root, &active, self.owner())
000229 |             .map_err(|_| "账号元数据无法安全读取；请检查恢复状态")?;
000230 |         let action = in_place_action_for_status(&status);
000231 |         let scoped = action
000232 |             .map(|action| {
000233 |                 use switcher_core::migration::MigrationIntent;
000234 |                 coordinator.readiness_for(match action {
000235 |                     InPlaceAction::InitializeA => MigrationIntent::InitializeA,
000236 |                     InPlaceAction::AssociateA => MigrationIntent::AssociateA,
000237 |                 })
000238 |             })
000239 |             .transpose()
000240 |             .map_err(|_| "无法安全核对原位准备状态，请保留资料并检查恢复入口")?;
000241 |         Ok(with_in_place_preparation(
000242 |             account_preparation_view(readiness),
000243 |             action,
000244 |             scoped,
000245 |         ))
000246 |     }
000247 |     pub fn create_migration_backup(&self) -> OperationOutcome {
000248 |         let coordinator =
000249 |             match switcher_core::migration::MigrationCoordinator::for_current_user(self.edition) {
000250 |                 Ok(value) => value,
000251 |                 Err(_) => {
000252 |                     return waiting(
000253 |                         "无法确认本机迁移上下文",
000254 |                         "核对系统用户目录后重新预览准备状态",
000255 |                     );
000256 |                 }
000257 |             };
000258 |         match coordinator.create_verified_backup() {
000259 |             Ok(()) => completed(
000260 |                 "本机迁移备份与隔离恢复已验证；账号操作仍需完整准备门禁和 Keychain 依赖验收",
000261 |             ),
000262 |             Err(error) => waiting(
000263 |                 diagnostics::redact(&error.to_string()),
000264 |                 "保留现有资料，先处理安装、Claude 进程或待恢复事务，再重新预览准备状态",
000265 |             ),
000266 |         }
000267 |     }
000268 |     pub fn account_action(&self, action: AccountAction) -> OperationOutcome {
000269 |         let coordinator =
000270 |             match switcher_core::migration::MigrationCoordinator::for_current_user(self.edition) {
000271 |                 Ok(value) => value,
000272 |                 Err(error) => return waiting(error.to_string(), "检查本机用户目录与迁移准备状态"),
000273 |             };
000274 |         let result = match action {
000275 |             AccountAction::InitializeA => coordinator.initialize_a(),
000276 |             AccountAction::AssociateA => coordinator.associate_a(),
000277 |             AccountAction::SelectA => coordinator.select_a(),
000278 |             AccountAction::SelectB => coordinator.select_b(),
000279 |             AccountAction::Recover => coordinator.recover(),
000280 |         };
000281 |         match result {
000282 |             Ok(()) => {
000283 |                 let _ = self.sessions.replace(None);
000284 |                 let _ = self.log("info", "账号事务已完成；启动前需重新检查环境");
000285 |                 completed("账号事务已完成；登录状态仍需实际核实")
000286 |             }
000287 |             Err(error) => account_action_failure(action, error.to_string()),
000288 |         }
000289 |     }

000321 |     pub fn preview_region(
000322 |         &self,
000323 |         region: Option<RegionPreset>,
000324 |         request_id: String,
000325 |     ) -> RepairPreview {
000326 |         let original = self.region_view().unwrap_or(RegionView {
000327 |             country: "未设置".into(),
000328 |             timezone: "未设置".into(),
000329 |             locale: "未设置".into(),
000330 |         });

000333 |             .unwrap_or_else(|| original.clone());

000335 |             .and_then(|coordinator| coordinator.preview(region));
000336 |         match result {
000337 |             Ok(plan) => {

000340 |                     Ok(mut saved) => {
000341 |                         let prepared = if preview.can_execute && !preview.recovery_required {
000342 |                             Some(plan)
000343 |                         } else {
000344 |                             None
000345 |                         };
000346 |                         if !saved.complete(&request_id, prepared) {
000347 |                             return RepairPreview {
000348 |                                 can_execute: false,
000349 |                                 message: "此计划已取消或被新的预览取代".into(),
000350 |                                 ..preview
000351 |                             };
000352 |                         }
000353 |                         preview
000354 |                     }
000355 |                     Err(_) => RepairPreview {
000356 |                         can_execute: false,
000357 |                         message: "修复计划状态不可用".into(),
000358 |                         ..preview
000359 |                     },
000360 |                 }
000361 |             }
000362 |             Err(error) => RepairPreview {
000363 |                 can_execute: false,
000364 |                 message: error.into(),
000365 |                 original,
000366 |                 target,
000367 |                 network_service: "未读取".into(),
000368 |                 original_ipv6: "未读取".into(),
000369 |                 target_ipv6: "关闭".into(),
000370 |                 user_changes: Vec::new(),

000378 |     pub fn cancel_repair(&self, request_id: &str) -> OperationOutcome {
000379 |         match self.repair_plan.lock() {
000380 |             Ok(mut plan) => {
000381 |                 plan.cancel(request_id);
000382 |                 completed("修复计划已取消")
000383 |             }
000384 |             Err(_) => waiting("修复计划状态不可用", "重新连接后预览"),
000385 |         }

000387 |     pub fn recover_repair(&self) -> OperationOutcome {
000388 |         if let Ok(mut plan) = self.repair_plan.lock() {
000389 |             *plan = RepairPlanState::default();
000390 |         }
000391 |         let result = regions::RegionCoordinator::for_current_user(self.edition)
000392 |             .and_then(|coordinator| coordinator.recover_user_repair());
000393 |         match result {
000394 |             Ok(recovered) => {
000395 |                 let _ = self.sessions.replace(None);
000396 |                 completed(if recovered {
000397 |                     "修复事务已处理。请核对地区目标、系统时区与 IPv6，再重新检查环境。"
000398 |                 } else {
000399 |                     "没有待恢复的修复事务。"
000400 |                 })
000401 |             }
000402 |             Err(error) => waiting(error, "保留事务与备份，核对冲突后重试恢复"),
000403 |         }
000404 |     }
000405 |     pub fn execute_repair(&self, request_id: &str) -> OperationOutcome {
000406 |         let prepared = self
000407 |             .repair_plan
000408 |             .lock()
000409 |             .ok()
000410 |             .and_then(|mut value| value.take(request_id));
000411 |         let Some(prepared) = prepared else {
000412 |             return waiting("尚无可执行的修复计划", "先预览修复计划");
000413 |         };
000414 |         let result = regions::RegionCoordinator::for_current_user(self.edition)
000415 |             .and_then(|coordinator| coordinator.execute(prepared));
000416 |         match result {
000417 |             Ok(()) => {
000418 |                 let _ = self.sessions.replace(None);
000419 |                 let _ = self.log("info", "系统组件已执行修复并保存地区目标");
000420 |                 completed("系统修复已完成并保存地区目标；请重新检查环境与地区格式")
000421 |             }
000422 |             Err(error) => waiting(error, "核对逐项恢复结果，再重新预览"),
000423 |         }
000424 |     }

000435 |             "等待固定来源签名刷新通过；广告与软件安装暂不恢复",
000436 |         ))

000472 |         }

000477 |         ))
000478 |     }

000521 |     /// No caller-controlled path or contents. Return exactly the exported snapshot.
000522 |     pub fn export_activity(&self) -> Result<(String, Vec<ActivityEntry>), String> {
000523 |         self.prepare_directory()?;
000524 |         let directory = self.directory().join("exports");
000525 |         storage::create_private_directory(&directory)
000526 |             .map_err(|_| "无法安全创建私有日志导出目录")?;
000527 |         let entries = self.activity()?;
000528 |         let path = directory.join("activity-redacted.json");
000529 |         storage::atomic_json(&path, &entries).map_err(|_| "脱敏日志无法安全写入固定导出文件")?;
000530 |         Ok((path.to_string_lossy().into_owned(), entries))
000531 |     }
000532 |     pub fn log(&self, level: &str, message: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
000533 |         self.prepare_directory()?;
000534 |         let mut entries = self.activity()?;
000535 |         entries.push(ActivityEntry {
000536 |             observed_at: checks::unix_now(),
000537 |             level: level.into(),
000538 |             message: diagnostics::redact(message),
000539 |         });
000540 |         while entries.len() > 100
000541 |             || serde_json::to_vec(&entries)
000542 |                 .map_err(|_| "活动记录编码失败")?
000543 |                 .len()
000544 |                 > 60 * 1024
000545 |         {
000546 |             entries.remove(0);
000547 |         }
000548 |         storage::atomic_json(&self.directory().join("activity.json"), &entries)
000549 |             .map_err(|_| "活动记录保存失败".into())
000550 |     }
000551 | }

000579 | /// Project only fixed readiness blockers. Never expose Keychain metadata or selectors.
000580 | pub fn account_preparation_view(
000581 |     readiness: switcher_core::migration::MigrationReadiness,
000582 | ) -> AccountPreparation {
000583 |     use switcher_core::migration::DependencyAssessment;
000584 |     let mut blockers = preparation_blockers(readiness.blockers);
000585 |     if readiness.keychain.dependency_assessment != DependencyAssessment::HandlingPlanAvailable
000586 |         && !blockers
000587 |             .iter()
000588 |             .any(|item| item.code == "keychain_dependencies_unresolved")
000589 |     {
000590 |         blockers.push(PreparationBlocker {
000591 |             code: "keychain_dependencies_unresolved".into(),
000592 |             message: "当前 Claude 版本与 Keychain 依赖的处理方式尚未确定".into(),
000593 |             recovery: "核对当前 Claude 版本与 Keychain 依赖；处理方式确定后仍需实际登录验收。"
000594 |                 .into(),
000595 |         });
000596 |     }
000597 |     let backup_allowed = blockers.iter().all(|item| {
000598 |         matches!(
000599 |             item.code.as_str(),
000600 |             "backup_missing"
000601 |                 | "backup_stale_or_unverified"
000602 |                 | "backup_receipt_invalid"
000603 |                 | "backup_waits_installation"
000604 |                 | "keychain_dependencies_unresolved"
000605 |         )
000606 |     });
000607 |     AccountPreparation {
000608 |         ready: readiness.ready && blockers.is_empty(),
000609 |         backup_allowed,
000610 |         blockers,
000611 |         in_place_action: None,
000612 |         in_place_ready: false,
000613 |         in_place_blockers: Vec::new(),
000614 |     }
000615 | }

000617 | /// Derive the only applicable fixed action from inspected account state.
000618 | pub fn in_place_action_for_status(
000619 |     status: &switcher_core::accounts::AccountStatus,
000620 | ) -> Option<InPlaceAction> {
000621 |     if status.current.is_some() {
000622 |         None
000623 |     } else if status.a_saved {
000624 |         Some(InPlaceAction::AssociateA)
000625 |     } else {
000626 |         Some(InPlaceAction::InitializeA)
000627 |     }
000628 | }

000630 | /// Scoped readiness never changes the global directory-exchange gate.
000631 | pub fn with_in_place_preparation(
000632 |     mut view: AccountPreparation,
000633 |     action: Option<InPlaceAction>,
000634 |     scoped: Option<switcher_core::migration::MigrationReadiness>,
000635 | ) -> AccountPreparation {
000636 |     if let (Some(action), Some(scoped)) = (action, scoped) {
000637 |         view.in_place_blockers = preparation_blockers(scoped.blockers);
000638 |         view.in_place_ready = scoped.ready && view.in_place_blockers.is_empty();
000639 |         view.in_place_action = Some(action);
000640 |     }
000641 |     view
000642 | }
000643 | 
000644 | fn preparation_blockers(
000645 |     blockers: Vec<switcher_core::migration::MigrationBlocker>,
000646 | ) -> Vec<PreparationBlocker> {
000647 |     blockers
000648 |         .into_iter()
000649 |         .map(|item| {
000650 |             let recovery = match item.code.as_str() {
000651 |                 "installation_unverified" => {
000652 |                     "先完成正式系统组件安装与后台健康验证，再预览准备状态。"
000653 |                 }
000654 |                 "claude_running_or_unknown" => {
000655 |                     "完全退出 Claude；无法确认进程状态时先排查，再重试。"
000656 |                 }
000657 |                 "claude_application_unverified" => {
000658 |                     "核对官方 Claude 应用签名与版本；保留现有资料，不绕过应用核验。"
000659 |                 }
000660 |                 "association_source_mismatch" => {
000661 |                     "当前资料与已存 A 不一致；保留两个来源，不覆盖档案，先核对恢复方案。"
000662 |                 }
000663 |                 "account_recovery_pending" => "先恢复中断的账号事务，再重新预览原位准备状态。",
000664 |                 "intent_state_mismatch" => {
000665 |                     "重新读取本机账号状态；当前操作不适用于已关联或不一致的档案。"
000666 |                 }
000667 |                 "repair_recovery_pending" => "先从地区设置恢复修复事务，再重新核对。",
000668 |                 "backup_missing" => "满足安装与进程门禁后，由本人点击创建并验证迁移备份。",
000669 |                 "backup_stale_or_unverified" | "backup_receipt_invalid" => {
000670 |                     "保留原备份；重新创建并验证迁移备份，不手改收据。"
000671 |                 }
000672 |                 "backup_waits_installation" => {
000673 |                     "完成正式安装并退出 Claude 后，重新核对现有备份收据。"
000674 |                 }
000675 |                 "keychain_dependencies_unresolved" => {
000676 |                     "核对当前 Claude 版本、共享材料与设备状态的处理方式；元数据可读不等于登录状态已保留。"
000677 |                 }
000678 |                 _ => "保留现有资料，检查恢复入口；未识别状态不能视为放行。",
000679 |             };
000680 |             PreparationBlocker {
000681 |                 code: item.code,
000682 |                 message: diagnostics::redact(&item.message),
000683 |                 recovery: recovery.into(),
000684 |             }
000685 |         })
000686 |         .collect()
000687 | }
000688 | 
000689 | #[derive(Default)]
000690 | struct CheckSessionState {
000691 |     next: u64,
000692 |     active: Option<u64>,
000693 |     report: Option<CheckReport>,
000694 | }
000695 | #[derive(Default)]
000696 | struct CheckSessions(Mutex<CheckSessionState>);
000697 | struct CheckSession<'a> {
000698 |     sessions: &'a CheckSessions,
000699 |     epoch: u64,
000700 | }
000701 | impl CheckSessions {
000702 |     fn begin(&self) -> Result<CheckSession<'_>, String> {
000703 |         self.begin_initialized(|| {})
000704 |     }
000705 |     fn begin_initialized(&self, initialized: impl FnOnce()) -> Result<CheckSession<'_>, String> {
000706 |         let mut state = self.0.lock().map_err(|_| "检查状态不可用")?;
000707 |         if state.active.is_some() {
000708 |             return Err("环境检查正在进行".into());
000709 |         }
000710 |         state.next = state.next.checked_add(1).ok_or("检查代次已耗尽")?;
000711 |         let epoch = state.next;
000712 |         state.active = Some(epoch);
000713 |         state.report = None;
000714 |         initialized();
000715 |         Ok(CheckSession {
000716 |             sessions: self,
000717 |             epoch,
000718 |         })
000719 |     }
000720 |     fn report(&self) -> Result<Option<CheckReport>, String> {
000721 |         Ok(self.0.lock().map_err(|_| "检查状态不可用")?.report.clone())
000722 |     }
000723 |     fn replace(&self, report: Option<CheckReport>) -> Result<(), String> {
000724 |         let mut state = self.0.lock().map_err(|_| "检查状态不可用")?;
000725 |         state.active = None;
000726 |         state.report = report;
000727 |         Ok(())
000728 |     }
000729 |     fn cancel(&self) -> Result<bool, String> {
000730 |         let mut state = self.0.lock().map_err(|_| "检查状态不可用")?;
000731 |         let running = state.active.take().is_some();
000732 |         state.report = None;
000733 |         Ok(running)
000734 |     }
000735 | }
000736 | impl CheckSession<'_> {
000737 |     fn active(&self) -> bool {
000738 |         self.sessions
000739 |             .0
000740 |             .lock()
000741 |             .is_ok_and(|state| state.active == Some(self.epoch))
000742 |     }
000743 |     async fn wait_cancelled(&self) {
000744 |         while self.active() {
000745 |             tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
000746 |         }
000747 |     }
000748 |     fn publish(&self, report: CheckReport) -> Result<(), String> {
000749 |         let mut state = self.sessions.0.lock().map_err(|_| "检查状态不可用")?;
000750 |         if state.active != Some(self.epoch) {
000751 |             return Err("本次检查已取消或失效，未保存报告".into());
000752 |         }
000753 |         state.report = Some(report);
000754 |         Ok(())
000755 |     }
000756 | }
000757 | impl Drop for CheckSession<'_> {
000758 |     fn drop(&mut self) {
000759 |         if let Ok(mut state) = self.sessions.0.lock()
000760 |             && state.active == Some(self.epoch)
000761 |         {
000762 |             state.active = None;
000763 |         }
000764 |     }
000765 | }
000766 | 
000767 | #[cfg(test)]
000768 | mod check_session_tests {
000769 |     use super::*;
000770 |     use std::sync::{Barrier, mpsc};
000771 |     fn report() -> CheckReport {
000772 |         CheckReport {
000773 |             schema_version: 1,
000774 |             observed_at: 1,
000775 |             completed_at: 2,
000776 |             duration_ms: 1,
000777 |             network_generation: "synthetic".into(),
000778 |             checks: vec![],
000779 |             summary: switcher_domain::checks::CheckSummary {
000780 |                 passed: 0,
000781 |                 advisory: 0,
000782 |                 blocking: 14,
000783 |             },
000784 |             launch: switcher_domain::checks::LaunchDecision {
000785 |                 allowed: false,
000786 |                 reasons: vec![],
000787 |             },
000788 |         }
000789 |     }
000790 |     #[test]
000791 |     fn check_session_cancel_during_begin_initialization_cannot_be_overwritten() {
000792 |         let sessions = CheckSessions::default();
000793 |         let (ready_tx, ready_rx) = mpsc::channel();
000794 |         let (attempt_tx, attempt_rx) = mpsc::channel();
000795 |         let (done_tx, done_rx) = mpsc::channel();
000796 |         std::thread::scope(|scope| {
000797 |             let sessions = &sessions;
000798 |             scope.spawn(move || {
000799 |                 ready_rx.recv().unwrap();
000800 |                 attempt_tx.send(()).unwrap();
000801 |                 done_tx.send(sessions.cancel()).unwrap();
000802 |             });
000803 |             let session = sessions
000804 |                 .begin_initialized(|| {
000805 |                     ready_tx.send(()).unwrap();
000806 |                     attempt_rx.recv().unwrap();
000807 |                     // The cancel call is now contending with the actual begin lock.
000808 |                     assert!(matches!(done_rx.try_recv(), Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Empty)));
000809 |                 })
000810 |                 .unwrap();
000811 |             assert!(done_rx.recv().unwrap().unwrap());
000812 |             assert!(!session.active());
000813 |             assert!(session.publish(report()).is_err());
000814 |             assert!(sessions.report().unwrap().is_none());
000815 |         });
000816 |     }
000817 |     #[test]
000818 |     fn check_session_simultaneously_ready_report_and_cancel_never_leave_a_report() {
000819 |         let sessions = CheckSessions::default();
000820 |         let session = sessions.begin().unwrap();
000821 |         let ready = Barrier::new(3);
000822 |         std::thread::scope(|scope| {
000823 |             let publish = scope.spawn(|| {
000824 |                 ready.wait();
000825 |                 session.publish(report())
000826 |             });
000827 |             let cancel = scope.spawn(|| {
000828 |                 ready.wait();
000829 |                 sessions.cancel()
000830 |             });
000831 |             ready.wait();
000832 |             let _ = publish.join().unwrap();
000833 |             assert!(cancel.join().unwrap().unwrap());
000834 |         });
000835 |         assert!(sessions.report().unwrap().is_none());
000836 |         assert!(!session.active());
000837 |         assert!(session.publish(report()).is_err());
000838 |     }
000839 |     #[test]
000840 |     fn check_session_late_cancel_clears_published_report_and_rejects_late_publish() {

000843 |         session.publish(report()).unwrap();
000844 |         assert!(sessions.report().unwrap().is_some());
000845 |         assert!(sessions.cancel().unwrap());
000846 |         assert!(sessions.report().unwrap().is_none());
000847 |         assert!(session.publish(report()).is_err());
000848 |     }
000849 |     #[test]
000850 |     fn check_session_old_drop_does_not_release_new_check_and_drop_always_releases_own() {
000851 |         let sessions = CheckSessions::default();
000852 |         let old = sessions.begin().unwrap();
000853 |         assert!(sessions.begin().is_err());
000854 |         assert!(sessions.cancel().unwrap());
000855 |         let new = sessions.begin().unwrap();
000856 |         drop(old);
000857 |         assert!(new.active());
000858 |         assert!(sessions.begin().is_err());
000859 |         new.publish(report()).unwrap();
000860 |         drop(new);
000861 |         let next = sessions.begin().unwrap();
000862 |         assert!(sessions.report().unwrap().is_none());
000863 |         drop(next);
000864 |         assert!(sessions.begin().is_ok());

FILE: current/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/native_probe.rs

000001 | //! A headless installation probe runs before Tauri, account storage or lifecycle initialization.
000002 | use switcher_domain::Edition;
000003 | 
000004 | fn parse(args: &[String]) -> Result<(&str, &str), &'static str> {
000005 |     if args.len() != 3 || args[0] != "--installation-health" {
000006 |         return Err("installation_health_arguments");
000007 |     }
000008 |     for (value, length) in [(&args[1], 32), (&args[2], 64)] {
000009 |         if value.len() != length
000010 |             || !value
000011 |                 .bytes()
000012 |                 .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))
000013 |         {
000014 |             return Err("installation_health_arguments");
000015 |         }
000016 |     }
000017 |     Ok((&args[1], &args[2]))
000018 | }
000019 | 
000020 | /// None leaves ordinary startup unchanged. Probe stdout is diagnostic only.
000021 | pub fn run(args: &[String], edition: Edition) -> Option<i32> {
000022 |     if args.first().map(String::as_str) != Some("--installation-health") {
000023 |         return None;
000024 |     }
000025 |     let parsed = parse(args);
000026 |     let bad_arguments = parsed.is_err();
000027 |     let result = parsed.and_then(|(generation, nonce)| {
000028 |         switcher_core::installation_health::gui_probe(edition, generation, nonce)
000029 |     });
000030 |     println!(
000031 |         "{}",
000032 |         serde_json::json!({
000033 |             "ok": result.is_ok(),
000034 |             "code": result.err().unwrap_or("installation_health_reported"),
000035 |             "diagnostic_only": true,
000036 |         })
000037 |     );
000038 |     Some(if bad_arguments {
000039 |         64
000040 |     } else if result.is_ok() {
000041 |         0
000042 |     } else {
000043 |         78
000044 |     })
000045 | }
000046 | 
000047 | #[cfg(test)]
000048 | mod tests {
000049 |     use super::*;
000050 |     fn args() -> Vec<String> {
000051 |         vec![
000052 |             "--installation-health".into(),
000053 |             "a".repeat(32),
000054 |             "b".repeat(64),
000055 |         ]
000056 |     }
000057 |     #[test]
000058 |     fn only_exact_health_arguments_are_accepted() {
000059 |         let valid = args();
000060 |         assert_eq!(parse(&valid), Ok((valid[1].as_str(), valid[2].as_str())));
000061 |         for malformed in [
000062 |             vec![],
000063 |             vec![valid[0].clone()],
000064 |             valid[..2].to_vec(),
000065 |             [valid.clone(), vec!["extra".into()]].concat(),
000066 |         ] {
000067 |             assert_eq!(parse(&malformed), Err("installation_health_arguments"));
000068 |         }
000069 |         for (index, bad) in [
000070 |             (0, "--other".into()),
000071 |             (1, "A".repeat(32)),
000072 |             (2, "b".repeat(63)),
000073 |             (2, "/".repeat(64)),
000074 |         ] {
000075 |             let mut malformed = args();
000076 |             malformed[index] = bad;
000077 |             assert_eq!(parse(&malformed), Err("installation_health_arguments"));
000078 |         }
000079 |     }
000080 |     #[test]
000081 |     fn ordinary_startup_is_not_a_probe() {
000082 |         assert_eq!(run(&[], Edition::Personal), None);
000083 |         assert_eq!(run(&["--version".into()], Edition::Public), None);
000084 |     }
000085 | }

FILE: current/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/publisher/README.md
SHA256: 9d6d4182d26c18f6d51992f4a675e16bdfe6750d93ec689233acb713b3dadf23

000001 | # Personal 本机广告运营
000002 | 
000003 | 用户已选择先在本机运营。当前唯一实际适配器是 `LocalObjectStore`。
000004 | 它使用固定用户私有目录，不需要 R2、S3、云账号或网络凭据。
000005 | `protocol::ObjectStore` 保留一个发布接口，没有预建多云架构。
000006 | 
000007 | ## 操作
000008 | 
000009 | 1. 在 Personal 的「广告运营」编辑三个广告位。每位最多两期，UTC 排期不能重叠。
000010 | 2. 点击「生成广告签名密钥」。此明确操作把私钥存入 macOS Keychain；打开草稿不会生成密钥。
000011 | 3. 导入 PNG/JPEG，检查图片和链接，再发布到本机预览或本机正式。
000012 | 4. 本机预览保存到隔离预览目录。两个发行的普通广告区读取本机正式目录。
000013 | 5. 暂停或恢复历史后，再发布到本机正式。历史恢复生成更高序号，不回退旧清单。
000014 | 
000015 | 两个发行仍分别保存广告开关与商家确认。需要开启广告位并确认用途，才显示广告。
000016 | 本机正式发布不向其他设备或远程用户投放。远程上线仍是 WAIT。
000017 | 
000018 | ```text
000019 | Personal 草稿 ── Keychain 签名 ── 不可变版本/素材
000020 |                                     |
000021 |                            本机 current 原子 CAS
000022 |                                     |
000023 |                             签名与素材回读验证
000024 |                                     |
000025 |                    Personal / Public 各自验证后展示
000026 | ```
000027 | 
000028 | ## 固定目录与安全边界
000029 | 
000030 | - 草稿：`~/.claude-switcher/v2/personal/publisher`。
000031 | - 本机发布：`~/.claude-switcher/v2/local-ads/{preview,production}`。
000032 | - 公共验证材料：`local-ads/trust.json`，只含 schema、固定 key_id 和公钥。
000033 | - 客户端缓存：`v2/{personal,public}/ads`。Public 不创建或修改 `local-ads` 发布树。
000034 | - 私钥：Keychain。备份密码、私钥和凭据不进入命令参数、日志或普通配置文件。
000035 | 
000036 | 「公共验证材料」指同一用户的两个发行共享的公钥。发布文件仍是 0700 目录内的 0600 私有文件。
000037 | 目录、文件所有者、链接、权限和读取上限均接受校验；IPC 不接受源路径或任意 URL。
000038 | 素材与版本用同卷 `renamex_np(RENAME_EXCL)` 原子创建；既有内容不同则冲突。
000039 | `current.json` 用文件哈希作 ETag，在同一生命周期锁内比较并原子替换。
000040 | 任何提交写盘错误均视为结果未知；重试先回查同一个 release_id。
000041 | 签名清单、不可变版本与所有素材回读全部通过后，才记录成功历史。
000042 | 客户端不建立本机 HTTP 服务，不发送本机内容网络请求。
000043 | 客户端仍检查签名、channel/key_id、最高序号、UTC、素材哈希与静态图片限制。
000044 | 坏广告只收起广告并显示原因，不阻断账号工作台。
000045 | 
000046 | ## 加密备份与恢复
000047 | 
000048 | 备份采用 Argon2id 与 XChaCha20-Poly1305，包含草稿、历史、素材及 Keychain 私钥材料。
000049 | 备份文件本身加密；请保存密码并复制到安全位置。
000050 | 错误密码或不合格备份不会切换代际。恢复到未配置密钥的新草稿目录也可导入备份内的验证公钥。
000051 | 已有不同公钥时拒绝覆盖。恢复后，正式发布先回读本机正式最高序号，再补齐素材并续发。
000052 | 恢复旧备份不会降低本机已认证最高序号。
000053 | 
000054 | ## 验证与限制
000055 | 
000056 | 最新本地证据见 `docs/acceptance/evidence/e10/local-publisher-tests.log` 和 E09 的 `local-ad-runtime-tests.log`。
000057 | 测试使用真实临时文件存储及内存 Vault，不写真实 Keychain。
000058 | 测试覆盖 CAS、不可变对象、锁替换、硬链接/符号链接/宽权限拒绝、重启、两发行读取、暂停、历史、预览隔离和加密恢复。
000059 | 真实原生窗口、实际用户明确生成 Keychain 密钥后的发布，应另记现场证据。
000060 | 真实远程 HTTPS、云端权限、CDN 传播与连续 20min 在线验收仍 WAIT。
000061 | 未来只有用户明确要求远程投放后，才选择一个服务并核对其条件写、固定 HTTPS 源和授权材料。

000001 | //! Bounded password backup using Argon2id and XChaCha20-Poly1305.
000002 | use super::{Error, Result};
000003 | use argon2::{Algorithm, Argon2, Params, Version};
000004 | use chacha20poly1305::{
000005 |     XChaCha20Poly1305, XNonce,
000006 |     aead::{Aead, KeyInit, OsRng, Payload, rand_core::RngCore},
000007 | };
000008 | use zeroize::Zeroizing;
000009 | 
000010 | pub const LIMIT: usize = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
000011 | const HEADER: usize = 8 + 4 + 4 + 4 + 16 + 24;
000012 | const MAGIC: &[u8; 8] = b"CSADBK01";
000013 | const MEMORY_KIB: u32 = 64 * 1024;
000014 | const ITERATIONS: u32 = 3;
000015 | const LANES: u32 = 1;
000016 | 
000017 | fn key(passphrase: &[u8], salt: &[u8]) -> Result<Zeroizing<[u8; 32]>> {
000018 |     if passphrase.len() < 12 || passphrase.len() > 1024 {
000019 |         return Err(Error::Invalid("backup_passphrase_length"));
000020 |     }
000021 |     let params = Params::new(MEMORY_KIB, ITERATIONS, LANES, Some(32))
000022 |         .map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("backup_kdf"))?;
000023 |     let mut key = Zeroizing::new([0u8; 32]);
000024 |     Argon2::new(Algorithm::Argon2id, Version::V0x13, params)
000025 |         .hash_password_into(passphrase, salt, &mut *key)
000026 |         .map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("backup_kdf"))?;
000027 |     Ok(key)
000028 | }
000029 | 
000030 | pub fn seal(plaintext: &[u8], passphrase: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
000031 |     if plaintext.len() > LIMIT - HEADER - 16 {
000032 |         return Err(Error::Invalid("backup_size"));
000033 |     }
000034 |     let mut salt = [0; 16];
000035 |     let mut nonce = [0; 24];
000036 |     OsRng.try_fill_bytes(&mut salt).map_err(|_| Error::Random)?;
000037 |     OsRng
000038 |         .try_fill_bytes(&mut nonce)
000039 |         .map_err(|_| Error::Random)?;
000040 |     let mut header = Vec::with_capacity(HEADER);
000041 |     header.extend_from_slice(MAGIC);
000042 |     for parameter in [MEMORY_KIB, ITERATIONS, LANES] {
000043 |         header.extend_from_slice(&parameter.to_be_bytes());
000044 |     }
000045 |     header.extend_from_slice(&salt);
000046 |     header.extend_from_slice(&nonce);
000047 |     let key = key(passphrase, &salt)?;
000048 |     let cipher = XChaCha20Poly1305::new((&*key).into());
000049 |     let ciphertext = cipher
000050 |         .encrypt(
000051 |             XNonce::from_slice(&nonce),
000052 |             Payload {
000053 |                 msg: plaintext,
000054 |                 aad: &header,
000055 |             },
000056 |         )
000057 |         .map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("backup_encrypt"))?;
000058 |     header.extend_from_slice(&ciphertext);
000059 |     Ok(header)
000060 | }
000061 | 
000062 | pub fn open(bytes: &[u8], passphrase: &[u8]) -> Result<Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>> {
000063 |     if bytes.len() < HEADER + 16 || bytes.len() > LIMIT || &bytes[..8] != MAGIC {
000064 |         return Err(Error::Invalid("backup_header"));
000065 |     }
000066 |     // Exact supported parameters prevent unauthenticated memory/CPU amplification.
000067 |     for (offset, expected) in [(8, MEMORY_KIB), (12, ITERATIONS), (16, LANES)] {
000068 |         if bytes[offset..offset + 4] != expected.to_be_bytes() {
000069 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("backup_kdf_parameters"));
000070 |         }
000071 |     }
000072 |     let key = key(passphrase, &bytes[20..36])?;
000073 |     let cipher = XChaCha20Poly1305::new((&*key).into());
000074 |     cipher
000075 |         .decrypt(
000076 |             XNonce::from_slice(&bytes[36..60]),
000077 |             Payload {
000078 |                 msg: &bytes[HEADER..],
000079 |                 aad: &bytes[..HEADER],
000080 |             },
000081 |         )
000082 |         .map(Zeroizing::new)
000083 |         .map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("backup_password_or_integrity"))
000084 | }

000001 | //! The only configured publication adapter: private files on this user's Mac.
000002 | use super::{
000003 |     Error, Result,
000004 |     protocol::{self, Object, ObjectStore, StoreError},
000005 | };
000006 | use std::{
000007 |     ffi::CString,
000008 |     fs, io,
000009 |     os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt,
000010 |     path::{Path, PathBuf},
000011 | };
000012 | use switcher_core::{
000013 |     ad_runtime::local::{LocalTrust, directory, validate_directory},
000014 |     ads, signed, storage,
000015 | };
000016 | use switcher_domain::ads::AdChannel;
000017 | 
000018 | pub struct LocalObjectStore {
000019 |     root: PathBuf,
000020 |     channel: AdChannel,
000021 |     lock: storage::LifecycleLock,
000022 | }
000023 | fn failure(error: io::Error) -> StoreError {
000024 |     if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied {
000025 |         StoreError::Permission
000026 |     } else {
000027 |         StoreError::Unavailable
000028 |     }
000029 | }
000030 | impl LocalObjectStore {
000031 |     /// The application root comes from DesktopState, never an IPC argument.
000032 |     pub fn open(application_root: &Path, channel: AdChannel, trust: &LocalTrust) -> Result<Self> {
000033 |         trust.validate().map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000034 |         let root = directory(application_root);
000035 |         storage::create_private_directory(&root)?;
000036 |         let lock = storage::LifecycleLock::try_acquire(&root)?;
000037 |         for environment in ["preview", "production"] {
000038 |             let parent = root.join(environment);
000039 |             storage::create_private_directory(&parent)?;
000040 |             storage::create_private_directory(&parent.join("assets"))?;
000041 |             storage::create_private_directory(&parent.join("versions"))?;
000042 |         }
000043 |         let path = root.join("trust.json");
000044 |         match storage::read_private_bytes(&path, 4096) {
000045 |             Ok(bytes) => {
000046 |                 let previous: LocalTrust =
000047 |                     signed::strict_json(&bytes, 4096).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000048 |                 if &previous != trust {
000049 |                     return Err(Error::Invalid("local_trust_key_changed"));
000050 |                 }
000051 |             }
000052 |             Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
000053 |                 storage::atomic_write(
000054 |                     &path,
000055 |                     &serde_json::to_vec(trust).map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("local_trust_json"))?,
000056 |                 )?;
000057 |             }
000058 |             Err(error) => return Err(Error::Io(error)),
000059 |         }
000060 |         Ok(Self {
000061 |             root,
000062 |             channel,
000063 |             lock,
000064 |         })
000065 |     }
000066 |     fn path(&self, key: &str) -> std::result::Result<PathBuf, StoreError> {
000067 |         if !self.lock.protects(&self.root) {
000068 |             return Err(StoreError::Permission);
000069 |         }
000070 |         validate_directory(&self.root).map_err(|_| StoreError::Permission)?;
000071 |         let prefix = format!("{}/", protocol::prefix(self.channel));
000072 |         let suffix = key.strip_prefix(&prefix).ok_or(StoreError::Permission)?;
000073 |         let valid = suffix == "current.json"
000074 |             || suffix.strip_prefix("assets/").is_some_and(|name| {
000075 |                 name.rsplit_once('.').is_some_and(|(hash, extension)| {
000076 |                     hash.len() == 64
000077 |                         && hash
000078 |                             .bytes()
000079 |                             .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))
000080 |                         && matches!(extension, "png" | "jpg")
000081 |                 })
000082 |             })
000083 |             || suffix
000084 |                 .strip_prefix("versions/")
000085 |                 .and_then(|name| name.strip_suffix(".json"))
000086 |                 .is_some_and(|id| {
000087 |                     !id.is_empty()
000088 |                         && id.len() <= 128
000089 |                         && id
000090 |                             .bytes()
000091 |                             .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(b, b'-' | b'_'))
000092 |                 });
000093 |         if !valid {
000094 |             return Err(StoreError::Permission);
000095 |         }
000096 |         let path = self.root.join(key);
000097 |         validate_directory(&self.root.join(protocol::prefix(self.channel)))
000098 |             .map_err(|_| StoreError::Permission)?;
000099 |         validate_directory(path.parent().ok_or(StoreError::Permission)?)
000100 |             .map_err(|_| StoreError::Permission)?;
000101 |         Ok(path)
000102 |     }
000103 |     fn read(&self, key: &str, limit: usize) -> std::result::Result<Option<Object>, StoreError> {
000104 |         if limit > ads::MAX_IMAGE_BYTES {
000105 |             return Err(StoreError::Permission);
000106 |         }
000107 |         let path = self.path(key)?;
000108 |         match storage::read_private_bytes(&path, limit) {
000109 |             Ok(bytes) => Ok(Some(Object {
000110 |                 etag: signed::digest(&bytes),
000111 |                 bytes,
000112 |             })),
000113 |             Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
000114 |             Err(error) => Err(failure(error)),
000115 |         }
000116 |     }
000117 | }
000118 | impl ObjectStore for LocalObjectStore {
000119 |     fn channel(&self) -> AdChannel {
000120 |         self.channel
000121 |     }
000122 |     fn requires_credentials(&self) -> bool {
000123 |         false
000124 |     }
000125 |     fn current(&mut self) -> std::result::Result<Option<Object>, StoreError> {
000126 |         self.read(
000127 |             &protocol::current_key(self.channel),
000128 |             ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES,
000129 |         )
000130 |     }
000131 |     fn public_read(
000132 |         &mut self,
000133 |         key: &str,
000134 |         limit: usize,
000135 |     ) -> std::result::Result<Option<Object>, StoreError> {
000136 |         self.read(key, limit)
000137 |     }
000138 |     fn put_immutable(&mut self, key: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::result::Result<(), StoreError> {
000139 |         if bytes.len() > ads::MAX_IMAGE_BYTES || key == protocol::current_key(self.channel) {
000140 |             return Err(StoreError::Permission);
000141 |         }
000142 |         let target = self.path(key)?;
000143 |         if let Some(existing) = self.read(key, ads::MAX_IMAGE_BYTES)? {
000144 |             return if existing.bytes == bytes {
000145 |                 storage::sync_directory(target.parent().ok_or(StoreError::Permission)?)
000146 |                     .map_err(failure)
000147 |             } else {
000148 |                 Err(StoreError::Conflict)
000149 |             };
000150 |         }
000151 |         let temporary = target.parent().ok_or(StoreError::Permission)?.join(format!(
000152 |             ".publish-{}",
000153 |             super::random_id().map_err(|_| StoreError::Unavailable)?
000154 |         ));
000155 |         storage::atomic_write(&temporary, bytes).map_err(failure)?;
000156 |         // Same-volume exclusive rename is atomic and never exposes an nlink=2 object.
000157 |         if !self.lock.protects(&self.root) {
000158 |             let _ = fs::remove_file(&temporary);
000159 |             return Err(StoreError::Permission);
000160 |         }
000161 |         let from =
000162 |             CString::new(temporary.as_os_str().as_bytes()).map_err(|_| StoreError::Permission)?;
000163 |         let to = CString::new(target.as_os_str().as_bytes()).map_err(|_| StoreError::Permission)?;
000164 |         // SAFETY: both NUL-terminated paths remain alive; RENAME_EXCL refuses any destination.
000165 |         let result = unsafe { libc::renamex_np(from.as_ptr(), to.as_ptr(), libc::RENAME_EXCL) };
000166 |         if result == 0 {
000167 |             return storage::sync_directory(target.parent().ok_or(StoreError::Permission)?)
000168 |                 .map_err(failure);
000169 |         }
000170 |         let error = io::Error::last_os_error();
000171 |         let _ = fs::remove_file(&temporary);
000172 |         if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists {
000173 |             match self.read(key, ads::MAX_IMAGE_BYTES)? {
000174 |                 Some(existing) if existing.bytes == bytes => {
000175 |                     storage::sync_directory(target.parent().ok_or(StoreError::Permission)?)
000176 |                         .map_err(failure)
000177 |                 }
000178 |                 _ => Err(StoreError::Conflict),
000179 |             }
000180 |         } else {
000181 |             Err(failure(error))
000182 |         }
000183 |     }
000184 |     fn commit_current(
000185 |         &mut self,
000186 |         expected_etag: Option<&str>,
000187 |         bytes: &[u8],
000188 |     ) -> std::result::Result<(), StoreError> {
000189 |         if bytes.len() > ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES {
000190 |             return Err(StoreError::Permission);
000191 |         }
000192 |         let current = self.current()?;
000193 |         if current.as_ref().map(|o| o.etag.as_str()) != expected_etag {
000194 |             return Err(StoreError::Conflict);
000195 |         }
000196 |         let path = self.path(&protocol::current_key(self.channel))?;
000197 |         // An fsync error may follow the rename commit point. The caller must reconcile.
000198 |         storage::atomic_write(&path, bytes).map_err(|_| StoreError::OutcomeUnknown)
000199 |     }
000200 | }

000024 | 
000025 | pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Error>;
000026 | #[derive(Debug)]
000027 | pub enum Error {
000028 |     Invalid(&'static str),
000029 |     Io(std::io::Error),
000030 |     SecretUnavailable,
000031 |     Random,
000032 |     MissingSource,
000033 |     MissingSigningKey,
000034 |     PendingOperation,
000035 |     RecoveryReadRequired,
000036 |     Store(StoreError),
000037 | }
000038 | impl From<std::io::Error> for Error {
000039 |     fn from(error: std::io::Error) -> Self {
000040 |         Self::Io(error)
000041 |     }
000042 | }
000043 | impl std::fmt::Display for Error {
000044 |     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
000045 |         match self {
000046 |             Self::Invalid(reason) => write!(f, "发布数据无效：{reason}"),
000047 |             Self::Io(_) => f.write_str("本地发布数据读写失败"),
000048 |             Self::SecretUnavailable => f.write_str("Keychain 凭据不可用或访问被取消"),
000049 |             Self::Random => f.write_str("安全随机数不可用"),
000050 |             Self::MissingSigningKey => {
000051 |                 f.write_str("尚未配置广告签名公钥，请先导入或明确生成广告密钥")
000052 |             }
000053 |             Self::MissingSource => f.write_str("尚未配置广告托管源，未执行远端发布"),
000054 |             Self::PendingOperation => f.write_str("请先回查未完成发布，沿用已有 release_id"),
000055 |             Self::RecoveryReadRequired => f.write_str("恢复备份后，必须先回读正式最高内容序号"),
000056 |             Self::Store(StoreError::Conflict) => {
000057 |                 f.write_str("内容已被其他发布者更改，请重新读取并合并")
000058 |             }
000059 |             Self::Store(StoreError::OutcomeUnknown) => {
000060 |                 f.write_str("发布结果未知，将先回查已有 release_id")
000061 |             }
000062 |             Self::Store(StoreError::Unavailable) => f.write_str("托管源访问或公开回读失败"),
000063 |             Self::Store(StoreError::Permission) => f.write_str("托管源访问被拒绝"),
000064 |         }
000065 |     }
000066 | }
000067 | impl std::error::Error for Error {}
000068 |
