000140 | /// Keep one instance behind the desktop's mutex. The root lock excludes other
000141 | /// Personal processes. No real source or Keychain write is implicit in `open`.
000142 | pub struct Publisher {
000143 |     root: PathBuf,
000144 |     generation: String,
000145 |     state: State,
000146 |     key_id: String,
000147 |     public_key: Option<VerifyingKey>,
000148 |     _lock: storage::LifecycleLock,
000149 | }
000150 | 
000151 | fn channel_index(channel: AdChannel) -> usize {
000152 |     if channel == AdChannel::Preview { 0 } else { 1 }
000153 | }
000154 | fn random_id() -> Result<String> {
000155 |     let mut bytes = [0; 16];
000156 |     OsRng
000157 |         .try_fill_bytes(&mut bytes)
000158 |         .map_err(|_| Error::Random)?;
000159 |     Ok(bytes.iter().map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}")).collect())
000160 | }
000161 | fn valid_generation(value: &str) -> bool {
000162 |     value.len() == 32
000163 |         && value
000164 |             .bytes()
000165 |             .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))
000166 | }
000167 | fn encode<T: Serialize>(value: &T, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
000168 |     let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(value).map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("local_json"))?;
000169 |     if bytes.len() > limit {
000170 |         return Err(Error::Invalid("local_size"));
000171 |     }
000172 |     Ok(bytes)
000173 | }
000174 | fn read_private(path: &Path, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
000175 |     storage::validate_no_symlinks(path.parent().ok_or(Error::Invalid("local_path"))?)?;
000176 |     let file = OpenOptions::new()
000177 |         .read(true)
000178 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK)
000179 |         .open(path)?;
000180 |     let metadata = file.metadata()?;
000181 |     if !metadata.is_file()
000182 |         || metadata.nlink() != 1
000183 |         || metadata.uid() != fs::metadata(path.parent().ok_or(Error::Invalid("local_path"))?)?.uid()
000184 |         || metadata.mode() & 0o077 != 0
000185 |         || metadata.len() > limit as u64
000186 |     {
000187 |         return Err(Error::Invalid("unsafe_local_file"));
000188 |     }
000189 |     let mut bytes = Vec::new();
000190 |     file.take(limit as u64 + 1).read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
000191 |     if bytes.len() > limit {
000192 |         return Err(Error::Invalid("local_size"));
000193 |     }
000194 |     Ok(bytes)
000195 | }
000196 | impl Publisher {
000197 |     pub fn open(
000198 |         root: &Path,
000199 |         key_id: &str,
000200 |         public_key: Option<VerifyingKey>,
000201 |         now: u64,
000202 |     ) -> Result<Self> {
000203 |         storage::create_private_directory(root)?;
000204 |         let lock = storage::LifecycleLock::try_acquire(root)?;
000205 |         let mut public_key = public_key;
000206 |         match read_private(&root.join("trust.json"), 4096) {
000207 |             Ok(bytes) => {
000208 |                 let trust: Trust = signed::strict_json(&bytes, 4096).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000209 |                 if trust.key_id != key_id {
000210 |                     return Err(Error::Invalid("trust_key_id"));
000211 |                 }
000212 |                 let stored = VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&trust.public_key)
000213 |                     .map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("trust_public_key"))?;
000214 |                 if public_key.is_some_and(|key| key != stored) {
000215 |                     return Err(Error::Invalid("trust_public_key_mismatch"));
000216 |                 }
000217 |                 public_key = Some(stored);
000218 |             }
000219 |             Err(Error::Io(error)) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
000220 |             Err(error) => return Err(error),
000221 |         }
000222 |         let active_path = root.join("active.json");
000223 |         let (generation, state) = match read_private(&active_path, 4096) {
000224 |             Ok(bytes) => {
000225 |                 let active: Active = signed::strict_json(&bytes, 4096).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000226 |                 if active.schema != 1 || !valid_generation(&active.generation) {
000227 |                     return Err(Error::Invalid("active_generation"));
000228 |                 }
000229 |                 let state = signed::strict_json(
000230 |                     &read_private(
000231 |                         &root.join(&active.generation).join("state.json"),
000232 |                         STATE_LIMIT,
000233 |                     )?,
000234 |                     STATE_LIMIT,
000235 |                 )
000236 |                 .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000237 |                 (active.generation, state)
000238 |             }
000239 |             Err(Error::Io(error)) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
000240 |                 let generation = random_id()?;
000241 |                 let draft = AdManifest {
000242 |                     schema_version: 1,
000243 |                     channel: AdChannel::Preview,
000244 |                     content_revision: 1,
000245 |                     key_id: key_id.into(),
000246 |                     issued_at: now,
000247 |                     expires_at: now
000248 |                         .checked_add(ads::MAX_MANIFEST_LIFETIME)
000249 |                         .ok_or(Error::Invalid("draft_time"))?,
000250 |                     release_id: random_id()?,
000251 |                     slots: AdSlot::ALL
000252 |                         .into_iter()
000253 |                         .map(|slot| SlotContent {
000254 |                             slot,
000255 |                             enabled: true,
000256 |                             campaigns: vec![],
000257 |                         })
000258 |                         .collect(),
000259 |                 };
000260 |                 ads::validate_manifest(&draft, draft.channel, key_id, now)
000261 |                     .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000262 |                 let state = State {
000263 |                     schema: 1,
000264 |                     draft,
000265 |                     history: vec![],
000266 |                     assets: vec![],
000267 |                     sequences: Default::default(),
000268 |                     pending: None,
000269 |                     recovery_required: false,
000270 |                 };
000271 |                 Self::write_generation(root, &generation, &state, &[])?;
000272 |                 storage::atomic_write(
000273 |                     &active_path,
000274 |                     &encode(
000275 |                         &Active {
000276 |                             schema: 1,
000277 |                             generation: generation.clone(),
000278 |                         },
000279 |                         4096,
000280 |                     )?,
000281 |                 )?;
000282 |                 (generation, state)
000283 |             }
000284 |             Err(error) => return Err(error),
000285 |         };
000286 |         let publisher = Self {
000287 |             root: root.into(),
000288 |             generation,
000289 |             state,
000290 |             key_id: key_id.into(),
000291 |             public_key,
000292 |             _lock: lock,
000293 |         };
000294 |         publisher.validate_state(&publisher.state)?;
000295 |         Ok(publisher)
000296 |     }
000297 | 
000298 |     fn directory(&self) -> PathBuf {
000299 |         self.root.join(&self.generation)
000300 |     }
000301 |     fn persist(&mut self, state: State) -> Result<()> {
000302 |         self.validate_state(&state)?;
000303 |         storage::atomic_write(
000304 |             &self.directory().join("state.json"),
000305 |             &encode(&state, STATE_LIMIT)?,
000306 |         )?;
000307 |         self.state = state;
000308 |         Ok(())
000309 |     }
000310 |     fn validate_state(&self, state: &State) -> Result<()> {
000311 |         if state.schema != 1
000312 |             || state.history.len() > HISTORY_LIMIT
000313 |             || state.assets.len() > ASSET_LIMIT
000314 |         {
000315 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("publisher_state"));
000316 |         }
000317 |         ads::validate_manifest(
000318 |             &state.draft,
000319 |             state.draft.channel,
000320 |             &self.key_id,
000321 |             state.draft.issued_at,
000322 |         )
000323 |         .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000324 |         let mut hashes = std::collections::HashSet::new();
000325 |         for asset in &state.assets {
000326 |             ads::validate_asset(asset).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000327 |             if !hashes.insert(&asset.sha256) {
000328 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("asset_duplicate"));
000329 |             }
000330 |         }
000331 |         let mut releases = std::collections::HashSet::new();
000332 |         for record in &state.history {
000333 |             ads::validate_manifest(
000334 |                 &record.manifest,
000335 |                 record.manifest.channel,
000336 |                 &self.key_id,
000337 |                 record.manifest.issued_at,
000338 |             )
000339 |             .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000340 |             if !releases.insert((
000341 |                 &record.manifest.release_id,
000342 |                 channel_index(record.manifest.channel),
000343 |             )) || record.payload_hash
000344 |                 != signed::digest(&encode(&record.manifest, ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES)?)
000345 |             {
000346 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("history_record"));
000347 |             }
000348 |         }
000349 |         if let Some(pending) = &state.pending {
000350 |             let manifest = if let Some(key) = &self.public_key {
000351 |                 ads::verify_manifest(
000352 |                     &pending.wire,
000353 |                     key,
000354 |                     pending.manifest.channel,
000355 |                     &self.key_id,
000356 |                     pending.manifest.issued_at,
000357 |                     true,
000358 |                 )
000359 |                 .map_err(Error::Invalid)?
000360 |                 .0
000361 |             } else {
000362 |                 let envelope: signed::Envelope =
000363 |                     signed::strict_json(&pending.wire, ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES)
000364 |                         .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000365 |                 let manifest: AdManifest =
000366 |                     signed::strict_json(envelope.payload.as_bytes(), ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES)
000367 |                         .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000368 |                 ads::validate_manifest(
000369 |                     &manifest,
000370 |                     pending.manifest.channel,
000371 |                     &self.key_id,
000372 |                     manifest.issued_at,
000373 |                 )
000374 |                 .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000375 |                 manifest
000376 |             };
000377 |             if manifest != pending.manifest {
000378 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("pending_manifest"));
000379 |             }
000380 |             if pending.expected_etag.as_ref().is_some_and(|etag| {
000381 |                 etag.is_empty() || etag.len() > 1024 || etag.chars().any(char::is_control)
000382 |             }) {
000383 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("etag"));
000384 |             }
000385 |         }
000386 |         Ok(())
000387 |     }
000388 |     pub fn draft_read(&self) -> AdManifest {
000389 |         self.state.draft.clone()
000390 |     }
000391 |     pub fn draft_save(&mut self, mut manifest: AdManifest, now: u64) -> Result<AdManifest> {
000392 |         if self
000393 |             .state
000394 |             .pending
000395 |             .as_ref()
000396 |             .is_some_and(|pending| pending.phase != Phase::Conflict)
000397 |         {
000398 |             return Err(Error::PendingOperation);
000399 |         }
000400 |         let next = self
000401 |             .state
000402 |             .sequences
000403 |             .iter()
000404 |             .map(|sequence| sequence.highest)
000405 |             .chain([self.state.draft.content_revision])
000406 |             .max()
000407 |             .unwrap_or_default()
000408 |             .checked_add(1)
000409 |             .ok_or(Error::Invalid("revision_overflow"))?;
000410 |         manifest.content_revision = next;
000411 |         manifest.release_id = random_id()?;
000412 |         manifest.issued_at = now;
000413 |         manifest.expires_at = now
000414 |             .checked_add(ads::MAX_MANIFEST_LIFETIME)
000415 |             .ok_or(Error::Invalid("draft_time"))?;
000416 |         ads::validate_manifest(&manifest, manifest.channel, &self.key_id, now)
000417 |             .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000418 |         let mut state = self.state.clone();
000419 |         state.draft = manifest.clone();
000420 |         state.pending = None;
000421 |         self.persist(state)?;
000422 |         Ok(manifest)
000423 |     }
000424 |     #[cfg(test)]
000425 |     fn draft_save_bytes(&mut self, bytes: &[u8], now: u64) -> Result<AdManifest> {
000426 |         self.draft_save(
000427 |             signed::strict_json(bytes, ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES).map_err(Error::Invalid)?,
000428 |             now,
000429 |         )
000430 |     }
000431 |     pub fn pause(&mut self, slot: Option<AdSlot>, now: u64) -> Result<AdManifest> {
000432 |         let mut draft = self.draft_read();
000433 |         for content in &mut draft.slots {
000434 |             if slot.is_none_or(|slot| content.slot == slot) {
000435 |                 content.enabled = false;
000436 |             }
000437 |         }
000438 |         self.draft_save(draft, now)
000439 |     }
000440 |     pub fn restore_history(&mut self, release_id: &str, now: u64) -> Result<AdManifest> {
000441 |         let manifest = self
000442 |             .state
000443 |             .history
000444 |             .iter()
000445 |             .rev()
000446 |             .find(|record| record.manifest.release_id == release_id)
000447 |             .ok_or(Error::Invalid("history_missing"))?
000448 |             .manifest
000449 |             .clone();
000450 |         self.draft_save(manifest, now)
000451 |     }
000452 |     pub fn import_image(&mut self, bytes: &[u8], mime: &str) -> Result<ImageAsset> {
000453 |         let (bytes, asset) = ads::normalize_image(bytes, mime).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000454 |         if let Some(existing) = self
000455 |             .state
000456 |             .assets
000457 |             .iter()
000458 |             .find(|existing| existing.sha256 == asset.sha256)
000459 |         {
000460 |             if existing != &asset {
000461 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("asset_conflict"));
000462 |             }
000463 |             return Ok(existing.clone());
000464 |         }
000465 |         if self.state.assets.len() >= ASSET_LIMIT {
000466 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("asset_capacity_no_history_pruned"));
000467 |         }
000468 |         storage::atomic_write(&self.directory().join("assets").join(&asset.sha256), &bytes)?;
000469 |         let mut state = self.state.clone();
000470 |         state.assets.push(asset.clone());
000471 |         self.persist(state)?;
000472 |         Ok(asset)
000473 |     }
000474 |     fn image_bytes(&self, asset: &ImageAsset) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
000475 |         ads::validate_asset(asset).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000476 |         if !self.state.assets.contains(asset) {
000477 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("asset_missing"));
000478 |         }
000479 |         let bytes = read_private(
000480 |             &self.directory().join("assets").join(&asset.sha256),
000481 |             ads::MAX_IMAGE_BYTES,
000482 |         )?;
000483 |         ads::validate_image(&bytes, asset).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000484 |         Ok(bytes)
000485 |     }
000486 |     pub fn image_preview(&self, sha256: &str) -> Result<String> {
000487 |         let asset = self
000488 |             .state
000489 |             .assets
000490 |             .iter()
000491 |             .find(|asset| asset.sha256 == sha256)
000492 |             .ok_or(Error::Invalid("asset_missing"))?;
000493 |         let bytes = self.image_bytes(asset)?;
000494 |         use base64::Engine;
000495 |         let result = format!(
000496 |             "data:{};base64,{}",
000497 |             asset.mime,
000498 |             base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(bytes)
000499 |         );
000500 |         Ok(result)
000501 |     }
000502 |     pub fn status(&self) -> Status {
000503 |         Status {
000504 |             phase: self.state.pending.as_ref().map(|pending| pending.phase),
000505 |             pending_release_id: self
000506 |                 .state
000507 |                 .pending
000508 |                 .as_ref()
000509 |                 .map(|pending| pending.manifest.release_id.clone()),
000510 |             recovery_required: self.state.recovery_required,
000511 |             history: self.state.history.clone(),
000512 |         }
000513 |     }
000514 |     pub fn credentials_save(&mut self, vault: &mut dyn Vault, secrets: &Secrets) -> Result<()> {
000515 |         let signer = self.check_signer(secrets)?;
000516 |         vault.store(&self.generation, secrets)?;
000517 |         storage::atomic_write(
000518 |             &self.root.join("trust.json"),
000519 |             &encode(
000520 |                 &Trust {
000521 |                     key_id: self.key_id.clone(),
000522 |                     public_key: signer.verifying_key().to_bytes(),
000523 |                 },
000524 |                 4096,
000525 |             )?,
000526 |         )?;
000527 |         self.public_key = Some(signer.verifying_key());
000528 |         Ok(())
000529 |     }
000530 |     /// Call only for an explicit operator action. Opening drafts never generates a key.
000531 |     pub fn generate_signing_key(
000532 |         &mut self,
000533 |         vault: &mut dyn Vault,
000534 |         preview_credential: Vec<u8>,
000535 |         production_credential: Vec<u8>,
000536 |     ) -> Result<[u8; 32]> {
000537 |         if self.public_key.is_some() {
000538 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("signing_key_already_configured"));
000539 |         }
000540 |         let mut secrets = Secrets {
000541 |             signing_seed: vec![0; 32],
000542 |             preview_credential,
000543 |             production_credential,
000544 |         };
000545 |         OsRng
000546 |             .try_fill_bytes(&mut secrets.signing_seed)
000547 |             .map_err(|_| Error::Random)?;
000548 |         self.credentials_save(vault, &secrets)?;
000549 |         Ok(self.verifying_key()?.to_bytes())
000550 |     }
000551 |     pub fn configured_public_key(&self) -> Option<[u8; 32]> {
000552 |         self.public_key.map(|key| key.to_bytes())
000553 |     }
000554 |     fn verifying_key(&self) -> Result<&VerifyingKey> {
000555 |         self.public_key.as_ref().ok_or(Error::MissingSigningKey)
000556 |     }
000557 |     fn check_signer(&self, secrets: &Secrets) -> Result<SigningKey> {
000558 |         secrets.validate()?;
000559 |         let seed: &[u8; 32] = secrets
000560 |             .signing_seed
000561 |             .as_slice()
000562 |             .try_into()
000563 |             .map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("signing_seed"))?;
000564 |         let signer = SigningKey::from_bytes(seed);
000565 |         if self
000566 |             .public_key
000567 |             .is_some_and(|key| signer.verifying_key() != key)
000568 |         {
000569 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("signing_public_key_mismatch"));
000570 |         }
000571 |         Ok(signer)
000572 |     }
000573 | 
000574 |     pub fn publish(
000575 |         &mut self,
000576 |         channel: AdChannel,
000577 |         now: u64,
000578 |         source: Option<&mut dyn ObjectStore>,
000579 |         vault: &dyn Vault,
000580 |     ) -> Result<Publication> {
000581 |         let source = source.ok_or(Error::MissingSource)?;
000582 |         self.publish_with(source, vault, channel, now)
000583 |     }
000584 |     fn read_remote(
000585 |         &mut self,
000586 |         source: &mut dyn ObjectStore,
000587 |         now: u64,
000588 |     ) -> Result<Option<protocol::Object>> {
000589 |         let current = source.current()?;
000590 |         let channel = source.channel();
000591 |         let mut state = self.state.clone();
000592 |         if let Some(object) = &current {
000593 |             if object.etag.is_empty()
000594 |                 || object.etag.len() > 1024
000595 |                 || object.etag.chars().any(char::is_control)
000596 |             {
000597 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("etag"));
000598 |             }
000599 |             let (manifest, hash): (AdManifest, String) = signed::verify(
000600 |                 &object.bytes,
000601 |                 self.verifying_key()?,
000602 |                 ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES,
000603 |             )
000604 |             .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000605 |             // Expired current still authenticates its replay watermark, never display.
000606 |             ads::validate_manifest(&manifest, channel, &self.key_id, manifest.issued_at)
000607 |                 .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000608 |             if manifest.issued_at > now {
000609 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("remote_clock"));
000610 |             }
000611 |             state.sequences[channel_index(channel)]
000612 |                 .remember(manifest.content_revision, hash)
000613 |                 .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000614 |         } else if state.sequences[channel_index(channel)].highest != 0 {
000615 |             return Err(Error::Invalid(
000616 |                 "remote_current_missing_after_authenticated_release",
000617 |             ));
000618 |         }
000619 |         if channel == AdChannel::Production {
000620 |             state.recovery_required = false;
000621 |         }
000622 |         self.persist(state)?;
000623 |         Ok(current)
000624 |     }
000625 |     // Production publishing performs this read through publish_with. The test
000626 |     // entry isolates recovery's watermark gate without publishing a new draft.
000627 |     #[cfg(test)]
000628 |     fn reconcile_production(&mut self, source: &mut dyn ObjectStore, now: u64) -> Result<()> {
000629 |         protocol::require_scope(source, AdChannel::Production)?;
000630 |         self.read_remote(source, now)?;
000631 |         Ok(())
000632 |     }
000633 |     pub fn publish_with(
000634 |         &mut self,
000635 |         source: &mut dyn ObjectStore,
000636 |         vault: &dyn Vault,
000637 |         channel: AdChannel,
000638 |         now: u64,
000639 |     ) -> Result<Publication> {
000640 |         protocol::require_scope(source, channel)?;
000641 |         self.verifying_key()?;
000642 |         self.validate_state(&self.state)?;
000643 |         if self.state.recovery_required && channel != AdChannel::Production {
000644 |             return Err(Error::RecoveryReadRequired);
000645 |         }
000646 |         if self.state.recovery_required {
000647 |             self.read_remote(source, now)?;
000648 |         }
000649 |         if self.state.pending.is_none() {
000650 |             if self.state.history.len() >= HISTORY_LIMIT {
000651 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("history_capacity_no_history_pruned"));
000652 |             }
000653 |             let current = self.read_remote(source, now)?;
000654 |             if let Some(object) = &current {
000655 |                 let (manifest, _): (AdManifest, String) = signed::verify(
000656 |                     &object.bytes,
000657 |                     self.verifying_key()?,
000658 |                     ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES,
000659 |                 )
000660 |                 .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000661 |                 if manifest.expires_at > now
000662 |                     && manifest == self.state.draft
000663 |                     && self
000664 |                         .state
000665 |                         .history
000666 |                         .iter()
000667 |                         .any(|record| record.manifest == manifest)
000668 |                 {
000669 |                     let mut state = self.state.clone();
000670 |                     state.pending = Some(Pending {
000671 |                         manifest,
000672 |                         wire: object.bytes.clone(),
000673 |                         expected_etag: Some(object.etag.clone()),
000674 |                         phase: Phase::ReadbackFailed,
000675 |                     });
000676 |                     self.persist(state)?;
000677 |                     return self.finish_readback(source, now);
000678 |                 }
000679 |             }
000680 |             let secrets = vault.load(&self.generation)?;
000681 |             let signer = self.check_signer(&secrets)?;
000682 |             let credential = if channel == AdChannel::Preview {
000683 |                 &secrets.preview_credential
000684 |             } else {
000685 |                 &secrets.production_credential
000686 |             };
000687 |             if source.requires_credentials() && credential.is_empty() {
000688 |                 return Err(Error::SecretUnavailable);
000689 |             }
000690 |             let mut manifest = self.draft_read();
000691 |             manifest.channel = channel;
000692 |             if self.state.history.iter().any(|record| {
000693 |                 record.manifest.release_id == manifest.release_id
000694 |                     && record.manifest.channel == channel
000695 |             }) {
000696 |                 manifest.release_id = random_id()?;
000697 |             }
000698 |             manifest.content_revision = manifest.content_revision.max(
000699 |                 self.state.sequences[channel_index(channel)]
000700 |                     .highest
000701 |                     .checked_add(1)
000702 |                     .ok_or(Error::Invalid("revision_overflow"))?,
000703 |             );
000704 |             manifest.issued_at = now;
000705 |             manifest.expires_at = now
000706 |                 .checked_add(ads::MAX_MANIFEST_LIFETIME)
000707 |                 .ok_or(Error::Invalid("draft_time"))?;
000708 |             ads::validate_manifest(&manifest, channel, &self.key_id, now)
000709 |                 .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000710 |             for campaign in manifest.slots.iter().flat_map(|slot| &slot.campaigns) {
000711 |                 self.image_bytes(&campaign.image)?;
000712 |             }
000713 |             let payload = String::from_utf8(encode(&manifest, ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES)?)
000714 |                 .map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("manifest_utf8"))?;
000715 |             let signature = signer.sign(payload.as_bytes()).to_bytes().to_vec();
000716 |             let wire = encode(
000717 |                 &signed::Envelope { payload, signature },
000718 |                 ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES,
000719 |             )?;
000720 |             let mut state = self.state.clone();
000721 |             state.pending = Some(Pending {
000722 |                 manifest,
000723 |                 wire,
000724 |                 expected_etag: current.map(|object| object.etag),
000725 |                 phase: Phase::Prepared,
000726 |             });
000727 |             self.persist(state)?;
000728 |         }
000729 |         let pending = self
000730 |             .state
000731 |             .pending
000732 |             .clone()
000733 |             .ok_or(Error::Invalid("pending_missing"))?;
000734 |         protocol::require_scope(source, pending.manifest.channel)?;
000735 |         if pending.manifest.channel != channel {
000736 |             return Err(Error::PendingOperation);
000737 |         }
000738 |         if pending.phase == Phase::Conflict {
000739 |             return Err(Error::Store(StoreError::Conflict));
000740 |         }
000741 |         let public_current =
000742 |             source.public_read(&protocol::current_key(channel), ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES)?;
000743 |         if protocol::same_object(&public_current, &pending.wire) {
000744 |             return self.finish_readback(source, now);
000745 |         }
000746 |         if matches!(pending.phase, Phase::CommitUnknown | Phase::ReadbackFailed) {
000747 |             // Resolve the ambiguous authenticated commit before another write.
000748 |             let current = source.current()?;
000749 |             if protocol::same_object(&current, &pending.wire) {
000750 |                 return self.finish_readback(source, now);
000751 |             }
000752 |             if current.as_ref().map(|object| &object.etag) != pending.expected_etag.as_ref() {
000753 |                 self.set_phase(Phase::Conflict)?;
000754 |                 return Err(Error::Store(StoreError::Conflict));
000755 |             }
000756 |             let version = source.public_read(
000757 |                 &protocol::version_key(&pending.manifest),
000758 |                 ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES,
000759 |             )?;
000760 |             if !protocol::same_object(&version, &pending.wire) {
000761 |                 return Err(Error::Store(StoreError::OutcomeUnknown));
000762 |             }
000763 |         }
000764 |         self.set_phase(Phase::Uploading)?;
000765 |         for campaign in pending
000766 |             .manifest
000767 |             .slots
000768 |             .iter()
000769 |             .flat_map(|slot| &slot.campaigns)
000770 |         {
000771 |             let bytes = self.image_bytes(&campaign.image)?;
000772 |             let key = protocol::asset_key(channel, &campaign.image);
000773 |             source.put_immutable(&key, &bytes)?;
000774 |         }
000775 |         source.put_immutable(&protocol::version_key(&pending.manifest), &pending.wire)?;
000776 |         // Persist the uncertain state BEFORE sending the CAS request.
000777 |         self.set_phase(Phase::CommitUnknown)?;
000778 |         match source.commit_current(pending.expected_etag.as_deref(), &pending.wire) {
000779 |             Ok(()) => self.finish_readback(source, now),
000780 |             Err(StoreError::Conflict) => {
000781 |                 self.set_phase(Phase::Conflict)?;
000782 |                 Err(Error::Store(StoreError::Conflict))
000783 |             }
000784 |             Err(error) => Err(Error::Store(error)),
000785 |         }
000786 |     }
000787 |     fn set_phase(&mut self, phase: Phase) -> Result<()> {
000788 |         let mut state = self.state.clone();
000789 |         state
000790 |             .pending
000791 |             .as_mut()
000792 |             .ok_or(Error::Invalid("pending_missing"))?
000793 |             .phase = phase;
000794 |         self.persist(state)
000795 |     }
000796 |     fn finish_readback(&mut self, source: &mut dyn ObjectStore, now: u64) -> Result<Publication> {
000797 |         let result = self.verify_public_readback(source, now);
000798 |         if result.is_err() {
000799 |             self.set_phase(Phase::ReadbackFailed)?;
000800 |         }
000801 |         result
000802 |     }
000803 |     fn verify_public_readback(
000804 |         &mut self,
000805 |         source: &mut dyn ObjectStore,
000806 |         now: u64,
000807 |     ) -> Result<Publication> {
000808 |         let pending = self
000809 |             .state
000810 |             .pending
000811 |             .as_ref()
000812 |             .ok_or(Error::Invalid("pending_missing"))?;
000813 |         let current = source.public_read(
000814 |             &protocol::current_key(pending.manifest.channel),
000815 |             ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES,
000816 |         )?;
000817 |         let current = current.ok_or(Error::Store(StoreError::Unavailable))?;
000818 |         if current.bytes != pending.wire {
000819 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("public_current_readback"));
000820 |         }
000821 |         let (manifest, hash) = ads::verify_manifest(
000822 |             &current.bytes,
000823 |             self.verifying_key()?,
000824 |             pending.manifest.channel,
000825 |             &self.key_id,
000826 |             now,
000827 |             true,
000828 |         )
000829 |         .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000830 |         if manifest != pending.manifest {
000831 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("readback_release"));
000832 |         }
000833 |         let version = source
000834 |             .public_read(&protocol::version_key(&manifest), ads::MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES)?
000835 |             .ok_or(Error::Store(StoreError::Unavailable))?;
000836 |         if version.bytes != pending.wire {
000837 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("public_version_readback"));
000838 |         }
000839 |         for campaign in manifest.slots.iter().flat_map(|slot| &slot.campaigns) {
000840 |             let object = source
000841 |                 .public_read(
000842 |                     &protocol::asset_key(manifest.channel, &campaign.image),
000843 |                     ads::MAX_IMAGE_BYTES,
000844 |                 )?
000845 |                 .ok_or(Error::Store(StoreError::Unavailable))?;
000846 |             ads::validate_image(&object.bytes, &campaign.image).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000847 |         }
000848 |         let outcome = Publication {
000849 |             release_id: manifest.release_id.clone(),
000850 |             revision: manifest.content_revision,
000851 |             channel: manifest.channel,
000852 |         };
000853 |         let mut state = self.state.clone();
000854 |         state.sequences[channel_index(manifest.channel)]
000855 |             .remember(manifest.content_revision, hash.clone())
000856 |             .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000857 |         if !state
000858 |             .history
000859 |             .iter()
000860 |             .any(|record| record.manifest == manifest)
000861 |         {
000862 |             state.history.push(HistoryRecord {
000863 |                 manifest: manifest.clone(),
000864 |                 published_at: now,
000865 |                 payload_hash: hash,
000866 |             });
000867 |         }
000868 |         state.draft = manifest;
000869 |         state.pending = None;
000870 |         self.persist(state)?;
000871 |         Ok(outcome)
000872 |     }
000873 | 
000874 |     pub fn export_backup(&self, passphrase: &[u8], vault: &dyn Vault) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
000875 |         let secrets = vault.load(&self.generation)?;
000876 |         self.check_signer(&secrets)?;
000877 |         let assets = self
000878 |             .state
000879 |             .assets
000880 |             .iter()
000881 |             .map(|asset| {
000882 |                 self.image_bytes(asset).map(|bytes| BackupAsset {
000883 |                     asset: asset.clone(),
000884 |                     bytes,
000885 |                 })
000886 |             })
000887 |             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>>>()?;
000888 |         let snapshot = Snapshot {
000889 |             schema: 1,
000890 |             key_id: self.key_id.clone(),
000891 |             public_key: self.verifying_key()?.to_bytes(),
000892 |             state: self.state.clone(),
000893 |             assets,
000894 |             secrets,
000895 |         };
000896 |         let plaintext = Zeroizing::new(encode(&snapshot, backup::LIMIT - 76)?);
000897 |         backup::seal(&plaintext, passphrase)
000898 |     }
000899 |     pub fn restore_backup(
000900 |         &mut self,
000901 |         bytes: &[u8],
000902 |         passphrase: &[u8],
000903 |         vault: &mut dyn Vault,
000904 |     ) -> Result<()> {
000905 |         if self
000906 |             .state
000907 |             .pending
000908 |             .as_ref()
000909 |             .is_some_and(|pending| pending.phase != Phase::Conflict)
000910 |         {
000911 |             return Err(Error::PendingOperation);
000912 |         }
000913 |         let plaintext = backup::open(bytes, passphrase)?;
000914 |         let mut snapshot: Snapshot =
000915 |             signed::strict_json(&plaintext, backup::LIMIT).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000916 |         if snapshot.schema != 1
000917 |             || snapshot.key_id != self.key_id
000918 |             || self
000919 |                 .public_key
000920 |                 .is_some_and(|key| snapshot.public_key != key.to_bytes())
000921 |         {
000922 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("backup_scope"));
000923 |         }
000924 |         let restored_key = VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&snapshot.public_key)
000925 |             .map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("backup_public_key"))?;
000926 |         if self.check_signer(&snapshot.secrets)?.verifying_key() != restored_key {
000927 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("backup_secret_key_mismatch"));
000928 |         }
000929 |         if let Some(pending) = &snapshot.state.pending {
000930 |             ads::verify_manifest(
000931 |                 &pending.wire,
000932 |                 &restored_key,
000933 |                 pending.manifest.channel,
000934 |                 &self.key_id,
000935 |                 pending.manifest.issued_at,
000936 |                 true,
000937 |             )
000938 |             .map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000939 |         }
000940 |         self.validate_state(&snapshot.state)?;
000941 |         if snapshot.assets.len() != snapshot.state.assets.len() {
000942 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("backup_asset_index"));
000943 |         }
000944 |         let mut hashes = std::collections::HashSet::new();
000945 |         for asset in &snapshot.assets {
000946 |             if !snapshot.state.assets.contains(&asset.asset) || !hashes.insert(&asset.asset.sha256)
000947 |             {
000948 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("backup_asset_index"));
000949 |             }
000950 |             ads::validate_image(&asset.bytes, &asset.asset).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000951 |         }
000952 |         // Keep the local replay watermark when restoring an older backup.
000953 |         for index in 0..2 {
000954 |             let local = &self.state.sequences[index];
000955 |             if local.highest >= snapshot.state.sequences[index].highest {
000956 |                 snapshot.state.sequences[index] = local.clone();
000957 |             }
000958 |         }
000959 |         snapshot.state.recovery_required = true;
000960 |         if snapshot.state.pending.is_none() {
000961 |             // A previous installation may already have used this draft's ID.
000962 |             snapshot.state.draft.release_id = random_id()?;
000963 |             snapshot.state.draft.content_revision = snapshot
000964 |                 .state
000965 |                 .sequences
000966 |                 .iter()
000967 |                 .map(|sequence| sequence.highest)
000968 |                 .chain([snapshot.state.draft.content_revision])
000969 |                 .max()
000970 |                 .unwrap_or_default()
000971 |                 .checked_add(1)
000972 |                 .ok_or(Error::Invalid("revision_overflow"))?;
000973 |         }
000974 |         self.validate_state(&snapshot.state)?;
000975 |         let generation = random_id()?;
000976 |         Self::write_generation(&self.root, &generation, &snapshot.state, &snapshot.assets)?;
000977 |         // Both old active state and old Keychain item remain valid on any failure.
000978 |         // New files and the new secret item become reachable in one pointer rename.
000979 |         vault.store(&generation, &snapshot.secrets)?;
000980 |         if self.public_key.is_none() {
000981 |             storage::atomic_write(
000982 |                 &self.root.join("trust.json"),
000983 |                 &encode(
000984 |                     &Trust {
000985 |                         key_id: self.key_id.clone(),
000986 |                         public_key: restored_key.to_bytes(),
000987 |                     },
000988 |                     4096,
000989 |                 )?,
000990 |             )?;
000991 |             self.public_key = Some(restored_key);
000992 |         }
000993 |         let active = encode(
000994 |             &Active {
000995 |                 schema: 1,
000996 |                 generation: generation.clone(),
000997 |             },
000998 |             4096,
000999 |         )?;
001000 |         let committed = storage::atomic_write(&self.root.join("active.json"), &active);
001001 |         // Directory sync may fail after the rename commit point. Match the
001002 |         // on-disk generation in memory while preserving the durability error.
001003 |         if committed.is_ok()
001004 |             || read_private(&self.root.join("active.json"), 4096).is_ok_and(|bytes| bytes == active)
001005 |         {
001006 |             self.generation = generation;
001007 |             self.state = snapshot.state;
001008 |         }
001009 |         committed.map_err(Error::Io)
001010 |     }
001011 |     fn write_generation(
001012 |         root: &Path,
001013 |         generation: &str,
001014 |         state: &State,
001015 |         assets: &[BackupAsset],
001016 |     ) -> Result<()> {
001017 |         let directory = root.join(generation);
001018 |         storage::create_private_directory(&directory)?;
001019 |         let asset_directory = directory.join("assets");
001020 |         storage::create_private_directory(&asset_directory)?;
001021 |         for asset in assets {
001022 |             storage::atomic_write(&asset_directory.join(&asset.asset.sha256), &asset.bytes)?;
001023 |         }
001024 |         storage::atomic_write(&directory.join("state.json"), &encode(state, STATE_LIMIT)?)?;
001025 |         storage::sync_directory(root)?;
001026 |         Ok(())
001027 |     }
001028 | }

001031 | mod tests;

000001 | //! One-provider publication protocol; the actual provider is configured separately.
000002 | use super::{Error, Result};
000003 | use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
000004 | use switcher_domain::ads::{AdChannel, AdManifest};
000005 | 
000006 | #[derive(Clone)]
000007 | pub struct Object {
000008 |     pub bytes: Vec<u8>,
000009 |     pub etag: String,
000010 | }
000011 | 
000012 | #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
000013 | pub enum StoreError {
000014 |     Unavailable,
000015 |     Conflict,
000016 |     OutcomeUnknown,
000017 |     Permission,
000018 | }
000019 | 
000020 | /// All keys are product-relative and environment-prefixed, never user URLs.
000021 | /// A provider must enforce bounded reads, anonymous public readback, immutable
000022 | /// object create-if-absent, and CAS via If-Match / If-None-Match. Its credential
000023 | /// must have write access only to this environment's advertisement directory.
000024 | pub trait ObjectStore {
000025 |     fn channel(&self) -> AdChannel;
000026 |     fn requires_credentials(&self) -> bool {
000027 |         true
000028 |     }
000029 |     fn current(&mut self) -> std::result::Result<Option<Object>, StoreError>;
000030 |     fn public_read(
000031 |         &mut self,
000032 |         key: &str,
000033 |         limit: usize,
000034 |     ) -> std::result::Result<Option<Object>, StoreError>;
000035 |     fn put_immutable(&mut self, key: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::result::Result<(), StoreError>;
000036 |     fn commit_current(
000037 |         &mut self,
000038 |         expected_etag: Option<&str>,
000039 |         bytes: &[u8],
000040 |     ) -> std::result::Result<(), StoreError>;
000041 | }
000042 | 
000043 | #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
000044 | #[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
000045 | pub enum Phase {
000046 |     Prepared,
000047 |     Uploading,
000048 |     CommitUnknown,
000049 |     Conflict,
000050 |     ReadbackFailed,
000051 |     Published,
000052 | }
000053 | 
000054 | #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
000055 | #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
000056 | pub struct Pending {
000057 |     pub manifest: AdManifest,
000058 |     pub wire: Vec<u8>,
000059 |     pub expected_etag: Option<String>,
000060 |     pub phase: Phase,
000061 | }
000062 | 
000063 | pub fn prefix(channel: AdChannel) -> &'static str {
000064 |     match channel {
000065 |         AdChannel::Preview => "preview",
000066 |         AdChannel::Production => "production",
000067 |     }
000068 | }
000069 | 
000070 | pub fn current_key(channel: AdChannel) -> String {
000071 |     format!("{}/current.json", prefix(channel))
000072 | }
000073 | pub fn version_key(manifest: &AdManifest) -> String {
000074 |     format!(
000075 |         "{}/versions/{}.json",
000076 |         prefix(manifest.channel),
000077 |         manifest.release_id
000078 |     )
000079 | }
000080 | pub fn asset_key(channel: AdChannel, asset: &switcher_domain::ads::ImageAsset) -> String {
000081 |     format!(
000082 |         "{}/assets/{}.{}",
000083 |         prefix(channel),
000084 |         asset.sha256,
000085 |         if asset.mime == "image/png" {
000086 |             "png"
000087 |         } else {
000088 |             "jpg"
000089 |         }
000090 |     )
000091 | }
000092 | 
000093 | impl From<StoreError> for Error {
000094 |     fn from(value: StoreError) -> Self {
000095 |         Error::Store(value)
000096 |     }
000097 | }
000098 | 
000099 | pub(crate) fn same_object(actual: &Option<Object>, expected: &[u8]) -> bool {
000100 |     actual
000101 |         .as_ref()
000102 |         .is_some_and(|object| object.bytes == expected)
000103 | }
000104 | 
000105 | pub(crate) fn require_scope(store: &dyn ObjectStore, channel: AdChannel) -> Result<()> {
000106 |     if store.channel() == channel {
000107 |         Ok(())
000108 |     } else {
000109 |         Err(Error::Invalid("publisher_environment"))
000110 |     }
000111 | }

000001 | //! Personal-only secret storage. No command line process receives secret bytes.
000002 | use super::{Error, Result};
000003 | use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
000004 | use zeroize::Zeroize;
000005 | 
000006 | pub const SECRET_LIMIT: usize = 160 * 1024;
000007 | 
000008 | #[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
000009 | #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
000010 | pub struct Secrets {
000011 |     pub signing_seed: Vec<u8>,
000012 |     pub preview_credential: Vec<u8>,
000013 |     pub production_credential: Vec<u8>,
000014 | }
000015 | 
000016 | impl Secrets {
000017 |     pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
000018 |         if self.signing_seed.len() != 32
000019 |             || self.preview_credential.len() > 16 * 1024
000020 |             || self.production_credential.len() > 16 * 1024
000021 |         {
000022 |             return Err(Error::Invalid("secret_shape"));
000023 |         }
000024 |         Ok(())
000025 |     }
000026 | }
000027 | 
000028 | impl Drop for Secrets {
000029 |     fn drop(&mut self) {
000030 |         self.signing_seed.zeroize();
000031 |         self.preview_credential.zeroize();
000032 |         self.production_credential.zeroize();
000033 |     }
000034 | }
000035 | 
000036 | /// A generation uses one atomic Keychain item for its entire secret bundle.
000037 | /// Implementations must not log, format or persist unencrypted secret bytes.
000038 | pub trait Vault {
000039 |     fn load(&self, generation: &str) -> Result<Secrets>;
000040 |     fn store(&mut self, generation: &str, secrets: &Secrets) -> Result<()>;
000041 | }
000042 | 
000043 | pub struct KeychainVault;
000044 | 
000045 | impl Vault for KeychainVault {
000046 |     fn load(&self, generation: &str) -> Result<Secrets> {
000047 |         #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
000048 |         {
000049 |             let bytes = zeroize::Zeroizing::new(
000050 |                 security_framework::passwords::get_generic_password(
000051 |                     "com.claude-switcher.personal.publisher",
000052 |                     generation,
000053 |                 )
000054 |                 .map_err(|_| Error::SecretUnavailable)?,
000055 |             );
000056 |             let secrets: Secrets =
000057 |                 switcher_core::signed::strict_json(&bytes, SECRET_LIMIT).map_err(Error::Invalid)?;
000058 |             secrets.validate()?;
000059 |             Ok(secrets)
000060 |         }
000061 |         #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
000062 |         {
000063 |             let _ = generation;
000064 |             Err(Error::SecretUnavailable)
000065 |         }
000066 |     }
000067 | 
000068 |     fn store(&mut self, generation: &str, secrets: &Secrets) -> Result<()> {
000069 |         secrets.validate()?;
000070 |         #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
000071 |         {
000072 |             let bytes = zeroize::Zeroizing::new(
000073 |                 serde_json::to_vec(secrets).map_err(|_| Error::Invalid("secret_encoding"))?,
000074 |             );
000075 |             if bytes.len() > SECRET_LIMIT {
000076 |                 return Err(Error::Invalid("secret_size"));
000077 |             }
000078 |             security_framework::passwords::set_generic_password(
000079 |                 "com.claude-switcher.personal.publisher",
000080 |                 generation,
000081 |                 &bytes,
000082 |             )
000083 |             .map_err(|_| Error::SecretUnavailable)
000084 |         }
000085 |         #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
000086 |         {
000087 |             let _ = generation;
000088 |             Err(Error::SecretUnavailable)
000089 |         }
000090 |     }
000091 | }

000036 | struct MemoryVault {
000037 |     items: HashMap<String, Vec<u8>>,
000038 |     fail: bool,
000039 | }
000040 | impl Drop for MemoryVault {
000041 |     fn drop(&mut self) {
000042 |         use zeroize::Zeroize;
000043 |         for value in self.items.values_mut() {
000044 |             value.zeroize();
000045 |         }
000046 |     }

000050 |         let bytes = self.items.get(generation).ok_or(Error::SecretUnavailable)?;

000052 |     }
000053 |     fn store(&mut self, generation: &str, secrets: &Secrets) -> Result<()> {
000054 |         if self.fail {
000055 |             return Err(Error::SecretUnavailable);
000056 |         }
000057 |         self.items
000058 |             .insert(generation.into(), serde_json::to_vec(secrets).unwrap());
000059 |         Ok(())
000060 |     }
000061 | }

000077 | struct MemoryStore {
000078 |     channel: AdChannel,
000079 |     objects: HashMap<String, Object>,
000080 |     commits: usize,
000081 |     conflict: bool,
000082 |     unknown_after_commit: bool,
000083 |     unknown_before_commit: bool,
000084 |     corrupt_readback: bool,
000085 |     hide_current: bool,
000086 |     next_etag: u64,
000087 |     accesses: Vec<String>,
000088 | }
000089 | impl MemoryStore {
000090 |     fn new(channel: AdChannel) -> Self {
000091 |         Self {
000092 |             channel,
000093 |             objects: HashMap::new(),
000094 |             commits: 0,
000095 |             conflict: false,
000096 |             unknown_after_commit: false,
000097 |             unknown_before_commit: false,
000098 |             corrupt_readback: false,
000099 |             hide_current: false,
000100 |             next_etag: 0,
000101 |             accesses: vec![],
000102 |         }
000103 |     }
000104 |     fn key(&self) -> String {
000105 |         protocol::current_key(self.channel)
000106 |     }
000107 | }
000108 | impl ObjectStore for MemoryStore {
000109 |     fn channel(&self) -> AdChannel {
000110 |         self.channel
000111 |     }
000112 |     fn current(&mut self) -> std::result::Result<Option<Object>, StoreError> {
000113 |         Ok(self.objects.get(&self.key()).cloned())
000114 |     }
000115 |     fn public_read(
000116 |         &mut self,
000117 |         key: &str,
000118 |         limit: usize,
000119 |     ) -> std::result::Result<Option<Object>, StoreError> {
000120 |         self.accesses.push(key.into());
000121 |         if self.hide_current && key == self.key() {
000122 |             return Ok(None);
000123 |         }
000124 |         let mut object = self.objects.get(key).cloned();
000125 |         if object
000126 |             .as_ref()
000127 |             .is_some_and(|object| object.bytes.len() > limit)
000128 |         {
000129 |             return Err(StoreError::Unavailable);
000130 |         }
000131 |         if self.corrupt_readback
000132 |             && key.contains("/assets/")
000133 |             && let Some(object) = &mut object
000134 |         {
000135 |             object.bytes[0] ^= 1;
000136 |         }
000137 |         Ok(object)
000138 |     }
000139 |     fn put_immutable(&mut self, key: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::result::Result<(), StoreError> {
000140 |         if !key.starts_with(&format!("{}/", protocol::prefix(self.channel))) {
000141 |             return Err(StoreError::Permission);
000142 |         }
000143 |         if let Some(existing) = self.objects.get(key) {
000144 |             return if existing.bytes == bytes {
000145 |                 Ok(())
000146 |             } else {
000147 |                 Err(StoreError::Conflict)
000148 |             };
000149 |         }
000150 |         self.objects.insert(
000151 |             key.into(),
000152 |             Object {
000153 |                 bytes: bytes.into(),
000154 |                 etag: "immutable".into(),
000155 |             },
000156 |         );
000157 |         Ok(())
000158 |     }
000159 |     fn commit_current(
000160 |         &mut self,
000161 |         expected_etag: Option<&str>,
000162 |         bytes: &[u8],
000163 |     ) -> std::result::Result<(), StoreError> {
000164 |         self.commits += 1;
000165 |         if self.conflict
000166 |             || self
000167 |                 .objects
000168 |                 .get(&self.key())
000169 |                 .map(|object| object.etag.as_str())
000170 |                 != expected_etag
000171 |         {
000172 |             return Err(StoreError::Conflict);
000173 |         }
000174 |         if self.unknown_before_commit {
000175 |             self.unknown_before_commit = false;
000176 |             return Err(StoreError::OutcomeUnknown);
000177 |         }
000178 |         self.next_etag += 1;
000179 |         self.objects.insert(
000180 |             self.key(),
000181 |             Object {
000182 |                 bytes: bytes.into(),
000183 |                 etag: format!("etag-{}", self.next_etag),
000184 |             },
000185 |         );
000186 |         if self.unknown_after_commit {
000187 |             self.unknown_after_commit = false;
000188 |             Err(StoreError::OutcomeUnknown)
000189 |         } else {
000190 |             Ok(())
000191 |         }
000192 |     }
000193 | }

000665 | fn local_publication_both_editions_pause_history_backup_and_signature_verification() {
000666 |     use local_store::LocalObjectStore;
000667 |     use switcher_core::ad_runtime::{AdRuntime, local::LocalTrust};
000668 |     use switcher_domain::{Edition, ads::AdPreferences};

000674 |         &root.0.join("drafts"),
000675 |         "ads-personal-v1",
000676 |         Some(signer().verifying_key()),
000677 |         now,
000678 |     )
000679 |     .unwrap();

000681 |     // No fabricated provider credential is needed in local mode.

000684 |     secrets.production_credential.clear();

000692 |         schema: 1,

000701 |     let preferences = AdPreferences {

000713 |     }

000716 |         before_reads
000717 |     );
000718 |     let backup = p.export_backup(b"local-backup-password", &vault).unwrap();
000719 |     p.pause(None, now).unwrap();
000720 |     p.publish(AdChannel::Production, now, Some(&mut store), &vault)
000721 |         .unwrap();
000722 |     for edition in [Edition::Personal, Edition::Public] {
000723 |         assert!(
000724 |             AdRuntime::new(root.0.clone(), edition)
000725 |                 .snapshot(&preferences)
000726 |                 .unwrap()
000727 |                 .is_none()
000728 |         );
000729 |     }

000731 |     p.publish(AdChannel::Production, now, Some(&mut store), &vault)
000732 |         .unwrap();

000734 |         AdRuntime::new(root.0.clone(), Edition::Public)
000735 |             .snapshot(&preferences)
000736 |             .unwrap()
000737 |             .is_some()
000738 |     );
000739 |     p.restore_backup(&backup, b"local-backup-password", &mut vault)
000740 |         .unwrap();
000741 |     p.publish(AdChannel::Production, now, Some(&mut store), &vault)
000742 |         .unwrap();
000743 |     assert!(p.status().history.last().unwrap().manifest.content_revision > first.revision);
000744 |     // Restoring on a fresh installation requires no pre-existing public key.
000745 |     let fresh_root = root.0.join("restored-drafts");
000746 |     let mut fresh = Publisher::open(&fresh_root, "ads-personal-v1", None, now).unwrap();
000747 |     let before = fs::read(fresh_root.join("active.json")).unwrap();

000749 |         fresh
000750 |             .restore_backup(&backup, b"wrong-password", &mut vault)
000751 |             .is_err()
000752 |     );

000754 |     assert!(!fresh_root.join("trust.json").exists());

000761 |         .unwrap();

000763 |     let restored_revision = fresh.draft_read().content_revision;
000764 |     drop(fresh);
000765 |     assert_eq!(
000766 |         Publisher::open(&fresh_root, "ads-personal-v1", None, now)
000767 |             .unwrap()
000768 |             .draft_read()
000769 |             .content_revision,
000770 |         restored_revision
000771 |     );
000772 |     // Preview writes are separate from the normal advertisement display channel.
000773 |     let production = store.current().unwrap().unwrap().bytes;
000774 |     drop(store);
000775 |     let mut preview = LocalObjectStore::open(&root.0, AdChannel::Preview, &trust).unwrap();
000776 |     p.publish(AdChannel::Preview, now, Some(&mut preview), &vault)
000777 |         .unwrap();
000778 |     drop(preview);
000779 |     let mut store = LocalObjectStore::open(&root.0, AdChannel::Production, &trust).unwrap();
000780 |     assert_eq!(store.current().unwrap().unwrap().bytes, production);
000781 |     // An edited local signature cannot become visible, even under private permissions.
000782 |     storage::atomic_write(
000783 |         &switcher_core::ad_runtime::local::directory(&root.0).join("production/current.json"),
000784 |         b"{}",
000785 |     )
000786 |     .unwrap();
000787 |     assert!(
000788 |         AdRuntime::new(root.0.clone(), Edition::Public)
000789 |             .snapshot(&preferences)
000790 |             .is_err()
000791 |     );
000792 |     // Failure backoff retains a diagnostic without re-parsing each UI poll.
000793 |     assert_eq!(
000794 |         AdRuntime::new(root.0.clone(), Edition::Public)
000795 |             .snapshot(&preferences)
000796 |             .unwrap_err(),
000797 |         "local_ad_previous_content_verification_failed"
000798 |     );
000799 | }
000800 | 
000801 | fn local_tree(path: &Path) -> Vec<(PathBuf, String)> {
000802 |     let mut files = Vec::new();
000803 |     for entry in fs::read_dir(path).unwrap() {
000804 |         let entry = entry.unwrap();
000805 |         if entry.file_type().unwrap().is_dir() {
000806 |             files.extend(local_tree(&entry.path()));
000807 |         } else {
000808 |             files.push((
000809 |                 entry.path(),
000810 |                 signed::digest(&fs::read(entry.path()).unwrap()),
000811 |             ));
000812 |         }
000813 |     }
000814 |     files.sort();
000815 |     files
000816 | }

000014 | type Desktop<'a> = State<'a, Arc<DesktopState>>;
000015 | fn with_publisher<T>(
000016 |     state: &DesktopState,
000017 |     run: impl FnOnce(&mut Publisher) -> publisher::Result<T>,
000018 | ) -> Result<T, String> {
000019 |     state.prepare_directory()?;

000021 |     let mut publisher = Publisher::open(
000022 |         &directory,
000023 |         "ads-personal-v1",
000024 |         None,
000025 |         switcher_core::checks::unix_now(),
000026 |     )
000027 |     .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
000028 |     run(&mut publisher).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
000029 | }

000042 | pub fn import_ad_image(state: Desktop<'_>, bytes: Vec<u8>) -> Result<ImportedImage, String> {
000043 |     if bytes.len() > 1024 * 1024 {
000044 |         return Err("图片超过 1 MiB 上限".into());
000045 |     }
000046 |     let mime = if bytes.starts_with(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n") {

000048 |     } else if bytes.starts_with(b"\xff\xd8\xff") {
000049 |         "image/jpeg"
000050 |     } else {
000051 |         return Err("请选择静态 PNG 或 JPEG 图片".into());
000052 |     };

000054 |         let asset = p.import_image(&bytes, mime)?;
000055 |         let preview_data_url = p.image_preview(&asset.sha256)?;
000056 |         Ok(ImportedImage {
000057 |             asset,
000058 |             preview_data_url,
000059 |         })
000060 |     })

000084 |                 "recovery_required"

000086 |                 "pending"
000087 |             } else if p.configured_public_key().is_none() {
000088 |                 "wait_signing_key"
000089 |             } else {
000090 |                 "ready_local"
000091 |             }

000094 |                 "本地草稿可用。{key_status}{pending_status} 本机预览与正式目录已可运营；不会发布到远程用户。"
000095 |             ),

000097 |                 .history
000098 |                 .into_iter()
000099 |                 .map(|record| PublishRecord {
000100 |                     release_id: record.manifest.release_id,

000107 |                     published_at: record.published_at,
000108 |                     payload_hash: record.payload_hash,
000109 |                     verified: true,
000110 |                 })

000113 |     })

000001 | //! Desktop and guardian share this fixed-action entry and the account lifecycle lock.
000002 | //! No production release source or signing identity has been provisioned. This module
000003 | //! therefore makes zero update requests and cannot install an unauthenticated cache.
000004 | use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
