000006 |     io,
000007 |     path::{Path, PathBuf},
000008 | };
000009 | use switcher_core::{
000010 |     signed,
000011 |     storage::{self, LifecycleLock},
000012 |     time::{self, ClockSample, ClockState},
000013 |     updates::{self, UpdateSchedule, UpdateState},
000014 | };
000015 | use switcher_domain::{
000016 |     Edition,
000017 |     desktop::{OperationOutcome, UpdateAction, UpdateView},
000018 | };
000019 | 
000020 | const SOURCE_LOCAL: &str = "源码构建版本；升级请拉取源码、重新构建并运行本地安装脚本。";
000021 | 
000022 | /// Locked upstream source checked after AnySearch discovery on 2026-10-22:
000023 | /// https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/updater-v2.14.0/plugins/updater/src/config.rs
000024 | /// https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/updater-v2.14.0/plugins/updater/src/updater.rs
000025 | /// Keep endpoints empty until a release pins the real source. Do not set a comparator.
000026 | /// `restore_update` requires a signed version; `install` verifies the supplied bytes again.
000027 | pub fn strict_updater_config(pubkey: String) -> tauri_plugin_updater::Config {
000028 |     use base64::Engine;
000029 |     tauri_plugin_updater::Config {
000030 |         pubkey: base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(pubkey),
000031 |         require_signed_version: true,
000032 |         allow_downgrades: false,
000033 |         ..Default::default()
000034 |     }
000035 | }
000036 | 
000037 | /// Tauri's plugin configuration is deserialized from the application context.
000038 | /// The locked upstream Config implements Deserialize but intentionally not Serialize.
000039 | pub fn strict_updater_settings(pubkey: String) -> serde_json::Value {
000040 |     let config = strict_updater_config(pubkey);
000041 |     serde_json::json!({
000042 |         "pubkey": config.pubkey,
000043 |         "requireSignedVersion": config.require_signed_version,
000044 |         "allowDowngrades": config.allow_downgrades,
000045 |         "endpoints": [],
000046 |         "dangerousInsecureTransportProtocol": false,
000047 |         "dangerousAcceptInvalidCerts": false,
000048 |         "dangerousAcceptInvalidHostnames": false,
000049 |     })
000050 | }
000051 | 
000052 | /// The only production release-material entry. Provision reviewed pins at build time.
000053 | /// Both the platform plugin and the core runtime must consume this same value.
000054 | pub fn compiled_release_pins() -> Option<updates::ValidatedReleasePins> {
000055 |     None
000056 | }
000057 | 
000058 | #[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
000059 | #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
000060 | struct Persistent {
000061 |     schema_version: u32,
000062 |     edition: Edition,
000063 |     schedule: UpdateSchedule,
000064 |     #[serde(default, skip_serializing)]
000065 |     clock: ClockState,
000066 |     updates: UpdateState,
000067 |     status: String,
000068 |     message: String,
000069 | }
000070 | impl Persistent {
000071 |     fn new(edition: Edition, now: u64) -> Self {
000072 |         Self {
000073 |             schema_version: 1,
000074 |             edition,
000075 |             schedule: UpdateSchedule::new(edition, now),
000076 |             clock: ClockState::default(),
000077 |             updates: UpdateState::default(),
000078 |             status: "wait_materials".into(),
000079 |             message: "尚未检查签名更新通道。".into(),
000080 |         }
000081 |     }
000082 | }
000083 | 
000084 | pub struct UpdateRuntime {
000085 |     root: PathBuf,
000086 |     edition: Edition,
000087 |     pins: Option<updates::ValidatedReleasePins>,
000088 | }
000089 | impl UpdateRuntime {
000090 |     /// `root` is the switcher data root, not an account directory or user-provided URL.
000091 |     pub fn new(root: PathBuf, edition: Edition) -> Self {
000092 |         // Real release pins must be reviewed and compiled into this edition.
000093 |         // Credentials, IPC parameters and writable configuration cannot select them.
000094 |         if let Some(pins) = compiled_release_pins() {
000095 |             Self::with_release_pins(root, edition, pins)
000096 |         } else {
000097 |             Self {
000098 |                 root,
000099 |                 edition,
000100 |                 pins: None,
000101 |             }
000102 |         }
000103 |     }
000104 |     /// Only the desktop's reviewed build configuration may call this constructor.
000105 |     /// ValidatedReleasePins cannot arrive through serde or any registered command.
000106 |     pub(crate) fn with_release_pins(
000107 |         root: PathBuf,
000108 |         edition: Edition,
000109 |         pins: updates::ValidatedReleasePins,
000110 |     ) -> Self {
000111 |         Self {
000112 |             root,
000113 |             edition,
000114 |             pins: Some(pins),
000115 |         }
000116 |     }
000117 |     fn v2(&self) -> PathBuf {
000118 |         self.root.join("v2")
000119 |     }
000120 |     fn directory(&self) -> PathBuf {
000121 |         self.v2().join(match self.edition {
000122 |             Edition::Personal => "personal",
000123 |             Edition::Public => "public",
000124 |         })
000125 |     }
000126 |     fn state_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
000127 |         self.directory().join("updates.json")
000128 |     }
000129 |     fn lock(&self) -> io::Result<LifecycleLock> {
000130 |         storage::prepare_data_root(&self.root)?;
000131 |         storage::create_private_directory(&self.v2())?;
000132 |         let lock = LifecycleLock::try_acquire(&self.v2())?;
000133 |         storage::create_private_directory(&self.directory())?;
000134 |         Ok(lock)
000135 |     }
000136 |     fn load(&self, sample: &ClockSample) -> io::Result<Persistent> {
000137 |         let mut state = match storage::read_private_bytes(&self.state_path(), updates::STATE_LIMIT)
000138 |         {
000139 |             Ok(bytes) => {
000140 |                 let state: Persistent =
000141 |                     signed::strict_json(&bytes, updates::STATE_LIMIT).map_err(io::Error::other)?;
000142 |                 if state.schema_version != 1
000143 |                     || state.edition != self.edition
000144 |                     || state.schedule.edition != self.edition
000145 |                     || state.status.len() > 64
000146 |                     || state.message.len() > 2048
000147 |                 {
000148 |                     return Err(io::Error::other("update_state_context"));
000149 |                 }
000150 |                 // Apply the same bounded replay/failure validation as core restoration.
000151 |                 UpdateState::decode(&state.updates.encode().map_err(io::Error::other)?)
000152 |                     .map_err(io::Error::other)?;
000153 |                 state
000154 |             }
000155 |             Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
000156 |                 Persistent::new(self.edition, utc(sample)?)
000157 |             }
000158 |             Err(error) => return Err(error),
000159 |         };
000160 |         state.clock =
000161 |             match storage::read_private_bytes(&self.directory().join("clock.json"), 64 * 1024) {
000162 |                 Ok(bytes) => signed::strict_json(&bytes, 64 * 1024).map_err(io::Error::other)?,
000163 |                 Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => state.clock,
000164 |                 Err(error) => return Err(error),
000165 |             };
000166 |         state.clock.observe_passive(sample.clone());
000167 |         // The lifecycle lock proves no updater is currently working in another process.
000168 |         state.schedule.recover_process();
000169 |         if state.updates.recover_interrupted() {
000170 |             state.status = "recovery_required".into();
000171 |             state.message = "检测到中断的更新事务。请核验已安装组件及兼容回退工件。".into();
000172 |         }
000173 |         Ok(state)
000174 |     }
000175 |     fn save(&self, state: &Persistent) -> io::Result<()> {
000176 |         let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(state).map_err(io::Error::other)?;
000177 |         if bytes.len() > updates::STATE_LIMIT {
000178 |             return Err(io::Error::other("update_state_too_large"));
000179 |         }
000180 |         write_changed(&self.state_path(), &bytes)?;
000181 |         write_changed(
000182 |             &self.directory().join("clock.json"),
000183 |             &serde_json::to_vec(&state.clock).map_err(io::Error::other)?,
000184 |         )
000185 |     }
000186 |     pub fn snapshot(&self) -> UpdateView {
000187 |         let result = (|| -> io::Result<UpdateView> {
000188 |             let _lock = self.lock()?;
000189 |             let sample = time::sample().map_err(io::Error::other)?;
000190 |             let state = self.load(&sample)?;
000191 |             self.save(&state)?;
000192 |             let mut result = view(&state);
000193 |             if self.pins.is_none() {
000194 |                 source_local_view(&mut result);
000195 |             }
000196 |             let installed = self.pins.as_ref().and_then(|_| {
000197 |                 switcher_core::install::validate_installed(self.edition, unsafe { libc::geteuid() })
000198 |                     .ok()
000199 |             });
000200 |             if let Some(pins) = &self.pins
000201 |                 && let Some(record) = &installed
000202 |                 && pinned_installation(Ok(record.clone()), self.edition).is_ok()
000203 |                 && let (Ok(app), Ok(helper)) = (
000204 |                     updates::ValidatedReleasePins::version(env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")),
000205 |                     updates::ValidatedReleasePins::version(&record.helper_version),
000206 |                 )
000207 |                 && let Ok(candidate) = pins.restore(
000208 |                     &state.updates,
000209 |                     &app,
000210 |                     Some(&helper),
000211 |                     utc(&sample)?,
000212 |                     state.clock.trusted(),
000213 |                 )
000214 |             {
000215 |                 result.candidate_version = Some(candidate.metadata().version.to_string());
000216 |                 result.can_download = state.updates.candidate.as_ref().is_some_and(|pending| {
000217 |                     matches!(
000218 |                         pending.stage,
000219 |                         updates::InstallStage::Authenticated
000220 |                             | updates::InstallStage::Downloaded
000221 |                             | updates::InstallStage::Rejected
000222 |                     )
000223 |                 });
000224 |                 result.can_install =
000225 |                     state.clock.trusted()
000226 |                         && state.updates.candidate.as_ref().is_some_and(|pending| {
000227 |                             pending.stage == updates::InstallStage::Downloaded
000228 |                         });
000229 |             }
000230 |             if let Some(record) = &installed
000231 |                 && record.is_source_local()
000232 |             {
000233 |                 source_local_view(&mut result);
000234 |             }
000235 |             Ok(result)
000236 |         })();
000237 |         result.unwrap_or_else(|error| UpdateView {
000238 |             paused: None,
000239 |             state: error_code(&error).into(),
000240 |             message: error_message(&error),
000241 |             next_check_at: None,
000242 |             candidate_version: None,
000243 |             can_download: false,
000244 |             can_install: false,
000245 |         })
000246 |     }
000247 |     /// UI actions are individually initiated by the local user. Consent is never persisted.
000248 |     pub fn act(&self, action: UpdateAction) -> OperationOutcome {
000249 |         match action {
000250 |             UpdateAction::Check => self.run_task(updates::UpdateAction::Check, true),
000251 |             UpdateAction::Download => self.run_task(updates::UpdateAction::Download, true),
000252 |             UpdateAction::Install => self.run_task(updates::UpdateAction::Install, true),
000253 |             _ => self.change_schedule(action),
000254 |         }
000255 |     }
000256 |     /// Guardian/CLI use an enum. This entry never accepts a URL, candidate path, or shell text.
000257 |     pub fn run_task(&self, action: updates::UpdateAction, manual: bool) -> OperationOutcome {
000258 |         let result = (|| -> io::Result<OperationOutcome> {
000259 |             let lock = self.lock()?;
000260 |             let sample = time::sample().map_err(io::Error::other)?;
000261 |             self.run_locked(action, manual, &sample, &lock)
000262 |         })();
000263 |         result.unwrap_or_else(outcome_error)
000264 |     }
000265 |     fn run_locked(
000266 |         &self,
000267 |         action: updates::UpdateAction,
000268 |         manual: bool,
000269 |         sample: &ClockSample,
000270 |         lock: &LifecycleLock,
000271 |     ) -> io::Result<OperationOutcome> {
000272 |         if action == updates::UpdateAction::Install
000273 |             && let Err(reason) = updates::ensure_installation_idle(&self.v2(), lock)
000274 |         {
000275 |             return Ok(outcome(
000276 |                 false,
000277 |                 "地区或配置修复尚未恢复完成，暂不能准备或安装更新。",
000278 |                 Some(reason),
000279 |             ));
000280 |         }
000281 |         let mut state = self.load(sample)?;
000282 |         if manual {
000283 |             state.clock.observe(sample.clone());
000284 |         }
000285 |         let now = utc(sample)?;
000286 |         if !state.clock.allows_verified_refresh()
000287 |             || (action != updates::UpdateAction::Check && !state.clock.trusted())
000288 |         {
000289 |             self.save(&state)?;
000290 |             return Ok(outcome(
000291 |                 false,
000292 |                 "系统时间需要恢复。修正时间后点击恢复，再完成固定源验签刷新。",
000293 |                 Some("clock_recovery_required"),
000294 |             ));
000295 |         }
000296 |         match state.schedule.begin(action, manual, now) {
000297 |             Ok(true) => (),
000298 |             Ok(false) => {
000299 |                 return Ok(outcome(
000300 |                     false,
000301 |                     "相同更新任务已经在执行。",
000302 |                     Some("update_in_progress"),
000303 |                 ));
000304 |             }
000305 |             Err(reason) => {
000306 |                 self.save(&state)?;
000307 |                 let message = match reason {
000308 |                     "personal_requires_user_action" => {
000309 |                         "Personal 的每次检查、下载和安装均需本人操作。"
000310 |                     }
000311 |                     "updates_paused" => "自动更新已暂停。",
000312 |                     "installation_deferred" => "本次安装已延后。",
000313 |                     "check_not_due" => "尚未到达下次检查时间。",
000314 |                     _ => "更新任务目前不可执行。",
000315 |                 };
000316 |                 return Ok(outcome(false, message, Some(reason)));
000317 |             }
000318 |         }
000319 |         if let Some(pins) = &self.pins {
000320 |             state.clock.observe(sample.clone());
000321 |             self.save(&state)?; // Reserve the task before network or staging side effects.
000322 |             let result = self.execute_pinned(action, manual, sample, &mut state, pins, lock);
000323 |             match result {
000324 |                 Ok(result) => {
000325 |                     self.save(&state)?;
000326 |                     return Ok(result);
000327 |                 }
000328 |                 Err(reason) => {
000329 |                     if matches!(reason, "update_network" | "update_http_status_or_redirect") {
000330 |                         state.schedule.transient_failure(now);
000331 |                     } else {
000332 |                         state.schedule.authorization_cancelled();
000333 |                     }
000334 |                     state.status = "update_failed".into();
000335 |                     state.message = if reason == "source_local_rebuild_required" {
000336 |                         SOURCE_LOCAL.into()
000337 |                     } else {
000338 |                         format!("更新未完成：{reason}。请按更新状态检查或恢复组件。")
000339 |                     };
000340 |                     self.save(&state)?;
000341 |                     return Ok(outcome(false, &state.message, Some(reason)));
000342 |                 }
000343 |             }
000344 |         }
000345 |         // No transport is created while production materials are absent.
000346 |         state.schedule.authorization_cancelled();
000347 |         if action == updates::UpdateAction::Check {
000348 |             state.schedule.next_check_at = now.saturating_add(updates::CHECK_INTERVAL);
000349 |         }
000350 |         if !matches!(
000351 |             state.status.as_str(),
000352 |             "recovery_required"
000353 |                 | "update_failed"
000354 |                 | "wait_recovery_materials"
000355 |                 | "failed_artifacts_suppressed"
000356 |         ) {
000357 |             state.status = "source_local_rebuild_required".into();
000358 |             state.message = SOURCE_LOCAL.into();
000359 |         }
000360 |         self.save(&state)?;
000361 |         Ok(outcome(
000362 |             false,
000363 |             &state.message,
000364 |             Some(match state.status.as_str() {
000365 |                 "recovery_required" => "update_recovery_required",
000366 |                 "update_failed" => "update_failed",
000367 |                 "wait_recovery_materials" => "wait_update_recovery_pkg",
000368 |                 "failed_artifacts_suppressed" => "failed_artifacts_suppressed",
000369 |                 _ => "source_local_rebuild_required",
000370 |             }),
000371 |         ))
000372 |     }
000373 |     fn execute_pinned(
000374 |         &self,
000375 |         action: updates::UpdateAction,
000376 |         manual: bool,
000377 |         sample: &ClockSample,
000378 |         state: &mut Persistent,
000379 |         pins: &updates::ValidatedReleasePins,
000380 |         lock: &LifecycleLock,
000381 |     ) -> Result<OperationOutcome, &'static str> {
000382 |         if pins.edition() != self.edition {
000383 |             return Err("release_context");
000384 |         }
000385 |         let record = pinned_installation(
000386 |             switcher_core::install::validate_installed(self.edition, unsafe { libc::geteuid() }),
000387 |             self.edition,
000388 |         )?;
000389 |         let may_recover = match updates::ensure_installation_idle(&self.v2(), lock) {
000390 |             Ok(()) => true,
000391 |             Err("repair_recovery_pending") if action != updates::UpdateAction::Install => false,
000392 |             Err(reason) => return Err(reason),
000393 |         };
000394 |         let mut coordinator = switcher_core::install::NativeUpdateCoordinator::new(
000395 |             &self.v2(),
000396 |             lock,
000397 |             state.clock.clone(),
000398 |         )?;
000399 |         if may_recover {
000400 |             coordinator.recover(&mut state.updates)?;
000401 |         }
000402 |         self.save(state).map_err(|_| "update_state_write")?;
000403 |         let app = updates::ValidatedReleasePins::version(env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"))?;
000404 |         let helper = updates::ValidatedReleasePins::version(&record.helper_version)?;
000405 |         let now = utc(sample).map_err(|_| "utc_before_epoch")?;
000406 |         let transport = updates::ReleaseTransport::new(pins.clone())?;
000407 |         let candidate = match action {
000408 |             updates::UpdateAction::Check => {
000409 |                 let candidate = transport.check_blocking(
000410 |                     &state.updates,
000411 |                     &app,
000412 |                     Some(&helper),
000413 |                     now,
000414 |                     state.clock.allows_verified_refresh(),
000415 |                 )?;
000416 |                 let refreshed = time::sample()?;
000417 |                 state.clock.observe(refreshed.clone());
000418 |                 if !state.clock.allows_verified_refresh() {
000419 |                     return Err("clock_recovery_required");
000420 |                 }
000421 |                 let fresh_now = utc(&refreshed).map_err(|_| "utc_before_epoch")?;
000422 |                 if candidate.metadata().expires_at <= fresh_now
000423 |                     || candidate.metadata().issued_at > fresh_now
000424 |                 {
000425 |                     return Err("metadata_expired");
000426 |                 }
000427 |                 let accepted = state.updates.accept(&candidate, false)?;
000428 |                 state.clock.record_verified_success(refreshed)?;
000429 |                 state.schedule.success(action, now)?;
000430 |                 self.save(state).map_err(|_| "update_state_write")?;
000431 |                 if !accepted {
000432 |                     state.status = "failed_artifacts_suppressed".into();
000433 |                     state.message =
000434 |                         "该 app/helper 工件组合此前失败。请完成恢复后再显式重试。".into();
000435 |                     return Ok(outcome(
000436 |                         false,
000437 |                         &state.message,
000438 |                         Some("failed_artifacts_suppressed"),
000439 |                     ));
000440 |                 }
000441 |                 if manual {
000442 |                     state.status = "authenticated".into();
000443 |                     state.message = format!(
000444 |                         "已验签更新 {}。下载需再次操作。",
000445 |                         candidate.metadata().version
000446 |                     );
000447 |                     return Ok(outcome(true, &state.message, None));
000448 |                 }
000449 |                 // Public may download automatically; Personal cannot enter this branch.
000450 |                 state
000451 |                     .schedule
000452 |                     .begin(updates::UpdateAction::Download, false, now)?;
000453 |                 self.download_candidate(&transport, &candidate, state, now)?;
000454 |                 let sample = time::sample()?;
000455 |                 let fresh_now = utc(&sample).map_err(|_| "utc_before_epoch")?;
000456 |                 state.clock.observe(sample.clone());
000457 |                 if !state.clock.trusted() {
000458 |                     return Err("clock_recovery_required");
000459 |                 }
000460 |                 if let Err(reason) =
000461 |                     state
000462 |                         .schedule
000463 |                         .begin(updates::UpdateAction::Install, false, fresh_now)
000464 |                 {
000465 |                     return Ok(outcome(
000466 |                         true,
000467 |                         "更新已暂存。自动安装正在暂停或延后。",
000468 |                         Some(reason),
000469 |                     ));
000470 |                 }
000471 |                 self.save(state).map_err(|_| "update_state_write")?;
000472 |                 return self.execute_pinned(
000473 |                     updates::UpdateAction::Install,
000474 |                     false,
000475 |                     &sample,
000476 |                     state,
000477 |                     pins,
000478 |                     lock,
000479 |                 );
000480 |             }
000481 |             _ => pins.restore(
000482 |                 &state.updates,
000483 |                 &app,
000484 |                 Some(&helper),
000485 |                 now,
000486 |                 state.clock.allows_verified_refresh(),
000487 |             )?,
000488 |         };
000489 |         if action == updates::UpdateAction::Download {
000490 |             if manual {
000491 |                 state.updates.accept(&candidate, true)?;
000492 |             }
000493 |             self.download_candidate(&transport, &candidate, state, now)?;
000494 |             return Ok(outcome(true, "已验证并暂存更新。安装需再次操作。", None));
000495 |         }
000496 |         updates::ensure_installation_idle(&self.v2(), lock)?;
000497 |         let staging = self
000498 |             .directory()
000499 |             .join("update-staging")
000500 |             .join(candidate.payload_hash());
000501 |         let app_bytes = storage::read_private_bytes(
000502 |             &staging.join("app.tar.gz"),
000503 |             updates::MAX_ARTIFACT_SIZE as usize,
000504 |         )
000505 |         .map_err(|_| "cached_app_missing")?;
000506 |         let app_proof =
000507 |             transport.verify_cached(&candidate, updates::ArtifactKind::App, &app_bytes)?;
000508 |         let helper_proof = if candidate.metadata().helper.is_some() {
000509 |             let bytes = storage::read_private_bytes(
000510 |                 &staging.join("helper.pkg"),
000511 |                 updates::MAX_ARTIFACT_SIZE as usize,
000512 |             )
000513 |             .map_err(|_| "cached_helper_missing")?;
000514 |             Some(transport.verify_cached(&candidate, updates::ArtifactKind::Helper, &bytes)?)
000515 |         } else {
000516 |             None
000517 |         };
000518 |         let serial = std::time::SystemTime::now()
000519 |             .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
000520 |             .map_err(|_| "utc_before_epoch")?
000521 |             .as_nanos();
000522 |         let expanded = staging.join(format!("app-expanded-{}-{serial}", std::process::id()));
000523 |         let app_path = updates::stage_app_archive(&candidate, &app_proof, &app_bytes, &expanded)?;
000524 |         let helper_path = staging.join("helper.pkg");
000525 |         let prepared = updates::assess_staged_installation(
000526 |             candidate,
000527 |             &app_proof,
000528 |             &app_path,
000529 |             helper_proof
000530 |                 .as_ref()
000531 |                 .map(|proof| (proof, helper_path.as_path())),
000532 |         );
000533 |         match prepared {
000534 |             Err(reason) => {
000535 |                 let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&expanded);
000536 |                 // Apple failure is not successful installation. Quarantine this candidate's
000537 |                 // bytes by removing the private staging tree; retain failure and sequence.
000538 |                 let restored = pins.restore(&state.updates, &app, Some(&helper), now, true)?;
000539 |                 state.updates.record_failure(&restored, reason, now)?;
000540 |                 let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&staging);
000541 |                 Err(reason)
000542 |             }
000543 |             Ok(prepared) => {
000544 |                 use updates::UpdateInstallCoordinator;
000545 |                 let installed = coordinator.install(&prepared, &mut state.updates);
000546 |                 let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&expanded);
000547 |                 if let Err("wait_update_recovery_pkg") = installed {
000548 |                     state.schedule.authorization_cancelled();
000549 |                     state.status = "wait_recovery_materials".into();
000550 |                     state.message = "等待已验证的上一兼容版本恢复 PKG；尚未开始安装。".into();
000551 |                     return Ok(outcome(
000552 |                         false,
000553 |                         &state.message,
000554 |                         Some("wait_update_recovery_pkg"),
000555 |                     ));
000556 |                 }
000557 |                 installed?;
000558 |                 if !state
000559 |                     .updates
000560 |                     .candidate
000561 |                     .as_ref()
000562 |                     .is_some_and(|pending| pending.stage == updates::InstallStage::Healthy)
000563 |                 {
000564 |                     return Err("update_health_unconfirmed");
000565 |                 }
000566 |                 state
000567 |                     .schedule
000568 |                     .success(updates::UpdateAction::Install, now)?;
000569 |                 state.status = "installed".into();
000570 |                 state.message =
000571 |                     "更新已安装，组件身份和后台健康检查已通过。请重新打开切换器。".into();
000572 |                 Ok(outcome(true, &state.message, None))
000573 |             }
000574 |         }
000575 |     }
000576 |     fn download_candidate(
000577 |         &self,
000578 |         transport: &updates::ReleaseTransport,
000579 |         candidate: &updates::Candidate,
000580 |         state: &mut Persistent,
000581 |         now: u64,
000582 |     ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000583 |         let download = |kind| transport.download_blocking(candidate, kind, now);
000584 |         let record_failure = |state: &mut Persistent, reason| {
000585 |             if matches!(
000586 |                 reason,
000587 |                 "artifact_size"
000588 |                     | "artifact_sha256"
000589 |                     | "update_response_too_large"
000590 |                     | "minisign_shape"
000591 |                     | "minisign_signature"
000592 |                     | "minisign_signed_version"
000593 |             ) {
000594 |                 state.updates.record_failure(candidate, reason, now)?;
000595 |             }
000596 |             Err::<_, &'static str>(reason)
000597 |         };
000598 |         let (app, app_proof) = match download(updates::ArtifactKind::App) {
000599 |             Ok(value) => value,
000600 |             Err(reason) => return record_failure(state, reason),
000601 |         };
000602 |         let helper = if candidate.metadata().helper.is_some() {
000603 |             Some(match download(updates::ArtifactKind::Helper) {
000604 |                 Ok(value) => value,
000605 |                 Err(reason) => return record_failure(state, reason),
000606 |             })
000607 |         } else {
000608 |             None
000609 |         };
000610 |         let sample = time::sample()?;
000611 |         state.clock.observe(sample.clone());
000612 |         if !state.clock.trusted() {
000613 |             return Err("clock_recovery_required");
000614 |         }
000615 |         let fresh_now = utc(&sample).map_err(|_| "utc_before_epoch")?;
000616 |         if fresh_now >= candidate.metadata().expires_at
000617 |             || fresh_now < candidate.metadata().issued_at
000618 |         {
000619 |             return Err("metadata_expired");
000620 |         }
000621 |         let parent = self.directory().join("update-staging");
000622 |         storage::create_private_directory(&parent).map_err(|_| "staging_directory")?;
000623 |         let staging = parent.join(candidate.payload_hash());
000624 |         storage::create_private_directory(&staging).map_err(|_| "staging_directory")?;
000625 |         let written = (|| {
000626 |             storage::atomic_write(&staging.join("app.tar.gz"), &app)
000627 |                 .map_err(|_| "staging_write")?;
000628 |             if let Some((bytes, _)) = &helper {
000629 |                 storage::atomic_write(&staging.join("helper.pkg"), bytes)
000630 |                     .map_err(|_| "staging_write")?;
000631 |             }
000632 |             state.updates.mark_downloaded(
000633 |                 candidate,
000634 |                 &app_proof,
000635 |                 helper.as_ref().map(|(_, proof)| proof),
000636 |             )?;
000637 |             Ok::<_, &'static str>(())
000638 |         })();
000639 |         if written.is_err() {
000640 |             let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&staging);
000641 |         }
000642 |         written?;
000643 |         state
000644 |             .schedule
000645 |             .success(updates::UpdateAction::Download, now)?;
000646 |         state.status = "downloaded".into();
000647 |         state.message = "工件已通过 SHA256、Minisign 和签名版本验证。尚未安装。".into();
000648 |         Ok(())
000649 |     }
000650 |     fn change_schedule(&self, action: UpdateAction) -> OperationOutcome {
000651 |         let result = (|| -> io::Result<OperationOutcome> {
000652 |             let _lock = self.lock()?;
000653 |             let sample = time::sample().map_err(io::Error::other)?;
000654 |             let now = utc(&sample)?;
000655 |             let mut state = self.load(&sample)?;
000656 |             state.clock.observe(sample.clone());
000657 |             let message = match action {
000658 |                 UpdateAction::Pause => {
000659 |                     state.schedule.paused = true;
000660 |                     "已暂停自动检查、下载和安装。"
000661 |                 }
000662 |                 UpdateAction::Resume => {
000663 |                     state.schedule.paused = false;
000664 |                     if !state.clock.trusted() {
000665 |                         state.clock.establish_baseline(sample);
000666 |                         "已建立本地时间基线。等待固定更新源验签刷新。"
000667 |                     } else {
000668 |                         "已恢复更新调度。"
000669 |                     }
000670 |                 }
000671 |                 UpdateAction::Defer => {
000672 |                     state.schedule.deferred_until = Some(now.saturating_add(24 * 60 * 60));
000673 |                     "已将自动安装延后 24 小时。"
000674 |                 }
000675 |                 _ => return Err(io::Error::other("unsupported_schedule_action")),
000676 |             };
000677 |             self.save(&state)?;
000678 |             Ok(outcome(true, message, None))
000679 |         })();
000680 |         result.unwrap_or_else(outcome_error)
000681 |     }
000682 | }
000683 | // This gate consumes the existing installed-identity validator's result. It never
000684 | // substitutes a writable record or a branch flag for native identity validation.
000685 | fn pinned_installation(
000686 |     installed: Result<switcher_domain::system::InstallRecord, &'static str>,
000687 |     edition: Edition,
000688 | ) -> Result<switcher_domain::system::InstallRecord, &'static str> {
000689 |     let record = installed.map_err(|_| "installed_identity_required")?;
000690 |     record
000691 |         .validate()
000692 |         .map_err(|_| "installed_identity_required")?;
000693 |     if record.edition != edition {
000694 |         return Err("installed_identity_required");
000695 |     }
000696 |     record.developer_team()?;
000697 |     Ok(record)
000698 | }
000699 | fn source_local_view(result: &mut UpdateView) {
000700 |     if matches!(
000701 |         result.state.as_str(),
000702 |         "wait_materials" | "source_local_rebuild_required" | "checked"
000703 |     ) {
000704 |         result.state = "source_local_rebuild_required".into();
000705 |         result.message = SOURCE_LOCAL.into();
000706 |     } else if !result.message.contains("源码构建版本") {
000707 |         result.message.push(' ');
000708 |         result.message.push_str(SOURCE_LOCAL);
000709 |     }
000710 |     result.next_check_at = None;
000711 |     result.candidate_version = None;
000712 |     result.can_download = false;
000713 |     result.can_install = false;
000714 | }
000715 | fn utc(sample: &ClockSample) -> io::Result<u64> {
000716 |     sample
000717 |         .utc_secs
000718 |         .try_into()
000719 |         .map_err(|_| io::Error::other("utc_before_epoch"))
000720 | }
000721 | fn write_changed(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> io::Result<()> {
000722 |     match storage::read_private_bytes(path, updates::STATE_LIMIT) {
000723 |         Ok(old) if old == bytes => return Ok(()),
000724 |         Ok(_) => (),
000725 |         Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => (),
000726 |         Err(error) => return Err(error),
000727 |     }
000728 |     storage::atomic_write(path, bytes)
000729 | }
000730 | fn view(state: &Persistent) -> UpdateView {
000731 |     let (status, message) = if !state.clock.trusted() {
000732 |         (
000733 |             "clock_recovery_required",
000734 |             "系统时间需要恢复。修正时间后点击恢复，再完成固定源验签刷新。",
000735 |         )
000736 |     } else if state.schedule.paused {
000737 |         ("paused", "自动更新已暂停。")
000738 |     } else {
000739 |         (state.status.as_str(), state.message.as_str())
000740 |     };
000741 |     UpdateView {
000742 |         paused: Some(state.schedule.paused),
000743 |         state: status.into(),
000744 |         message: message.into(),
000745 |         next_check_at: if state.edition == Edition::Public {
000746 |             Some(state.schedule.next_check_at)
000747 |         } else {
000748 |             None
000749 |         },
000750 |         candidate_version: None,
000751 |         can_download: false,
000752 |         can_install: false,
000753 |     }
000754 | }
000755 | fn outcome(completed: bool, message: &str, recovery: Option<&str>) -> OperationOutcome {
000756 |     OperationOutcome {
000757 |         completed,
000758 |         message: message.into(),
000759 |         recovery: recovery.map(str::to_owned),
000760 |     }
000761 | }
000762 | fn error_code(error: &io::Error) -> &'static str {
000763 |     if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock {
000764 |         "lifecycle_busy"
000765 |     } else {
000766 |         "update_storage_error"
000767 |     }
000768 | }
000769 | fn error_message(error: &io::Error) -> String {
000770 |     if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock {
000771 |         "账号或更新任务持有生命周期锁。请稍后重试。".into()
000772 |     } else {
000773 |         "无法安全读取或保存更新状态。请检查 v2 更新目录的权限和恢复记录。".into()
000774 |     }
000775 | }
000776 | fn outcome_error(error: io::Error) -> OperationOutcome {
000777 |     outcome(false, &error_message(&error), Some(error_code(&error)))
000778 | }

000811 |         for edition in [Edition::Personal, Edition::Public] {

000816 |                 let mut sample = time::sample().unwrap();

000819 |                 state.clock.record_verified_success(sample.clone()).unwrap();
000820 |                 state.updates.sequence.remember(7, "a".repeat(64)).unwrap();
000821 |                 runtime.save(&state).unwrap();
000822 |                 sample.utc_secs
000823 |             };
000824 |             assert_eq!(runtime.snapshot().state, "clock_recovery_required");

000826 |             assert_eq!(restarted.snapshot().state, "clock_recovery_required");

000831 |                 restored.clock.status,
000832 |                 switcher_core::time::ClockStatus::AwaitingVerifiedRefresh
000833 |             );
000834 |             assert_eq!(restored.clock.last_success_utc, Some(anchor));
000835 |             assert_eq!(restored.updates.sequence.highest, 7);
000836 |         }

001072 |     fn e11_consent_completed_personal_check_cannot_authorize_download_or_install_after_restart() {
001073 |         let fixture = Fixture::new();
001074 |         let runtime = UpdateRuntime::new(fixture.0.clone(), Edition::Personal);
001075 |         {
001076 |             let _lock = runtime.lock().unwrap();
001077 |             let mut state = runtime.load(&at(100)).unwrap();
001078 |             // Precondition only: emulate a completed Check schedule. Production pins
001079 |             // are absent, so this deliberately does not claim a successful network Check.

001083 |                     .begin(updates::UpdateAction::Check, true, 100),
001084 |                 Ok(true)
001085 |             );

001087 |                 .schedule
001088 |                 .success(updates::UpdateAction::Check, 101)
001089 |                 .unwrap();

001096 |             updates::UpdateAction::Install,

001098 |         .into_iter()

001100 |         {

001102 |             let restarted = UpdateRuntime::new(fixture.0.clone(), Edition::Personal);

001117 |                 Some("personal_requires_user_action")

001121 |             // missing-materials guard instead of inheriting any earlier consent.

001123 |                 .run_locked(action, true, &at(sample + 1), &lock)

001126 |             assert_eq!(
001127 |                 manual.recovery.as_deref(),
001128 |                 Some("source_local_rebuild_required")
001129 |             );

001131 |             let mut state = restarted.load(&at(sample + 2)).unwrap();
001132 |             state.status = "checked".into();

001134 |         }

001393 |     fn clock_recovery_status_preserves_actual_pause_setting_and_resume() {
001394 |         use switcher_core::time::ClockStatus;
001395 |         for edition in [Edition::Personal, Edition::Public] {
001396 |             for clock_status in [
001397 |                 ClockStatus::NeedsRecovery {
001398 |                     difference_secs: -600,
001399 |                 },
001400 |                 ClockStatus::AwaitingVerifiedRefresh,
001401 |             ] {
001402 |                 for paused in [false, true] {
001403 |                     let fixture = Fixture::new();
001404 |                     let runtime = UpdateRuntime::new(fixture.0.clone(), edition);
001405 |                     {
001406 |                         let _lock = runtime.lock().unwrap();
001407 |                         let mut state = Persistent::new(edition, 100);
001408 |                         state.schedule.paused = paused;
001409 |                         state.clock.status = clock_status.clone();
001410 |                         runtime.save(&state).unwrap();
001411 |                     }
001412 |                     let snapshot = runtime.snapshot();
001413 |                     assert_eq!(snapshot.state, "clock_recovery_required");
001414 |                     assert_eq!(snapshot.paused, Some(paused));
001415 |                     assert!(!snapshot.can_install);
001416 |                     assert!(runtime.act(UpdateAction::Resume).completed);
001417 |                     let restarted = UpdateRuntime::new(fixture.0.clone(), edition);
001418 |                     let resumed = restarted.snapshot();
001419 |                     assert_eq!(resumed.paused, Some(false));
001420 |                     assert_eq!(resumed.state, "clock_recovery_required");
001421 |                     assert!(!resumed.can_install);
001422 |                     let _lock = restarted.lock().unwrap();
001423 |                     let state = restarted.load(&time::sample().unwrap()).unwrap();
001424 |                     assert_eq!(state.clock.status, ClockStatus::AwaitingVerifiedRefresh);
001425 |                 }
001426 |             }
001427 |         }
001428 |     }

000001 | //! Actual fixed-path export under isolated directories; no HOME or native UI writes.
000002 | #![allow(dead_code)]
000003 | #[path = "../src/bridge.rs"]
000004 | mod bridge;
000005 | #[path = "../src/update_runtime.rs"]
000006 | mod update_runtime;
000007 | 
000008 | use std::os::unix::fs::{MetadataExt, PermissionsExt, symlink};
000009 | use switcher_core::storage;
000010 | use switcher_domain::Edition;
000011 | 
000012 | fn isolated(suffix: &str) -> bridge::DesktopState {
000013 |     let root = std::env::temp_dir().canonicalize().unwrap().join(format!(
000014 |         "switcher-e03-export-{}-{suffix}",
000015 |         std::process::id()
000016 |     ));
000017 |     storage::create_private_directory(&root).unwrap();
000018 |     let mut state = bridge::DesktopState::new(Edition::Personal).unwrap();
000019 |     state.root = root;
000020 |     state
000021 | }
000022 | 
000023 | #[test]
000024 | fn export_uses_the_same_redacted_snapshot_and_private_edition_path() {
000025 |     let mut state = isolated("content");
000026 |     for edition in [Edition::Personal, Edition::Public] {
000027 |         state.edition = edition;
000028 |         state.prepare_directory().unwrap();
000029 |         // Include historical unredacted input; export must apply the normal read rule.
000030 |         let raw = "IP 203.0.113.9\nCookie: E03_SYNTHETIC_SECRET_98d11\nhttps://user:password@private.example/path";
000031 |         storage::atomic_json(
000032 |             &state.directory().join("activity.json"),
000033 |             &serde_json::json!([{"observed_at":1,"level":"info","message":raw}]),
000034 |         )
000035 |         .unwrap();
000036 |         let (path, snapshot) = state.export_activity().unwrap();
000037 |         let expected_path = state.directory().join("exports/activity-redacted.json");
000038 |         assert_eq!(std::path::Path::new(&path), expected_path);
000039 |         let bytes = storage::read_private_bytes(&expected_path, 65536).unwrap();
000040 |         let exported: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap();
000041 |         assert_eq!(exported, serde_json::to_value(&snapshot).unwrap());
000042 |         assert_eq!(
000043 |             exported,
000044 |             serde_json::to_value(state.activity().unwrap()).unwrap()
000045 |         );
000046 |         assert_eq!(snapshot[0].message, switcher_core::diagnostics::redact(raw));
000047 |         let text = String::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap();
000048 |         for secret in [
000049 |             "203.0.113.9",
000050 |             "E03_SYNTHETIC_SECRET_98d11",
000051 |             "password",
000052 |             "private.example",
000053 |         ] {
000054 |             assert!(!text.contains(secret));
000055 |         }
000056 |         assert_eq!(
000057 |             std::fs::metadata(expected_path.parent().unwrap())
000058 |                 .unwrap()

000060 |                 & 0o777,
000061 |             0o700
000062 |         );

000064 |             std::fs::metadata(&expected_path).unwrap().mode() & 0o777,
000065 |             0o600
000066 |         );
000067 |         assert_eq!(std::fs::metadata(&expected_path).unwrap().nlink(), 1);
000068 |         // A later export replaces old data, without appending a stale export event.
000069 |         state.log("info", "后续脱敏事件").unwrap();
000070 |         let (_, next) = state.export_activity().unwrap();
000071 |         assert_eq!(next.len(), 2);
000072 |         let actual: serde_json::Value = storage::read_json(&expected_path).unwrap();
000073 |         assert_eq!(actual, serde_json::to_value(next).unwrap());

000001 | //! Exercise actual desktop log persistence and preview under an isolated HOME.
000002 | #![allow(dead_code)]
000003 | #[path = "../src/bridge.rs"]
000004 | mod bridge;
000005 | #[path = "../src/update_runtime.rs"]
000006 | mod update_runtime;
000007 | 
000008 | #[test]
000009 | fn desktop_log_privacy_roundtrip() {
000010 |     if std::env::var_os("SWITCHER_E11_LOG_CHILD").is_some() {
000011 |         let state = bridge::DesktopState::new(switcher_domain::Edition::Personal).unwrap();
000012 |         let raw = "出口203.0.113.9 [2001:db8::4]\nCookie: E11_SYNTHETIC_LOGIN_COOKIE_9ef0721a\nhttps://user:E11_SYNTHETIC_ACCOUNT_A_7df3502b@private.example:443/path";
000013 |         let expected = switcher_core::diagnostics::redact(raw);
000014 |         state.log("info", raw).unwrap();
000015 |         let persisted = switcher_core::storage::read_private_bytes(
000016 |             &state.directory().join("activity.json"),
000017 |             65536,
000018 |         )
000019 |         .unwrap();
000020 |         let preview = state.activity().unwrap();
000021 |         assert_eq!(preview.len(), 1);
000022 |         assert_eq!(preview[0].message, expected);
000023 |         // App.svelte exports this same activity array with JSON.stringify.
000024 |         let exported = serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&preview).unwrap();
000025 |         let stored: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&persisted).unwrap();
000026 |         let output: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&exported).unwrap();
000027 |         assert_eq!(stored, output);
000028 |         for bytes in [&persisted, &exported] {
000029 |             let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(bytes);
000030 |             for secret in [
000031 |                 "203.0.113.9",
000032 |                 "2001:db8::4",
000033 |                 "E11_SYNTHETIC_LOGIN_COOKIE_9ef0721a",
000034 |                 "E11_SYNTHETIC_ACCOUNT_A_7df3502b",
000035 |                 "private.example",
000036 |             ] {
000037 |                 assert!(!text.contains(secret));
000038 |             }
000039 |         }
000040 |         // A historical unredacted message also receives the shared rule on read.
000041 |         let entries = serde_json::json!([{"observed_at":1,"level":"info","message":raw}]);
000042 |         switcher_core::storage::atomic_json(&state.directory().join("activity.json"), &entries)
000043 |             .unwrap();
000044 |         assert_eq!(state.activity().unwrap()[0].message, expected);
000045 |         return;
000046 |     }
000047 |     let root = std::env::temp_dir()
000048 |         .canonicalize()
000049 |         .unwrap()
000050 |         .join(format!("switcher-e11-desktop-log-{}", std::process::id()));
000051 |     switcher_core::storage::create_private_directory(&root).unwrap();
000052 |     let output = std::process::Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
000053 |         .args(["--exact", "desktop_log_privacy_roundtrip", "--nocapture"])
000054 |         .env("SWITCHER_E11_LOG_CHILD", "1")
000055 |         .env("HOME", &root)
000056 |         .output()
000057 |         .unwrap();
000058 |     let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root);
000059 |     assert!(
000060 |         output.status.success(),
000061 |         "isolated desktop log test failed: {}",
000062 |         String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
000063 |     );
000064 | }
000065 |

FILE: current/apps/desktop/src/operations-date.ts
SHA256: c5c14461ec22e6ed333c5e2ce8778d15af6f2075bdd1103df95dd0540f452d45

000001 | /** Local editing uses the live system zone; UTC editing never depends on it. */
000002 | export function systemTimeZone(): string {
000003 |   return Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone;
000004 | }
000005 | 
000006 | export function dateInput(seconds: number, timeZone: string): string {
000007 |   const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
000008 |     timeZone, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit',
000009 |     hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23',
000010 |   }).formatToParts(new Date(seconds * 1000));
000011 |   const part = (type: Intl.DateTimeFormatPartTypes) => parts.find(p => p.type === type)!.value;
000012 |   return `${part('year')}-${part('month')}-${part('day')}T${part('hour')}:${part('minute')}`;
000013 | }
000014 | 
000015 | type ParsedDate = {status: 'ok'; value: number} | {status: 'zone_changed'} | {status: 'invalid'};
000016 | 
000017 | export function parseDate(input: string, displayedZone: string, utc: boolean): ParsedDate {
000018 |   // Check the input's rendered zone, including changes queued before a refresh.
000019 |   if (!utc && displayedZone !== systemTimeZone()) return {status: 'zone_changed'};
000020 |   const milliseconds = new Date(input + (utc ? 'Z' : '')).getTime();
000021 |   if (!utc && displayedZone !== systemTimeZone()) return {status: 'zone_changed'};
000022 |   return Number.isFinite(milliseconds)
000023 |     ? {status: 'ok', value: Math.floor(milliseconds / 1000)}
000024 |     : {status: 'invalid'};
000025 | }

000047 | /** Keep keyboard focus inside a modal, then restore the original control. */
000048 | export function dialogFocus(node: HTMLElement, close: () => void) {
000049 |   const previous = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
000050 |   const controls = () => Array.from(node.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button:not(:disabled),input:not(:disabled),select:not(:disabled),textarea:not(:disabled),a[href]'));
000051 |   queueMicrotask(() => (controls()[0] ?? node).focus());
000052 |   const keydown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
000053 |     if (event.key === 'Escape') { event.preventDefault(); close(); }
000054 |     if (event.key !== 'Tab') return;
000055 |     const items=controls(), first=items[0]??node, last=items.at(-1)??node;
000056 |     if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement===first || document.activeElement===node)) { event.preventDefault();last.focus(); }
000057 |     else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement===last) { event.preventDefault();first.focus(); }
000058 |   };
000059 |   node.addEventListener('keydown', keydown);
000060 |   return { destroy() { node.removeEventListener('keydown', keydown);previous?.focus(); } };
000061 | }

000001 | //! Narrow, root-owned installation attestation. This never commits an installation.
000002 | use crate::{platform::Platform, policy::SystemActions};
000003 | use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
000004 | use std::{
000005 |     fs::{self, File, OpenOptions},
000006 |     io::{Read, Write},

000008 |     path::Path,

000010 | };

000012 | 
000013 | pub fn boot_session_uuid() -> Result<String, &'static str> {
000014 |     crate::root_operation::boot_id()
000015 | }
000016 | 
000017 | /// Fixed, public-to-owner challenge. The nonce binds a receipt; it is not a secret.
000018 | pub fn load_challenge() -> Result<InstallHealthChallenge, &'static str> {
000019 |     let path = Path::new(SOURCE_LOCAL_HEALTH_CHALLENGE);
000020 |     crate::root_operation::safe_root(path.parent().ok_or("installation_health_path")?)?;
000021 |     read_json(path, 0, 0o644)
000022 | }
000023 | 
000024 | fn read_json<T: DeserializeOwned>(path: &Path, owner: u32, mode: u32) -> Result<T, &'static str> {
000025 |     let file = OpenOptions::new()
000026 |         .read(true)
000027 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK | libc::O_CLOEXEC)
000028 |         .open(path)
000029 |         .map_err(|_| "installation_health_file")?;
000030 |     let metadata = file.metadata().map_err(|_| "installation_health_file")?;
000031 |     if !metadata.is_file()
000032 |         || metadata.uid() != owner
000033 |         || metadata.mode() & 0o7777 != mode
000034 |         || metadata.nlink() != 1
000035 |         || metadata.len() > 16384
000036 |     {
000037 |         return Err("installation_health_permissions");
000038 |     }
000039 |     let mut bytes = Vec::new();
000040 |     file.take(16385)
000041 |         .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
000042 |         .map_err(|_| "installation_health_read")?;
000043 |     if bytes.len() > 16384 {
000044 |         return Err("installation_health_size");
000045 |     }
000046 |     serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_| "installation_health_schema")
000047 | }
000048 | 
000049 | fn require_pending() -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000050 |     let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(SOURCE_LOCAL_TRANSACTION)
000051 |         .map_err(|_| "installation_health_pending")?;
000052 |     if !metadata.is_file()
000053 |         || metadata.uid() != 0
000054 |         || metadata.mode() & 0o7777 != 0o600
000055 |         || metadata.nlink() != 1
000056 |     {
000057 |         return Err("installation_health_pending");
000058 |     }
000059 |     Ok(())
000060 | }
000061 | 
000062 | pub(crate) fn verified_challenge(
000063 |     record: &InstallRecord,
000064 |     now: u64,
000065 | ) -> Result<InstallHealthChallenge, &'static str> {
000066 |     require_pending()?;
000067 |     let current = crate::platform::load_root_record(Path::new(INSTALL_RECORD))?;
000068 |     if &current != record {
000069 |         return Err("installation_health_record_changed");
000070 |     }
000071 |     let challenge = load_challenge()?;
000072 |     challenge.validate_at(record, &boot_session_uuid()?, now)?;
000073 |     Ok(challenge)
000074 | }
000075 | 
000076 | pub(crate) fn allows_pending_action(action: &HelperAction) -> bool {
000077 |     matches!(
000078 |         action,
000079 |         HelperAction::BeginSession | HelperAction::InstallationHealth { .. }
000080 |     )
000081 | }

000083 | pub(crate) fn publish_receipt(receipt: &InstallHealthReceipt) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000084 |     if unsafe { libc::geteuid() } != 0 {
000085 |         return Err("root_required");
000086 |     }
000087 |     let now = SystemTime::now()
000088 |         .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
000089 |         .map_err(|_| "clock_unavailable")?
000090 |         .as_secs();
000091 |     let current = verified_challenge(&receipt.challenge.record, now)?;
000092 |     if current != receipt.challenge {
000093 |         return Err("installation_health_challenge_changed");
000094 |     }
000095 |     receipt.validate_at(&current.record, &boot_session_uuid()?, now)?;
000096 |     let path = Path::new(SOURCE_LOCAL_HEALTH_RECEIPT);
000097 |     crate::root_operation::safe_root(path.parent().ok_or("installation_health_path")?)?;
000098 |     match fs::symlink_metadata(path) {
000099 |         Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
000100 |         Ok(m) if m.is_file() && m.uid() == 0 && m.nlink() == 1 && m.mode() & 0o7777 == 0o600 => {}
000101 |         _ => return Err("installation_health_receipt_permissions"),
000102 |     }
000103 |     let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(receipt).map_err(|_| "installation_health_encoding")?;
000104 |     atomic_receipt(path, &bytes)
000105 | }
000106 | 
000107 | fn atomic_receipt(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000108 |     // Same-directory exclusive inode, private before writing, fsync before and after rename.
000109 |     let temp = path.with_extension(format!("{}.next", Platform.random_token()?));
000110 |     let result = (|| {
000111 |         let mut file = OpenOptions::new()
000112 |             .write(true)
000113 |             .create_new(true)
000114 |             .mode(0o600)
000115 |             .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_CLOEXEC)
000116 |             .open(&temp)
000117 |             .map_err(|_| "installation_health_receipt_create")?;
000118 |         file.write_all(bytes)
000119 |             .and_then(|_| file.sync_all())
000120 |             .map_err(|_| "installation_health_receipt_write")?;
000121 |         fs::rename(&temp, path).map_err(|_| "installation_health_receipt_commit")?;
000122 |         File::open(path.parent().ok_or("installation_health_path")?)
000123 |             .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000124 |             .map_err(|_| "installation_health_receipt_sync")
000125 |     })();
000126 |     if result.is_err() {
000127 |         let _ = fs::remove_file(temp);
000128 |     }
000129 |     result
000130 | }

000132 | #[derive(Default)]
000133 | pub(crate) struct HealthTracker {
000134 |     receipt: Option<InstallHealthReceipt>,
000135 | }
000136 | impl HealthTracker {
000137 |     pub(crate) fn observe(
000138 |         &mut self,
000139 |         challenge: InstallHealthChallenge,
000140 |         record: &InstallRecord,
000141 |         role: ClientRole,
000142 |         response: (&str, &str, u64),
000143 |         now: u64,
000144 |     ) -> Result<Option<InstallHealthReceipt>, &'static str> {
000145 |         let (generation, nonce, sequence) = response;
000146 |         // The production loader independently validates the live boot and current root record.
000147 |         challenge.validate_at(record, &challenge.boot_session_uuid, now)?;
000148 |         if generation != challenge.generation || nonce != challenge.nonce {
000149 |             return Err("installation_health_binding");
000150 |         }
000151 |         match role {
000152 |             ClientRole::Gui if sequence == 0 => {}
000153 |             ClientRole::Guardian if sequence > 0 => {}
000154 |             _ => return Err("installation_health_role"),
000155 |         }
000156 |         if self
000157 |             .receipt
000158 |             .as_ref()
000159 |             .is_none_or(|r| r.challenge != challenge)
000160 |         {
000161 |             self.receipt = Some(InstallHealthReceipt {
000162 |                 schema_version: 1,
000163 |                 challenge,
000164 |                 gui_verified: false,
000165 |                 guardian_verified: false,
000166 |                 guardian_loop_sequence: 0,
000167 |                 observed_at: now,
000168 |             });
000169 |         }
000170 |         let receipt = self.receipt.as_mut().ok_or("installation_health_state")?;
000171 |         match role {
000172 |             ClientRole::Gui => receipt.gui_verified = true,
000173 |             ClientRole::Guardian => {
000174 |                 receipt.guardian_verified = true;
000175 |                 receipt.guardian_loop_sequence = sequence;
000176 |             }
000177 |             ClientRole::Installer => return Err("installation_health_role"),
000178 |         }
000179 |         receipt.observed_at = now;
000180 |         Ok((receipt.gui_verified && receipt.guardian_verified).then(|| receipt.clone()))
000181 |     }
000182 | }

000001 | fn main() {
000002 |     #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
000003 |     {
000004 |         let args: Vec<_> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
000005 |         if args == ["--probe"] {
000006 |             let (self_valid, apple_valid, forged_valid) = switcher_helper::xpc::signature_probe();
000007 |             println!(
000008 |                 "self_signature_valid={self_valid}\napple_identity_valid={apple_valid}\nsynthetic_xpc_authenticated={forged_valid}"
000009 |             );
000010 |             return;
000011 |         }
000012 |         if args.len() == 2 && args[0] == "--root-operation" {
000013 |             if args[1].len() > 16384 {
000014 |                 std::process::exit(2);
000015 |             }
000016 |             let result = serde_json::from_str(&args[1])
000017 |                 .map_err(|_| "root_operation_encoding")
000018 |                 .and_then(switcher_helper::root_operation::execute);
000019 |             if let Err(code) = result {
000020 |                 eprintln!("{code}");
000021 |                 std::process::exit(1);
000022 |             }
000023 |             return;
000024 |         }
000025 |         if !args.is_empty() {
000026 |             eprintln!("helper accepts only launchd activation or --probe");
000027 |             std::process::exit(2);
000028 |         }
000029 |         let result = switcher_helper::platform::load_root_record(std::path::Path::new(
000030 |             switcher_domain::system::INSTALL_RECORD,
000031 |         ))
000032 |         .and_then(|record| {
000033 |             if !switcher_helper::root_operation::source_local_pending()? {
000034 |                 let _root_operation = switcher_helper::root_operation::RootLock::acquire()?;
000035 |                 switcher_helper::platform::recover_pending_repair()?;
000036 |             }
000037 |             switcher_helper::xpc::serve(record)
000038 |         });
000039 |         if let Err(code) = result {
000040 |             eprintln!("helper: {code}");
000041 |             std::process::exit(1);
000042 |         }
000043 |     }
000044 |     #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
000045 |     {
000046 |         eprintln!("helper requires macOS");
000047 |         std::process::exit(1);
000048 |     }
000049 | }

000001 | //! Authorization Services boundary. External forms are secrets: never persist or log them.
000002 | //! Uses the SDK Authorization.h ABI and the built-in, non-shared administrator right.
000003 | use std::{ffi::c_void, ptr};
000004 | 
000005 | #[repr(C)]
000006 | struct Item {
000007 |     name: *const std::ffi::c_char,
000008 |     length: usize,
000009 |     value: *mut c_void,
000010 |     flags: u32,
000011 | }
000012 | #[repr(C)]
000013 | struct Rights {
000014 |     count: u32,
000015 |     items: *mut Item,
000016 | }
000017 | #[repr(C)]
000018 | struct ExternalForm {
000019 |     bytes: [u8; 32],
000020 | }
000021 | #[link(name = "Security", kind = "framework")]
000022 | unsafe extern "C" {
000023 |     fn AuthorizationCreate(
000024 |         rights: *const Rights,
000025 |         environment: *const Rights,
000026 |         flags: u32,
000027 |         auth: *mut *mut c_void,
000028 |     ) -> i32;
000029 |     fn AuthorizationCopyRights(
000030 |         auth: *mut c_void,
000031 |         rights: *const Rights,
000032 |         environment: *const Rights,
000033 |         flags: u32,
000034 |         authorized: *mut *mut Rights,
000035 |     ) -> i32;
000036 |     fn AuthorizationMakeExternalForm(auth: *mut c_void, form: *mut ExternalForm) -> i32;
000037 |     fn AuthorizationCreateFromExternalForm(
000038 |         form: *const ExternalForm,
000039 |         auth: *mut *mut c_void,
000040 |     ) -> i32;
000041 |     fn AuthorizationFree(auth: *mut c_void, flags: u32) -> i32;
000042 | }
000043 | fn admin_right() -> Item {
000044 |     Item {
000045 |         name: c"system.privilege.admin".as_ptr(),
000046 |         length: 0,
000047 |         value: ptr::null_mut(),
000048 |         flags: 0,
000049 |     }
000050 | }
000051 | /// A fresh reference per operation. Never cache this object between repairs.
000052 | pub struct Authorization(*mut c_void);
000053 | impl Drop for Authorization {
000054 |     fn drop(&mut self) {
000055 |         // Destroy shared external-form rights as well as our reference, including error paths.
000056 |         unsafe {
000057 |             AuthorizationFree(self.0, 1 << 3);
000058 |         }
000059 |     }
000060 | }
000061 | impl Authorization {
000062 |     pub fn acquire() -> Result<Self, &'static str> {
000063 |         let mut auth = ptr::null_mut();
000064 |         let status = unsafe { AuthorizationCreate(ptr::null(), ptr::null(), 0, &mut auth) };
000065 |         if status != 0 || auth.is_null() {
000066 |             return Err("authorization_unavailable");
000067 |         }
000068 |         let owned = Self(auth);
000069 |         let mut item = admin_right();
000070 |         let rights = Rights {
000071 |             count: 1,
000072 |             items: &mut item,
000073 |         };
000074 |         let status =
000075 |             unsafe { AuthorizationCopyRights(auth, &rights, ptr::null(), 1 | 2, ptr::null_mut()) };
000076 |         match status {
000077 |             0 => Ok(owned),
000078 |             -60006 => Err("authorization_cancelled"),
000079 |             _ => Err("authorization_denied"),
000080 |         }
000081 |     }
000082 |     pub fn external_form(&self) -> Result<Vec<u8>, &'static str> {
000083 |         let mut form = ExternalForm { bytes: [0; 32] };
000084 |         if unsafe { AuthorizationMakeExternalForm(self.0, &mut form) } != 0 {
000085 |             return Err("authorization_external_form");
000086 |         }
000087 |         Ok(form.bytes.to_vec())
000088 |     }
000089 | }
000090 | /// Root must only inspect granted rights: never extend rights or allow UI here.
000091 | /// Destroying the imported reference consumes this native credential, preventing replay.
000092 | pub fn consume(form: &[u8]) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000093 |     let bytes: [u8; 32] = form.try_into().map_err(|_| "authorization_invalid")?;
000094 |     let mut auth = ptr::null_mut();
000095 |     if unsafe { AuthorizationCreateFromExternalForm(&ExternalForm { bytes }, &mut auth) } != 0
000096 |         || auth.is_null()
000097 |     {
000098 |         return Err("authorization_invalid");
000099 |     }
000100 |     let _owned = Authorization(auth);
000101 |     let mut item = admin_right();
000102 |     let rights = Rights {
000103 |         count: 1,
000104 |         items: &mut item,
000105 |     };
000106 |     if unsafe { AuthorizationCopyRights(auth, &rights, ptr::null(), 0, ptr::null_mut()) } != 0 {
000107 |         return Err("authorization_denied");
000108 |     }
000109 |     Ok(())
000110 | }
000111 | 
000112 | #[cfg(test)]
000113 | mod tests {
000114 |     use super::*;
000115 |     #[test]
000116 |     fn malformed_and_forged_external_forms_are_rejected_without_interaction() {
000117 |         assert_eq!(consume(&[]), Err("authorization_invalid"));
000118 |         assert_eq!(consume(&[0; 31]), Err("authorization_invalid"));
000119 |         // The real Security.framework import rejects a synthetic token. No acquisition/UI API.
000120 |         assert_eq!(consume(&[0; 32]), Err("authorization_invalid"));
000121 |     }
000122 | }

000001 | use crate::policy::SystemActions;

000014 | fn command(program: &str, args: &[&str]) -> Result<String, &'static str> {
000015 |     #[cfg(test)]
000016 |     if let Some(result) = dns_cache::test_io::command(program, args) {
000017 |         return result;
000018 |     }
000019 |     #[cfg(test)]
000020 |     if let Some(result) = no_tun_test_io::command(program, args) {
000021 |         return result;
000022 |     }
000023 |     let mut child = Command::new(program)
000024 |         .args(args)
000025 |         .env_clear()
000026 |         .env("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin")
000027 |         .env("LC_ALL", "C")
000028 |         .stdout(Stdio::piped())
000029 |         .stderr(Stdio::null())
000030 |         .spawn()
000031 |         .map_err(|_| "system_command_spawn")?;
000032 |     let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
000033 |     loop {
000034 |         match child.try_wait() {
000035 |             Ok(Some(status)) => {
000036 |                 if !status.success() {
000037 |                     return Err("system_command_failed");
000038 |                 }
000039 |                 let output = child
000040 |                     .wait_with_output()
000041 |                     .map_err(|_| "system_command_output")?;
000042 |                 if output.stdout.len() > 65536 {
000043 |                     return Err("system_command_output_large");
000044 |                 }
000045 |                 return String::from_utf8(output.stdout).map_err(|_| "system_command_encoding");
000046 |             }
000047 |             Ok(None) if Instant::now() < deadline => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)),
000048 |             _ => {
000049 |                 let _ = child.kill();
000050 |                 let _ = child.wait();
000051 |                 return Err("system_command_timeout");
000052 |             }
000053 |         }
000054 |     }
000055 | }
000056 | pub fn validate_timezone(zone: &str) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000057 |     if zone.is_empty()
000058 |         || zone.len() > 128
000059 |         || zone.starts_with('/')
000060 |         || zone
000061 |             .split('/')
000062 |             .any(|p| p.is_empty() || p == "." || p == "..")
000063 |         || !zone
000064 |             .bytes()
000065 |             .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"/_+-".contains(&b))
000066 |     {
000067 |         return Err("timezone_invalid");
000068 |     }
000069 |     let root = Path::new("/usr/share/zoneinfo")
000070 |         .canonicalize()
000071 |         .map_err(|_| "timezone_database")?;
000072 |     let path = root
000073 |         .join(zone)
000074 |         .canonicalize()
000075 |         .map_err(|_| "timezone_unknown")?;
000076 |     if !path.starts_with(root) || !path.is_file() {
000077 |         return Err("timezone_invalid");
000078 |     }
000079 |     Ok(())
000080 | }
000081 | fn route_uses_tunnel(output: &str) -> bool {
000082 |     let mut interfaces = output
000083 |         .lines()
000084 |         .filter_map(|line| line.trim().strip_prefix("interface:"))
000085 |         .map(str::trim);
000086 |     matches!((interfaces.next(), interfaces.next()), (Some(name), None) if name.strip_prefix("utun").is_some_and(|n| !n.is_empty() && n.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())))
000087 | }
000088 | fn require_tunnel() -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000089 |     let route = command("/sbin/route", &["-n", "get", "1.1.1.1"])?;
000090 |     if route_uses_tunnel(&route) {
000091 |         Ok(())
000092 |     } else {
000093 |         Err("tun_route_required")
000094 |     }
000095 | }

000115 |     fn random_token(&self) -> Result<String, &'static str> {
000116 |         let mut bytes = [0u8; 32];
000117 |         if unsafe { libc::getentropy(bytes.as_mut_ptr().cast(), bytes.len()) } != 0 {
000118 |             return Err("entropy_unavailable");

000121 |     }
000122 |     fn validate_policy(&self, p: &RegionPolicy) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000123 |         validate_timezone(&p.timezone)?;
000124 |         if p.network_service.is_empty()
