000132 |         if !services
000133 |             .lines()
000134 |             .skip(1)
000135 |             .any(|s| s.trim_start_matches('*') == p.network_service)
000136 |         {
000137 |             return Err("network_service_unknown");
000138 |         }
000139 |         Ok(())
000140 |     }
000141 |     fn read_state(&self, service: &str) -> Result<SystemState, &'static str> {
000142 |         // Validate the service against the system inventory before using it as an argument.
000143 |         self.validate_policy(&RegionPolicy {
000144 |             timezone: "UTC".into(),
000145 |             network_service: service.into(),
000146 |             ipv6: Ipv6Policy::Off,
000147 |         })?;
000148 |         let zone = command("/usr/sbin/systemsetup", &["-gettimezone"])?;
000149 |         let timezone = zone
000150 |             .trim()
000151 |             .strip_prefix("Time Zone: ")
000152 |             .ok_or("timezone_unreadable")?
000153 |             .to_string();
000154 |         let info = command("/usr/sbin/networksetup", &["-getinfo", service])?;
000155 |         let ipv6 = if info.lines().any(|s| s == "IPv6: Off") {
000156 |             Ipv6Policy::Off
000157 |         } else if info.lines().any(|s| s == "IPv6: Automatic") {
000158 |             Ipv6Policy::Automatic
000159 |         } else {
000160 |             return Err("ipv6_unreadable");
000161 |         };
000162 |         Ok(SystemState {
000163 |             timezone,
000164 |             network_service: service.into(),
000165 |             ipv6,
000166 |         })
000167 |     }
000168 |     fn prepare_plan(&self, plan: &RepairPlan) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000169 |         if read_repair_file::<RepairPlan>("repair-journal.json")?.is_some() {
000170 |             return Err("repair_recovery_required");
000171 |         }
000172 |         let path = repair_result_path(&plan.id, true)?;
000173 |         write_completion_at(&path, plan, "repair_not_started", false, repair_owner()?)
000174 |     }
000175 |     fn plan_not_started(&self, plan: &RepairPlan) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000176 |         #[cfg(test)]
000177 |         if no_tun_test_io::not_started(plan) {
000178 |             return Ok(());
000179 |         }
000180 |         if read_repair_file::<RepairPlan>("repair-journal.json")?.is_some() {
000181 |             return Err("repair_recovery_required");
000182 |         }
000183 |         remember_repair_completion(plan, "repair_not_started")
000184 |     }
000185 |     fn recover_repair(&self, plan_id: &str) -> Result<HelperResponse, &'static str> {
000186 |         // Validate the durable owner binding before any restoration write.
000187 |         read_repair_status(plan_id)?;
000188 |         let pending: Option<RepairPlan> = read_repair_file("repair-journal.json")?;
000189 |         if let Some(plan) = pending {
000190 |             if plan.id != plan_id {
000191 |                 return Err("repair_plan_id");
000192 |             }
000193 |             recover_pending_repair()?;
000194 |         }
000195 |         read_repair_status(plan_id)
000196 |     }
000197 |     fn repair_status(&self, plan_id: &str) -> Result<HelperResponse, &'static str> {
000198 |         read_repair_status(plan_id)
000199 |     }
000200 |     fn apply_plan(&self, p: &RepairPlan) -> Vec<RepairItemResult> {
000201 |         if let Err(code) = require_tunnel() {
000202 |             let code = self.plan_not_started(p).err().unwrap_or(code);
000203 |             return vec![RepairItemResult {
000204 |                 item: "tun_route".into(),
000205 |                 changed: false,
000206 |                 restored: false,
000207 |                 error: Some(code.into()),
000208 |             }];
000209 |         }
000210 |         if let Err(code) = persist_repair(p) {
000211 |             return vec![RepairItemResult {
000212 |                 item: "transaction".into(),
000213 |                 changed: false,
000214 |                 restored: false,
000215 |                 error: Some(code.into()),
000216 |             }];
000217 |         }
000218 |         let mut results = Vec::new();
000219 |         for (item, changed) in [
000220 |             ("timezone", p.original.timezone != p.target.timezone),
000221 |             ("ipv6", p.original.ipv6 != p.target.ipv6),
000222 |         ] {
000223 |             if !changed {
000224 |                 results.push(RepairItemResult {
000225 |                     item: item.into(),
000226 |                     changed: false,
000227 |                     restored: false,
000228 |                     error: None,
000229 |                 });
000230 |                 continue;
000231 |             }
000232 |             let result = require_tunnel().and_then(|()| {
000233 |                 if item == "timezone" {
000234 |                     command(
000235 |                         "/usr/sbin/systemsetup",
000236 |                         &["-settimezone", &p.target.timezone],
000237 |                     )
000238 |                 } else {
000239 |                     command(
000240 |                         "/usr/sbin/networksetup",
000241 |                         &[
000242 |                             if p.target.ipv6 == Ipv6Policy::Off {
000243 |                                 "-setv6off"
000244 |                             } else {
000245 |                                 "-setv6automatic"
000246 |                             },
000247 |                             &p.target.network_service,
000248 |                         ],
000249 |                     )
000250 |                 }
000251 |             });
000252 |             results.push(RepairItemResult {
000253 |                 item: item.into(),
000254 |                 changed: result.is_ok(),
000255 |                 restored: false,
000256 |                 error: result.err().map(str::to_string),
000257 |             });
000258 |             if results.last().is_some_and(|r| r.error.is_some()) {
000259 |                 break;
000260 |             }
000261 |         }
000262 |         let verified = results.iter().all(|r| r.error.is_none())
000263 |             && self
000264 |                 .read_state(&p.target.network_service)
000265 |                 .is_ok_and(|s| s.timezone == p.target.timezone && s.ipv6 == p.target.ipv6);
000266 |         // A cache refresh is transient. On failure retain the journal and report the
000267 |         // already-applied system items; recovery can restore configuration, not cache contents.
000268 |         if verified && !dns_cache::refresh_after_changes(&mut results, command) {
000269 |             return results;
000270 |         }
000271 |         if verified && let Err(code) = remember_managed(p) {
000272 |             results.push(RepairItemResult {
000273 |                 item: "system_backup".into(),
000274 |                 changed: false,
000275 |                 restored: false,
000276 |                 error: Some(code.into()),
000277 |             });
000278 |             return results;
000279 |         }
000280 |         if !verified {
000281 |             for result in &mut results {
000282 |                 if result.changed {
000283 |                     let rollback = if result.item == "timezone" {
000284 |                         command(
000285 |                             "/usr/sbin/systemsetup",
000286 |                             &["-settimezone", &p.original.timezone],
000287 |                         )
000288 |                     } else {
000289 |                         command(
000290 |                             "/usr/sbin/networksetup",
000291 |                             &[
000292 |                                 if p.original.ipv6 == Ipv6Policy::Off {
000293 |                                     "-setv6off"
000294 |                                 } else {
000295 |                                     "-setv6automatic"
000296 |                                 },
000297 |                                 &p.original.network_service,
000298 |                             ],
000299 |                         )
000300 |                     };
000301 |                     result.restored = rollback.is_ok();
000302 |                 }
000303 |                 if result.error.is_none() {
000304 |                     result.error = Some("repair_verification_failed".into());
000305 |                 }
000306 |             }
000307 |         }
000308 |         let restored = self
000309 |             .read_state(&p.original.network_service)
000310 |             .is_ok_and(|s| s.timezone == p.original.timezone && s.ipv6 == p.original.ipv6);
000311 |         if (verified || restored)
000312 |             && remember_repair_completion(
000313 |                 p,
000314 |                 if verified {
000315 |                     "repair_applied"
000316 |                 } else {
000317 |                     "repair_restored"
000318 |                 },
000319 |             )
000320 |             .and_then(|()| clear_repair())
000321 |             .is_err()
000322 |         {
000323 |             results.push(RepairItemResult {
000324 |                 item: "transaction".into(),
000325 |                 changed: false,
000326 |                 restored: false,
000327 |                 error: Some("repair_journal_cleanup".into()),
000328 |             });
000329 |         }
000330 |         results
000331 |     }
000332 |     fn restore_managed_system(&self) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000333 |         restore_managed(self)
000334 |     }
000335 |     fn commit_installation(&self, uid: u32) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000336 |         use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;
000337 |         self.guardian_status(uid)?;
000338 |         let base = Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT);
000339 |         let backup = base.join("component-backup");
000340 |         let metadata =
000341 |             std::fs::symlink_metadata(&backup).map_err(|_| "installation_backup_missing")?;
000342 |         if !metadata.is_dir() || metadata.uid() != 0 || metadata.mode() & 0o077 != 0 {
000343 |             return Err("installation_backup_permissions");
000344 |         }
000345 |         let allowed = [
000346 |             "local.claudeswitcher.helper",
000347 |             "local.claudeswitcher.helper.plist",
000348 |             "local.claudeswitcher.guardian.plist",
000349 |             "switcher-guardian",
000350 |             "install.json",
000351 |         ];
000352 |         for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&backup).map_err(|_| "installation_backup_read")? {
000353 |             let entry = entry.map_err(|_| "installation_backup_read")?;
000354 |             let name = entry.file_name();
000355 |             let metadata =
000356 |                 std::fs::symlink_metadata(entry.path()).map_err(|_| "installation_backup_read")?;
000357 |             if !allowed.iter().any(|v| name == *v)
000358 |                 || !metadata.is_file()
000359 |                 || metadata.uid() != 0
000360 |                 || metadata.mode() & 0o022 != 0
000361 |             {
000362 |                 return Err("installation_backup_permissions");
000363 |             }
000364 |         }
000365 |         use std::{io::Write, os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt};
000366 |         let record = load_root_record(Path::new(INSTALL_RECORD))?;
000367 |         if record.owner_uid != uid {
000368 |             return Err("installation_owner_uid");
000369 |         }
000370 |         let temporary = base.join(".component-committed.next");
000371 |         let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
000372 |             .write(true)
000373 |             .create_new(true)
000374 |             .mode(0o600)
000375 |             .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_CLOEXEC)
000376 |             .open(&temporary)
000377 |             .map_err(|_| "installation_commit_create")?;
000378 |         let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&record).map_err(|_| "installation_commit_encode")?;
000379 |         file.write_all(&bytes)
000380 |             .and_then(|_| file.sync_all())
000381 |             .map_err(|_| "installation_commit_write")?;
000382 |         std::fs::rename(temporary, base.join("component-committed.json"))
000383 |             .map_err(|_| "installation_commit_rename")?;
000384 |         std::fs::File::open(base)
000385 |             .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000386 |             .map_err(|_| "installation_commit_sync")?;
000387 |         // The marker is the commit point. Keep the rollback snapshot until the next transaction.
000388 |         let _ = std::fs::remove_file(base.join("component-pending"));
000389 |         Ok(())
000390 |     }
000391 |     fn guardian_status(&self, uid: u32) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000392 |         command(
000393 |             "/bin/launchctl",
000394 |             &["print", &format!("gui/{uid}/{GUARDIAN_LABEL}")],
000395 |         )
000396 |         .map(|_| ())
000397 |     }
000398 | }
000399 | 
000400 | pub fn load_root_record(path: &Path) -> Result<InstallRecord, &'static str> {
000401 |     use std::os::unix::fs::{MetadataExt, OpenOptionsExt};
000402 |     let mut parent = path.parent();
000403 |     while let Some(p) = parent {
000404 |         let m = std::fs::symlink_metadata(p).map_err(|_| "install_record_parent")?;
000405 |         if !m.is_dir() || m.uid() != 0 || m.mode() & 0o022 != 0 {
000406 |             return Err("install_record_parent_permissions");
000407 |         }
000408 |         parent = p.parent();
000409 |     }
000410 |     let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
000411 |         .read(true)
000412 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK | libc::O_CLOEXEC)
000413 |         .open(path)
000414 |         .map_err(|_| "install_record_missing")?;
000415 |     let m = file.metadata().map_err(|_| "install_record_metadata")?;
000416 |     if !m.is_file() || m.uid() != 0 || m.mode() & 0o022 != 0 || m.len() > 16384 {
000417 |         return Err("install_record_permissions");
000418 |     }
000419 |     use std::io::Read;
000420 |     let mut bytes = Vec::new();
000421 |     file.take(16385)
000422 |         .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
000423 |         .map_err(|_| "install_record_read")?;
000424 |     let record: InstallRecord =
000425 |         serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_| "install_record_invalid")?;
000426 |     record.validate()?;
000427 |     Ok(record)
000428 | }

000598 | fn persist_repair(plan: &RepairPlan) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000599 |     #[cfg(test)]
000600 |     if dns_cache::test_io::journal("persist", None) {
000601 |         return Ok(());
000602 |     }
000603 |     #[cfg(test)]
000604 |     no_tun_test_io::persist_tripwire();
000605 |     use std::{io::Write, os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt};
000606 |     let base = Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT);
000607 |     let path = base.join("repair-journal.json");
000608 |     if path.exists() {
000609 |         return Err("repair_recovery_required");
000610 |     }
000611 |     let temporary = base.join(".repair-journal.next");
000612 |     let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
000613 |         .write(true)
000614 |         .create_new(true)
000615 |         .mode(0o600)
000616 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_CLOEXEC)
000617 |         .open(&temporary)
000618 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_journal_create")?;
000619 |     let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(plan).map_err(|_| "repair_journal_encode")?;
000620 |     file.write_all(&bytes)
000621 |         .and_then(|_| file.sync_all())
000622 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_journal_write")?;
000623 |     std::fs::rename(temporary, path).map_err(|_| "repair_journal_commit")?;
000624 |     std::fs::File::open(base)
000625 |         .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000626 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_journal_sync")
000627 | }
000628 | #[derive(serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
000629 | #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
000630 | struct RepairCompletion {
000631 |     owner_uid: u32,
000632 |     code: String,
000633 |     plan: RepairPlan,
000634 | }
000635 | fn repair_result_path(plan_id: &str, create: bool) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf, &'static str> {
000636 |     use std::os::unix::fs::{DirBuilderExt, MetadataExt};
000637 |     if plan_id.len() != 64 || !plan_id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
000638 |         return Err("repair_plan_id");
000639 |     }
000640 |     let directory = Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join("repair-results");
000641 |     if create {
000642 |         match std::fs::DirBuilder::new().mode(0o700).create(&directory) {
000643 |             Ok(()) => (),
000644 |             Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => (),
000645 |             Err(_) => return Err("repair_results_directory"),
000646 |         }
000647 |     }
000648 |     if create {
000649 |         std::fs::File::open(SYSTEM_ROOT)
000650 |             .and_then(|file| file.sync_all())
000651 |             .map_err(|_| "repair_results_sync")?;
000652 |     }
000653 |     let m = std::fs::symlink_metadata(&directory).map_err(|_| "repair_results_directory")?;
000654 |     if !m.is_dir() || m.file_type().is_symlink() || m.uid() != 0 || m.mode() & 0o077 != 0 {
000655 |         return Err("repair_results_permissions");
000656 |     }
000657 |     Ok(directory.join(format!("{plan_id}.json")))
000658 | }
000659 | fn write_completion_at(
000660 |     path: &Path,
000661 |     plan: &RepairPlan,
000662 |     code: &str,
000663 |     replace: bool,
000664 |     owner_uid: u32,
000665 | ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000666 |     use std::{io::Write, os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt};
000667 |     // A unique private temporary inode avoids a stale pre-rename file blocking recovery.
000668 |     let temp = path.with_extension(format!("{}.next", Platform.random_token()?));
000669 |     let target = if replace { temp.as_path() } else { path };
000670 |     let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
000671 |         .write(true)
000672 |         .create_new(true)
000673 |         .mode(0o600)
000674 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW)
000675 |         .open(target)
000676 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_completion_create")?;
000677 |     let data = serde_json::to_vec(&RepairCompletion {
000678 |         owner_uid,
000679 |         code: code.into(),
000680 |         plan: plan.clone(),
000681 |     })
000682 |     .map_err(|_| "repair_completion_encoding")?;
000683 |     file.write_all(&data)
000684 |         .and_then(|()| file.sync_all())
000685 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_completion_write")?;
000686 |     if replace {
000687 |         std::fs::rename(temp, path).map_err(|_| "repair_completion_commit")?;
000688 |     }
000689 |     std::fs::File::open(path.parent().ok_or("repair_results_directory")?)
000690 |         .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000691 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_completion_sync")
000692 | }
000693 | fn repair_owner() -> Result<u32, &'static str> {
000694 |     Ok(load_root_record(Path::new(INSTALL_RECORD))?.owner_uid)
000695 | }
000696 | fn remember_repair_completion(plan: &RepairPlan, code: &str) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000697 |     #[cfg(test)]
000698 |     if dns_cache::test_io::journal("completion", Some(code)) {
000699 |         return Ok(());
000700 |     }
000701 |     let path = repair_result_path(&plan.id, true)?;
000702 |     write_completion_at(&path, plan, code, true, repair_owner()?)
000703 | }
000704 | fn read_repair_file<T: serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(name: &str) -> Result<Option<T>, &'static str> {
000705 |     use std::{
000706 |         io::Read,
000707 |         os::unix::fs::{MetadataExt, OpenOptionsExt},
000708 |     };
000709 |     let file = match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
000710 |         .read(true)
000711 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK)
000712 |         .open(Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join(name))
000713 |     {
000714 |         Ok(file) => file,
000715 |         Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
000716 |         Err(_) => return Err("repair_status_read"),
000717 |     };
000718 |     let m = file.metadata().map_err(|_| "repair_status_metadata")?;
000719 |     if !m.is_file() || m.uid() != 0 || m.mode() & 0o077 != 0 || m.nlink() != 1 || m.len() > 16384 {
000720 |         return Err("repair_status_permissions");
000721 |     }
000722 |     let mut bytes = Vec::new();
000723 |     file.take(16385)
000724 |         .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
000725 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_status_read")?;
000726 |     serde_json::from_slice(&bytes)
000727 |         .map(Some)
000728 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_status_schema")
000729 | }
000730 | fn status_response(
000731 |     plan_id: &str,
000732 |     pending: Option<RepairPlan>,
000733 |     completion: Option<RepairCompletion>,
000734 |     system: &impl SystemActions,
000735 |     owner_uid: u32,
000736 | ) -> Result<HelperResponse, &'static str> {
000737 |     if completion
000738 |         .as_ref()
000739 |         .is_some_and(|receipt| receipt.owner_uid != owner_uid)
000740 |         || (pending.is_some() && completion.is_none())
000741 |     {
000742 |         return Err("repair_result_owner");
000743 |     }
000744 |     if completion
000745 |         .as_ref()
000746 |         .is_some_and(|receipt| receipt.plan.id != plan_id)
000747 |     {
000748 |         return Ok(HelperResponse::status("repair_unknown", false));
000749 |     }
000750 |     let (code, plan) = if let Some(plan) = pending {
000751 |         ("repair_recovery_required", plan)
000752 |     } else if let Some(receipt) = completion {
000753 |         if !matches!(
000754 |             receipt.code.as_str(),
000755 |             "repair_applied" | "repair_restored" | "repair_not_started"
000756 |         ) {
000757 |             return Err("repair_status_schema");
000758 |         }
000759 |         (
000760 |             if receipt.code == "repair_applied" {
000761 |                 "repair_applied"
000762 |             } else if receipt.code == "repair_not_started" {
000763 |                 "repair_not_started"
000764 |             } else {
000765 |                 "repair_restored"
000766 |             },
000767 |             receipt.plan,
000768 |         )
000769 |     } else {
000770 |         return Ok(HelperResponse::status("repair_unknown", false));
000771 |     };
000772 |     if plan.id != plan_id {
000773 |         return Ok(HelperResponse::status("repair_unknown", false));
000774 |     }
000775 |     let state = system.read_state(&plan.target.network_service)?;
000776 |     let mut response = HelperResponse::status(code, code != "repair_not_started");
000777 |     response.state = Some(state);
000778 |     response.plan = Some(plan);
000779 |     Ok(response)
000780 | }
000781 | fn read_repair_status(plan_id: &str) -> Result<HelperResponse, &'static str> {
000782 |     if plan_id.len() != 64 || !plan_id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
000783 |         return Err("repair_plan_id");
000784 |     }
000785 |     let pending = read_repair_file("repair-journal.json")?;
000786 |     let path = repair_result_path(plan_id, false)?;
000787 |     let name = path
000788 |         .strip_prefix(SYSTEM_ROOT)
000789 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_results_directory")?
000790 |         .to_str()
000791 |         .ok_or("repair_plan_id")?;
000792 |     let completion = read_repair_file(name)?;
000793 |     status_response(plan_id, pending, completion, &Platform, repair_owner()?)
000794 | }
000795 | fn clear_repair() -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000796 |     #[cfg(test)]
000797 |     if dns_cache::test_io::journal("clear", None) {
000798 |         return Ok(());
000799 |     }
000800 |     std::fs::remove_file(Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join("repair-journal.json"))
000801 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_journal_remove")?;
000802 |     std::fs::File::open(SYSTEM_ROOT)
000803 |         .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000804 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_journal_sync")
000805 | }
000806 | /// Resume only a root-protected repair journal. External changes cause a conflict, never overwrite.
000807 | pub fn recover_pending_repair() -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000808 |     #[cfg(test)]
000809 |     if let Some(pending) = dns_cache::test_io::pending_plan() {
000810 |         return pending.as_ref().map_or(Ok(()), recover_saved_plan);
000811 |     }
000812 |     use std::{
000813 |         io::Read,
000814 |         os::unix::fs::{MetadataExt, OpenOptionsExt},
000815 |     };
000816 |     let path = Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join("repair-journal.json");
000817 |     let file = match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
000818 |         .read(true)
000819 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK | libc::O_CLOEXEC)
000820 |         .open(path)
000821 |     {
000822 |         Ok(file) => file,
000823 |         Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(()),
000824 |         Err(_) => return Err("repair_recovery_read"),
000825 |     };
000826 |     let m = file.metadata().map_err(|_| "repair_recovery_metadata")?;
000827 |     if !m.is_file() || m.uid() != 0 || m.mode() & 0o077 != 0 || m.len() > 16384 {
000828 |         return Err("repair_recovery_permissions");
000829 |     }
000830 |     let mut bytes = Vec::new();
000831 |     file.take(16385)
000832 |         .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
000833 |         .map_err(|_| "repair_recovery_read")?;
000834 |     let p: RepairPlan = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_| "repair_recovery_schema")?;
000835 |     recover_saved_plan(&p)
000836 | }
000837 | fn recover_saved_plan(p: &RepairPlan) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000838 |     let system = Platform;
000839 |     system.validate_policy(&p.target)?;
000840 |     validate_timezone(&p.original.timezone)?;
000841 |     if p.target.network_service != p.original.network_service {
000842 |         return Err("repair_recovery_service");
000843 |     }
000844 |     let current = system.read_state(&p.target.network_service)?;
000845 |     if ![p.original.timezone.as_str(), p.target.timezone.as_str()]
000846 |         .contains(&current.timezone.as_str())
000847 |         || ![p.original.ipv6, p.target.ipv6].contains(&current.ipv6)
000848 |     {
000849 |         return Err("repair_recovery_conflict");
000850 |     }
000851 |     if current.timezone != p.original.timezone {
000852 |         command(
000853 |             "/usr/sbin/systemsetup",
000854 |             &["-settimezone", &p.original.timezone],
000855 |         )?;
000856 |     }
000857 |     if current.ipv6 != p.original.ipv6 {
000858 |         command(
000859 |             "/usr/sbin/networksetup",
000860 |             &[
000861 |                 if p.original.ipv6 == Ipv6Policy::Off {
000862 |                     "-setv6off"
000863 |                 } else {
000864 |                     "-setv6automatic"
000865 |                 },
000866 |                 &p.original.network_service,
000867 |             ],
000868 |         )?;
000869 |     }
000870 |     let restored = system.read_state(&p.original.network_service)?;
000871 |     if restored.timezone != p.original.timezone || restored.ipv6 != p.original.ipv6 {
000872 |         return Err("repair_recovery_verification");
000873 |     }
000874 |     remember_repair_completion(p, "repair_restored")?;
000875 |     clear_repair()
000876 | }
000877 | 
000878 | #[derive(serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
000879 | #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
000880 | struct ManagedSystem {
000881 |     timezone_original: String,
000882 |     timezone_last: String,
000883 |     #[serde(default)]
000884 |     timezone_guard_plan_id: Option<String>,
000885 |     services: std::collections::BTreeMap<String, (Ipv6Policy, Ipv6Policy)>,
000886 | }
000887 | fn read_managed() -> Result<Option<ManagedSystem>, &'static str> {
000888 |     use std::{
000889 |         io::Read,
000890 |         os::unix::fs::{MetadataExt, OpenOptionsExt},
000891 |     };
000892 |     let path = Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join("system-original.json");
000893 |     let file = match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
000894 |         .read(true)
000895 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK | libc::O_CLOEXEC)
000896 |         .open(path)
000897 |     {
000898 |         Ok(f) => f,
000899 |         Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
000900 |         Err(_) => return Err("system_backup_read"),
000901 |     };
000902 |     let m = file.metadata().map_err(|_| "system_backup_metadata")?;
000903 |     if !m.is_file() || m.uid() != 0 || m.mode() & 0o077 != 0 || m.len() > 16384 {
000904 |         return Err("system_backup_permissions");
000905 |     }
000906 |     let mut bytes = Vec::new();
000907 |     file.take(16385)
000908 |         .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
000909 |         .map_err(|_| "system_backup_read")?;
000910 |     serde_json::from_slice(&bytes)
000911 |         .map(Some)
000912 |         .map_err(|_| "system_backup_schema")
000913 | }
000914 | fn remember_managed(plan: &RepairPlan) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000915 |     #[cfg(test)]
000916 |     if dns_cache::test_io::journal("managed", None) {
000917 |         return Ok(());
000918 |     }
000919 |     use std::{io::Write, os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt};
000920 |     let mut state = read_managed()?.unwrap_or(ManagedSystem {
000921 |         timezone_original: plan.original.timezone.clone(),
000922 |         timezone_last: plan.original.timezone.clone(),
000923 |         timezone_guard_plan_id: None,
000924 |         services: Default::default(),
000925 |     });
000926 |     state.timezone_last = plan.target.timezone.clone();
000927 |     state.timezone_guard_plan_id = Some(plan.id.clone());
000928 |     let item = state
000929 |         .services
000930 |         .entry(plan.target.network_service.clone())
000931 |         .or_insert((plan.original.ipv6, plan.original.ipv6));
000932 |     item.1 = plan.target.ipv6;
000933 |     let base = Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT);
000934 |     let temporary = base.join(".system-original.next");
000935 |     let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
000936 |         .write(true)
000937 |         .create_new(true)
000938 |         .mode(0o600)
000939 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_CLOEXEC)
000940 |         .open(&temporary)
000941 |         .map_err(|_| "system_backup_create")?;
000942 |     let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&state).map_err(|_| "system_backup_encode")?;
000943 |     file.write_all(&bytes)
000944 |         .and_then(|_| file.sync_all())
000945 |         .map_err(|_| "system_backup_write")?;
000946 |     std::fs::rename(temporary, base.join("system-original.json"))
000947 |         .map_err(|_| "system_backup_commit")?;
000948 |     std::fs::File::open(base)
000949 |         .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000950 |         .map_err(|_| "system_backup_sync")
000951 | }
000952 | fn restore_managed(system: &Platform) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000953 |     let Some(state) = read_managed()? else {
000954 |         return Ok(());
000955 |     };
000956 |     validate_timezone(&state.timezone_original)?;
000957 |     validate_timezone(&state.timezone_last)?;
000958 |     if state.services.is_empty() || state.services.len() > 32 {
000959 |         return Err("system_backup_services");
000960 |     }
000961 |     let mut originals = Vec::new();
000962 |     for (service, (original, last)) in &state.services {
000963 |         let current = system.read_state(service)?;
000964 |         if current.timezone != state.timezone_last && current.timezone != state.timezone_original {
000965 |             return Err("uninstall_timezone_conflict");
000966 |         }
000967 |         if current.ipv6 != *last && current.ipv6 != *original {
000968 |             return Err("uninstall_ipv6_conflict");
000969 |         }
000970 |         originals.push((current, *original));
000971 |     }
000972 |     for (current, ipv6) in originals {
000973 |         let plan = RepairPlan {
000974 |             id: system.random_token()?,
000975 |             original: current.clone(),
000976 |             target: RegionPolicy {
000977 |                 timezone: state.timezone_original.clone(),
000978 |                 network_service: current.network_service.clone(),
000979 |                 ipv6,
000980 |             },
000981 |             expires_at: 0,
000982 |         };
000983 |         persist_repair(&plan)?;
000984 |         if current.timezone != plan.target.timezone {
000985 |             command(
000986 |                 "/usr/sbin/systemsetup",
000987 |                 &["-settimezone", &plan.target.timezone],
000988 |             )?;
000989 |         }
000990 |         if current.ipv6 != ipv6 {
000991 |             command(
000992 |                 "/usr/sbin/networksetup",
000993 |                 &[
000994 |                     if ipv6 == Ipv6Policy::Off {
000995 |                         "-setv6off"
000996 |                     } else {
000997 |                         "-setv6automatic"
000998 |                     },
000999 |                     &current.network_service,
001000 |                 ],
001001 |             )?;
001002 |         }
001003 |         let verified = system.read_state(&current.network_service)?;
001004 |         if verified.timezone != plan.target.timezone || verified.ipv6 != ipv6 {
001005 |             return Err("uninstall_restore_verification");
001006 |         }
001007 |         clear_repair()?;
001008 |     }
001009 |     std::fs::remove_file(Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join("system-original.json"))
001010 |         .map_err(|_| "system_backup_remove")?;
001011 |     std::fs::File::open(SYSTEM_ROOT)
001012 |         .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
001013 |         .map_err(|_| "system_backup_sync")
001014 | }
001015 | 
001016 | // These scoped boundaries exist only in the unit-test binary. No environment or IPC switch.
001017 | #[cfg(test)]
001018 | mod no_tun_test_io {
001019 |     use super::*;
001020 |     use std::cell::RefCell;
001021 | 
001022 |     #[derive(Default)]
001023 |     struct Case {
001024 |         route: Option<Result<String, &'static str>>,
001025 |         commands: Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>,
001026 |         not_started: Vec<String>,
001027 |         persist_attempts: usize,
001028 |     }
001029 |     thread_local! {
001030 |         static CASE: RefCell<Option<Case>> = const { RefCell::new(None) };
001031 |     }
001032 |     struct Reset;
001033 |     impl Drop for Reset {
001034 |         fn drop(&mut self) {
001035 |             CASE.with(|case| *case.borrow_mut() = None);
001036 |         }
001037 |     }
001038 |     fn scoped(route: Option<Result<String, &'static str>>, run: impl FnOnce()) -> Case {
001039 |         CASE.with(|case| {
001040 |             assert!(
001041 |                 case.borrow().is_none(),
001042 |                 "no-TUN test boundary must not nest"
001043 |             );
001044 |             *case.borrow_mut() = Some(Case {
001045 |                 route,
001046 |                 ..Default::default()
001047 |             });
001048 |         });
001049 |         let _reset = Reset;
001050 |         run();
001051 |         CASE.with(|case| case.borrow_mut().take().unwrap())
001052 |     }
001053 |     pub(super) fn command(program: &str, args: &[&str]) -> Option<Result<String, &'static str>> {
001054 |         CASE.with(|case| {
001055 |             let mut case = case.borrow_mut();
001056 |             let case = case.as_mut()?;
001057 |             case.commands.push((
001058 |                 program.into(),
001059 |                 args.iter().map(|arg| (*arg).into()).collect(),
001060 |             ));
001061 |             assert_eq!(
001062 |                 program, "/sbin/route",
001063 |                 "unexpected command blocked before spawn"
001064 |             );
001065 |             assert_eq!(
001066 |                 args,
001067 |                 ["-n", "get", "1.1.1.1"],
001068 |                 "unexpected argv blocked before spawn"
001069 |             );
001070 |             Some(
001071 |                 case.route
001072 |                     .clone()
001073 |                     .expect("unconfigured route boundary blocked before spawn"),
001074 |             )
001075 |         })
001076 |     }
001077 |     pub(super) fn not_started(plan: &RepairPlan) -> bool {
001078 |         CASE.with(|case| {
001079 |             let mut case = case.borrow_mut();
001080 |             let Some(case) = case.as_mut() else {
001081 |                 return false;
001082 |             };
001083 |             case.not_started.push(plan.id.clone());
001084 |             true
001085 |         })
001086 |     }
001087 |     pub(super) fn persist_tripwire() {
001088 |         CASE.with(|case| {
001089 |             if let Some(case) = case.borrow_mut().as_mut() {
001090 |                 case.persist_attempts += 1;
001091 |                 panic!("journal tripwire blocked before any filesystem access");
001092 |             }
001093 |         });
001094 |     }
001095 |     fn plan() -> RepairPlan {
001096 |         RepairPlan {
001097 |             id: "a".repeat(64),
001098 |             original: SystemState {
001099 |                 timezone: "UTC".into(),
001100 |                 network_service: "Wi-Fi".into(),
001101 |                 ipv6: Ipv6Policy::Automatic,
001102 |             },
001103 |             target: RegionPolicy {
001104 |                 timezone: "Asia/Tokyo".into(),
001105 |                 network_service: "Wi-Fi".into(),
001106 |                 ipv6: Ipv6Policy::Off,
001107 |             },
001108 |             expires_at: 100,
001109 |         }
001110 |     }
001111 |     #[test]
001112 |     fn no_tun_apply_plan_never_reaches_system_or_journal_writes() {
001113 |         let cases = [
001114 |             (Ok("interface: en0".into()), "tun_route_required"),
001115 |             (Ok(String::new()), "tun_route_required"),
001116 |             (Ok("interface: utun".into()), "tun_route_required"),
001117 |             (Ok("interface: utun2evil".into()), "tun_route_required"),
001118 |             (Ok("gateway: utun3".into()), "tun_route_required"),
001119 |             (
001120 |                 Ok("interface: utun2\ninterface: en0".into()),
001121 |                 "tun_route_required",
001122 |             ),
001123 |             (Err("system_command_failed"), "system_command_failed"),
001124 |             (Err("system_command_timeout"), "system_command_timeout"),
001125 |         ];
001126 |         for (route, expected) in cases {
001127 |             let plan = plan();
001128 |             let case = scoped(Some(route), || {
001129 |                 let results = Platform.apply_plan(&plan);
001130 |                 assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
001131 |                 assert_eq!(results[0].item, "tun_route");
001132 |                 assert!(!results[0].changed);
001133 |                 assert!(!results[0].restored);
001134 |                 assert_eq!(results[0].error.as_deref(), Some(expected));
001135 |             });
001136 |             assert_eq!(
001137 |                 case.commands,
001138 |                 [(
001139 |                     "/sbin/route".into(),
001140 |                     vec!["-n".into(), "get".into(), "1.1.1.1".into()]
001141 |                 )]
001142 |             );
001143 |             assert_eq!(case.persist_attempts, 0);
001144 |             assert_eq!(case.not_started, [plan.id]);
001145 |         }
001146 |     }
001147 |     #[test]
001148 |     fn no_tun_positive_control_trips_before_root_journal_io() {
001149 |         let case = scoped(Some(Ok("interface: utun12".into())), || {
001150 |             let panic = std::panic::catch_unwind(|| Platform.apply_plan(&plan())).unwrap_err();
001151 |             assert_eq!(
001152 |                 panic.downcast_ref::<&str>().copied(),
001153 |                 Some("journal tripwire blocked before any filesystem access")
001154 |             );
001155 |         });
001156 |         assert_eq!(case.persist_attempts, 1);
001157 |         assert_eq!(case.commands.len(), 1);
001158 |         assert!(case.not_started.is_empty());
001159 |     }
001160 |     #[test]
001161 |     fn no_tun_missing_boundary_fails_closed_and_unwind_resets_scope() {
001162 |         let panic = std::panic::catch_unwind(|| {
001163 |             scoped(None, || {
001164 |                 Platform.apply_plan(&plan());
001165 |             });
001166 |         });
001167 |         assert!(panic.is_err());
001168 |         assert!(CASE.with(|case| case.borrow().is_none()));
001169 |         let case = scoped(Some(Ok("interface: en0".into())), || {
001170 |             Platform.apply_plan(&plan());
001171 |         });
001172 |         assert_eq!(case.persist_attempts, 0);
001173 |         assert_eq!(case.not_started.len(), 1);
001174 |     }
001175 |     #[test]
001176 |     fn no_tun_scope_is_thread_local_and_denies_unexpected_commands() {
001177 |         let case = scoped(Some(Ok("interface: en0".into())), || {
001178 |             assert!(
001179 |                 std::thread::spawn(|| CASE.with(|case| case.borrow().is_none()))
001180 |                     .join()
001181 |                     .unwrap()
001182 |             );
001183 |             assert!(
001184 |                 std::panic::catch_unwind(|| {
001185 |                     command("/usr/sbin/systemsetup", &["-settimezone", "Asia/Tokyo"]);
001186 |                 })
001187 |                 .is_err()
001188 |             );
001189 |             assert!(
001190 |                 std::panic::catch_unwind(|| {
001191 |                     command("/usr/sbin/networksetup", &["-setv6off", "Wi-Fi"]);
001192 |                 })
001193 |                 .is_err()
001194 |             );
001195 |         });
001196 |         assert_eq!(case.commands.len(), 2);
001197 |         assert_eq!(case.persist_attempts, 0);
001198 |         assert!(case.not_started.is_empty());

000001 | use super::RepairItemResult;

000003 | /// Refresh only after a verified administrator repair changed persistent system settings.
000004 | /// Caller retains its repair journal when this returns false.
000005 | pub(super) fn refresh_after_changes(
000006 |     results: &mut Vec<RepairItemResult>,
000007 |     mut run: impl FnMut(&str, &[&str]) -> Result<String, &'static str>,
000008 | ) -> bool {
000009 |     if !results
000010 |         .iter()
000011 |         .any(|item| matches!(item.item.as_str(), "timezone" | "ipv6") && item.changed)
000012 |     {
000013 |         return true;
000014 |     }
000015 |     for (item, program, args) in [
000016 |         (
000017 |             "dns_cache_flush",
000018 |             "/usr/bin/dscacheutil",
000019 |             &["-flushcache"][..],
000020 |         ),
000021 |         (
000022 |             "dns_responder_reload",
000023 |             "/usr/bin/killall",
000024 |             &["-HUP", "mDNSResponder"][..],
000025 |         ),
000026 |     ] {
000027 |         let outcome = run(program, args);
000028 |         let success = outcome.is_ok();
000029 |         results.push(RepairItemResult {
000030 |             item: item.into(),
000031 |             changed: success,
000032 |             restored: false,
000033 |             error: outcome.err().map(str::to_string),
000034 |         });
000035 |         if !success {
000036 |             return false;
000037 |         }
000038 |     }
000039 |     true
000040 | }

000042 | // Scoped unit-test interception, with no runtime/environment activation surface.
000043 | #[cfg(test)]
000044 | pub(super) mod test_io {
000045 |     use super::super::*;
000046 |     use std::cell::RefCell;
000047 | 
000048 |     struct Case {
000049 |         timezone: String,
000050 |         ipv6: Ipv6Policy,
000051 |         failure: Option<&'static str>,
000052 |         commands: Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>,
000053 |         journal: Vec<String>,
000054 |         plan: RepairPlan,
000055 |         pending: bool,
000056 |     }
000057 |     thread_local! {
000058 |         static CASE: RefCell<Option<Case>> = const { RefCell::new(None) };
000059 |     }
000060 |     struct Reset;
000061 |     impl Drop for Reset {
000062 |         fn drop(&mut self) {
000063 |             CASE.with(|case| *case.borrow_mut() = None);
000064 |         }
000065 |     }
000066 |     fn scoped(changed: bool, failure: Option<&'static str>, run: impl FnOnce()) -> Case {
000067 |         CASE.with(|case| {
000068 |             assert!(case.borrow().is_none());
000069 |             *case.borrow_mut() = Some(Case {
000070 |                 timezone: if changed { "UTC" } else { "Asia/Tokyo" }.into(),
000071 |                 ipv6: Ipv6Policy::Off,
000072 |                 failure,
000073 |                 commands: Vec::new(),
000074 |                 journal: Vec::new(),
000075 |                 plan: plan(changed),
000076 |                 pending: false,
000077 |             });
000078 |         });
000079 |         let _reset = Reset;
000080 |         run();
000081 |         CASE.with(|case| case.borrow_mut().take().unwrap())
000082 |     }
000083 |     pub(crate) fn command(program: &str, args: &[&str]) -> Option<Result<String, &'static str>> {
000084 |         CASE.with(|slot| {
000085 |             let mut slot = slot.borrow_mut();
000086 |             let case = slot.as_mut()?;
000087 |             case.commands
000088 |                 .push((program.into(), args.iter().map(|s| (*s).into()).collect()));
000089 |             Some(match (program, args) {
000090 |                 ("/sbin/route", ["-n", "get", "1.1.1.1"]) => Ok("interface: utun5".into()),
000091 |                 ("/usr/sbin/networksetup", ["-listallnetworkservices"]) => {
000092 |                     Ok("Services\nWi-Fi".into())
000093 |                 }
000094 |                 ("/usr/sbin/networksetup", ["-getinfo", "Wi-Fi"]) => Ok(format!(
000095 |                     "IPv6: {}",
000096 |                     if case.ipv6 == Ipv6Policy::Off {
000097 |                         "Off"
000098 |                     } else {
000099 |                         "Automatic"
000100 |                     }
000101 |                 )),
000102 |                 ("/usr/sbin/systemsetup", ["-gettimezone"]) => {
000103 |                     Ok(format!("Time Zone: {}", case.timezone))
000104 |                 }
000105 |                 ("/usr/sbin/systemsetup", ["-settimezone", zone @ ("Asia/Tokyo" | "UTC")]) => {
000106 |                     case.timezone = (*zone).into();
000107 |                     Ok(String::new())
000108 |                 }
000109 |                 ("/usr/bin/dscacheutil", ["-flushcache"])
000110 |                 | ("/usr/bin/killall", ["-HUP", "mDNSResponder"]) => {
000111 |                     if case.failure == Some(program) {
000112 |                         Err("system_command_timeout")
000113 |                     } else {
000114 |                         Ok(String::new())
000115 |                     }
000116 |                 }
000117 |                 _ => panic!(
000118 |                     "unconfigured DNS repair command blocked before spawn: {program} {args:?}"
000119 |                 ),
000120 |             })
000121 |         })
000122 |     }
000123 |     pub(crate) fn journal(operation: &str, code: Option<&str>) -> bool {
000124 |         CASE.with(|slot| {
000125 |             let mut slot = slot.borrow_mut();
000126 |             let Some(case) = slot.as_mut() else {
000127 |                 return false;
000128 |             };
000129 |             match operation {
000130 |                 "persist" => case.pending = true,
000131 |                 "clear" => case.pending = false,
000132 |                 _ => {}
000133 |             }
000134 |             case.journal.push(code.unwrap_or(operation).into());
000135 |             true
000136 |         })
000137 |     }
000138 |     pub(crate) fn pending_plan() -> Option<Option<RepairPlan>> {
000139 |         CASE.with(|slot| {
000140 |             slot.borrow()
000141 |                 .as_ref()
000142 |                 .map(|case| case.pending.then(|| case.plan.clone()))
000143 |         })
000144 |     }

000001 | //! Reassert the configured timezone with the legacy fixed systemsetup operation.
000002 | use super::*;
000003 | 
000004 | /// Retained for older callers. Unrelated transactions do not suppress the watchdog.
000005 | pub fn require_idle_timezone_guard(_root: &Path) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000006 |     Ok(())
000007 | }
000008 | 
000009 | pub(super) fn reconcile(expected: &str) -> Result<HelperResponse, &'static str> {
000010 |     reconcile_target(expected, command)
000011 | }
000012 | 
000013 | fn reconcile_target(
000014 |     expected: &str,
000015 |     mut run: impl FnMut(&str, &[&str]) -> Result<String, &'static str>,
000016 | ) -> Result<HelperResponse, &'static str> {
000017 |     validate_timezone(expected)?;
000018 |     let read_timezone = |output: String| -> Result<String, &'static str> {
000019 |         let zone = output
000020 |             .trim()
000021 |             .strip_prefix("Time Zone: ")
000022 |             .ok_or("timezone_unreadable")?;
000023 |         validate_timezone(zone)?;
000024 |         Ok(zone.into())
000025 |     };
000026 |     let current = read_timezone(run("/usr/sbin/systemsetup", &["-gettimezone"])?)?;
000027 |     let changed = current != expected;
000028 |     if changed {
000029 |         run("/usr/sbin/systemsetup", &["-settimezone", expected])?;
000030 |         if read_timezone(run("/usr/sbin/systemsetup", &["-gettimezone"])?)? != expected {
000031 |             return Err("timezone_guard_verification");
000032 |         }
000033 |     }
000034 |     let mut response = HelperResponse::status(
000035 |         if changed {
000036 |             "timezone_guard_restored"
000037 |         } else {
000038 |             "timezone_guard_unchanged"
000039 |         },
000040 |         true,
000041 |     );
000042 |     response.items.push(RepairItemResult {
000043 |         item: "timezone".into(),
000044 |         changed,
000045 |         restored: false,
000046 |         error: None,
000047 |     });
000048 |     Ok(response)
000049 | }

000020 | /// Fixed role union for the native system peer gate; request strings never contribute.
000021 | pub(crate) fn allowed_peer_requirement(record: &InstallRecord) -> Result<String, &'static str> {
000022 |     let mut roles = vec![ClientRole::Gui, ClientRole::Guardian];
000023 |     if !record.is_source_local() {
000024 |         roles.push(ClientRole::Installer);
000025 |     }
000026 |     roles
000027 |         .into_iter()
000028 |         .map(|role| record.client_requirement(role).map(|r| format!("({r})")))
000029 |         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
000030 |         .map(|parts| parts.join(" or "))
000031 | }

000097 | 
000098 |     pub fn disconnect(&mut self, connection: usize) {
000099 |         self.sessions.remove(&connection);
000100 |     }
000101 | 
000102 |     pub fn handle(
000103 |         &mut self,
000104 |         peer: &VerifiedPeer,
000105 |         request: HelperRequest,
000106 |         now: u64,
000107 |         system: &impl SystemActions,
000108 |     ) -> HelperResponse {
000109 |         let result = self.dispatch(peer, request, now, system);
000110 |         result.unwrap_or_else(|code| HelperResponse::status(code, false))
000111 |     }
000112 | 
000113 |     fn dispatch(
000114 |         &mut self,
000115 |         peer: &VerifiedPeer,
000116 |         request: HelperRequest,
000117 |         now: u64,
000118 |         system: &impl SystemActions,
000119 |     ) -> Result<HelperResponse, &'static str> {
000120 |         if peer.uid != self.record.owner_uid {
000121 |             return Err("owner_uid");
000122 |         }
000123 |         if peer.role != request.role {
000124 |             return Err("role_identity");
000125 |         }
000126 |         self.record.client_requirement(request.role)?;
000127 |         // Expiry removes write authority, but a still-bound unconsumed plan can record a
000128 |         // definite no-start result before its session is discarded.
000129 |         if let HelperAction::ApplyRepair { plan_id } = &request.operation {
000130 |             let expired_plan = self
000131 |                 .sessions
000132 |                 .get(&peer.connection)
000133 |                 .filter(|session| {
000134 |                     session.expires_at <= now
000135 |                         && session.role == peer.role
000136 |                         && request.session.as_deref() == Some(session.token.as_str())
000137 |                         && request.sequence > session.sequence
000138 |                 })
000139 |                 .and_then(|session| session.plan.as_ref())
000140 |                 .filter(|plan| &plan.id == plan_id)
000141 |                 .cloned();
000142 |             if let Some(plan) = expired_plan {
000143 |                 system.plan_not_started(&plan)?;
000144 |                 self.sessions.remove(&peer.connection);
000145 |                 let mut response = HelperResponse::status("repair_not_started", false);
000146 |                 response.plan = Some(plan);
000147 |                 return Ok(response);
000148 |             }
000149 |         }
000150 |         self.sessions.retain(|_, session| session.expires_at > now);
000151 |         if matches!(request.operation, HelperAction::BeginSession) {
000152 |             if request.session.is_some() || request.sequence != 0 {
000153 |                 return Err("session_begin");
000154 |             }
000155 |             if self.sessions.len() >= 64 {
000156 |                 return Err("session_limit");
000157 |             }
000158 |             let token = system.random_token()?;
000159 |             self.sessions.insert(
000160 |                 peer.connection,
000161 |                 Session {
000162 |                     token: token.clone(),
000163 |                     role: peer.role,
000164 |                     expires_at: now.saturating_add(300),
000165 |                     sequence: 0,
000166 |                     plan: None,
000167 |                     authorization: None,
000168 |                 },
000169 |             );
000170 |             let mut response = HelperResponse::status("session_ready", true);
000171 |             response.session = Some(token);
000172 |             return Ok(response);
000173 |         }
000174 |         let session = self
000175 |             .sessions
000176 |             .get_mut(&peer.connection)
000177 |             .ok_or("session_missing")?;
000178 |         if request.session.as_deref() != Some(session.token.as_str()) || session.role != peer.role {
000179 |             return Err("session_binding");
000180 |         }
000181 |         if request.sequence == 0 || request.sequence <= session.sequence {
000182 |             return Err("replayed_message");
000183 |         }
000184 |         session.sequence = request.sequence;
000185 |         let mut response = HelperResponse::status("ok", true);
000186 |         match request.operation {
000187 |             HelperAction::BeginSession => unreachable!(),
000188 |             HelperAction::InstallationHealth {
000189 |                 generation,
000190 |                 nonce,
000191 |                 loop_sequence,
000192 |             } => {
000193 |                 let challenge = system.installation_health_challenge(&self.record, now)?;
000194 |                 let receipt = self.installation_health.observe(
000195 |                     challenge,
000196 |                     &self.record,
000197 |                     peer.role,
000198 |                     (&generation, &nonce, loop_sequence),
000199 |                     now,
000200 |                 )?;
000201 |                 response.code = if let Some(receipt) = receipt {
000202 |                     system.installation_health_receipt(&receipt)?;
000203 |                     "installation_health_ready"
000204 |                 } else {
000205 |                     "installation_health_pending"
000206 |                 }
000207 |                 .into();
000208 |             }
000209 |             HelperAction::ReadSystemState => response.state = Some(system.read_state("Wi-Fi")?),
000210 |             HelperAction::ReconcileManagedTimezone { expected_timezone } => {
000211 |                 if peer.role != ClientRole::Guardian {
000212 |                     return Err("role_action");
000213 |                 }
000214 |                 return system.reconcile_managed_timezone(&expected_timezone);
000215 |             }
000216 |             HelperAction::GuardianStatus => system.guardian_status(self.record.owner_uid)?,
000217 |             HelperAction::RestoreManagedSystem => {
000218 |                 if !matches!(peer.role, ClientRole::Gui | ClientRole::Installer) {
000219 |                     return Err("role_action");
000220 |                 }
000221 |                 system.restore_managed_system()?;
000222 |             }
000223 |             HelperAction::CommitInstallation => {
000224 |                 if !matches!(peer.role, ClientRole::Gui | ClientRole::Installer) {
000225 |                     return Err("role_action");
000226 |                 }
000227 |                 system.commit_installation(self.record.owner_uid)?;
000228 |             }
000229 |             HelperAction::PreviewRepair { target } => {
000230 |                 if !matches!(peer.role, ClientRole::Gui | ClientRole::Installer) {
000231 |                     return Err("role_action");
000232 |                 }
000233 |                 system.validate_policy(&target)?;
000234 |                 let plan = RepairPlan {
000235 |                     id: system.random_token()?,
000236 |                     original: system.read_state(&target.network_service)?,
000237 |                     target,
000238 |                     expires_at: now.saturating_add(30),
000239 |                 };
000240 |                 system.prepare_plan(&plan)?;
000241 |                 session.authorization = None;
000242 |                 session.plan = Some(plan.clone());
000243 |                 response.plan = Some(plan);
000244 |             }
000245 |             HelperAction::AuthorizeRepair {
000246 |                 plan_id,
000247 |                 recovery,
000248 |                 external_form,
000249 |             } => {
000250 |                 session.authorization = None;
000251 |                 if !matches!(peer.role, ClientRole::Gui | ClientRole::Installer) {
000252 |                     return Err("role_action");
000253 |                 }
000254 |                 let plan = if recovery {
000255 |                     let status = system.repair_status(&plan_id)?;
000256 |                     if status.code != "repair_recovery_required" {
000257 |                         return Err("repair_recovery_not_required");
000258 |                     }
000259 |                     status.plan.ok_or("plan_missing")?
000260 |                 } else {
000261 |                     session.plan.clone().ok_or("plan_missing")?
000262 |                 };
000263 |                 if plan.id != plan_id {
000264 |                     return Err("plan_missing");
000265 |                 }
000266 |                 if !recovery {
000267 |                     validate_plan(&plan, now, system)?;
000268 |                 }
000269 |                 system.consume_authorization(&external_form)?;
000270 |                 session.authorization =
000271 |                     Some((plan_binding(&plan)?, recovery, now.saturating_add(30)));
000272 |                 response.code = "repair_authorized".into();
000273 |             }
000274 |             HelperAction::RecoverRepair { plan_id } => {
000275 |                 if !matches!(peer.role, ClientRole::Gui | ClientRole::Installer) {
000276 |                     return Err("role_action");
000277 |                 }
000278 |                 let status = system.repair_status(&plan_id)?;
000279 |                 let plan = status.plan.ok_or("plan_missing")?;
000280 |                 if plan.id != plan_id {
000281 |                     return Err("plan_missing");
000282 |                 }
000283 |                 consume_grant(session, &plan, true, now)?;
000284 |                 return system.recover_repair(&plan_id);
000285 |             }
000286 |             HelperAction::RepairStatus { plan_id } => {
000287 |                 if !matches!(peer.role, ClientRole::Gui | ClientRole::Installer) {
000288 |                     return Err("role_action");
000289 |                 }
000290 |                 return system.repair_status(&plan_id);
000291 |             }
000292 |             HelperAction::ApplyRepair { plan_id } => {
000293 |                 if !matches!(peer.role, ClientRole::Gui | ClientRole::Installer) {
000294 |                     return Err("role_action");
000295 |                 }
000296 |                 let plan = session.plan.take().ok_or("plan_missing")?;
000297 |                 if plan.id != plan_id {
000298 |                     return Err("plan_missing");
000299 |                 }
000300 |                 let preflight: Result<(), &'static str> = (|| {
000301 |                     consume_grant(session, &plan, false, now)?;
000302 |                     validate_plan(&plan, now, system)?;
000303 |                     Ok(())
000304 |                 })();
000305 |                 if preflight.is_err() {
000306 |                     system.plan_not_started(&plan)?;
000307 |                     let mut rejected = HelperResponse::status("repair_not_started", false);
000308 |                     rejected.plan = Some(plan);
000309 |                     return Ok(rejected);
000310 |                 }
000311 |                 response.items = system.apply_plan(&plan);
000312 |                 response.ok = response.items.iter().all(|i| i.error.is_none());
000313 |                 response.code = if response.ok {
000314 |                     "applied"
000315 |                 } else {
000316 |                     "repair_failed"
000317 |                 }
000318 |                 .into();
000319 |                 if !response.ok {
000320 |                     let status = system.repair_status(&plan.id)?;
000321 |                     if status.code == "repair_not_started" {
000322 |                         return Ok(status);
000323 |                     }
000324 |                 }
000325 |                 response.state = Some(system.read_state(&plan.target.network_service)?);
000326 |                 response.plan = Some(plan);
000327 |             }
000328 |         }
000329 |         Ok(response)
000330 |     }
000331 | }
000332 | 
000333 | fn plan_binding(plan: &RepairPlan) -> Result<Vec<u8>, &'static str> {
000334 |     serde_json::to_vec(plan).map_err(|_| "plan_binding")
000335 | }
000336 | fn consume_grant(
000337 |     session: &mut Session,
000338 |     plan: &RepairPlan,
000339 |     recovery: bool,
000340 |     now: u64,
000341 | ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000342 |     let binding = plan_binding(plan)?;
000343 |     match session.authorization.take() {
000344 |         Some((bytes, scope, expiry)) if bytes == binding && scope == recovery && expiry > now => {
000345 |             Ok(())
000346 |         }
000347 |         _ => Err("repair_authorization_required"),
000348 |     }
000349 | }
000350 | fn validate_plan(
000351 |     plan: &RepairPlan,
000352 |     now: u64,
000353 |     system: &impl SystemActions,
000354 | ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000355 |     if plan.expires_at <= now {
000356 |         return Err("plan_expired");
000357 |     }
000358 |     system.validate_repair_environment(&plan.target)?;
000359 |     let current = system.read_state(&plan.target.network_service)?;
000360 |     if current.timezone != plan.original.timezone
000361 |         || current.ipv6 != plan.original.ipv6
000362 |         || current.network_service != plan.original.network_service
000363 |     {
000364 |         return Err("system_changed");
000365 |     }
000366 |     Ok(())
000367 | }

000533 |     fn native_proof_binding_rejects_invalid_expired_replayed_and_changed_plans() {
000534 |         for case in [
000535 |             "invalid",
000536 |             "expired",
000537 |             "replayed",
000538 |             "target_changed",
000539 |             "original_changed",
000540 |             "expiry_changed",
000541 |             "environment_changed",
000542 |             "other_connection",
000543 |             "other_owner",
000544 |             "other_role",
000545 |         ] {
000546 |             let system = AuthorizationBoundary::default();
000547 |             let (mut s, p) = authorization_fixture(&system);
000548 |             let mut action = authorize_action(false);
000549 |             if case == "invalid"
000550 |                 && let HelperAction::AuthorizeRepair { external_form, .. } = &mut action
000551 |             {
000552 |                 external_form.clear();
000553 |             }
000554 |             let authorized = s.handle(&p, request(p.role, action, 2), 3, &system);
000555 |             assert_eq!(authorized.ok, case != "invalid", "{case}");
000556 |             if case == "replayed" {
000557 |                 assert!(
000558 |                     !s.handle(&p, request(p.role, authorize_action(false), 3), 4, &system)
000559 |                         .ok
000560 |                 );
000561 |             }
000562 |             let plan = s
000563 |                 .sessions
000564 |                 .get_mut(&p.connection)
000565 |                 .unwrap()
000566 |                 .plan
000567 |                 .as_mut()
000568 |                 .unwrap();
000569 |             match case {
000570 |                 "target_changed" => plan.target.timezone = "Europe/London".into(),
000571 |                 "original_changed" => plan.original.timezone = "Etc/UTC".into(),
000572 |                 "expiry_changed" => plan.expires_at += 1,
000573 |                 "environment_changed" => system.environment_changed.set(true),
000574 |                 _ => (),
000575 |             }
000576 |             let mut caller = peer(ClientRole::Gui);
000577 |             match case {
000578 |                 "other_connection" => caller.connection = 2,
000579 |                 "other_owner" => caller.uid = 502,
000580 |                 "other_role" => caller.role = ClientRole::Guardian,
000581 |                 _ => (),
000582 |             }
000583 |             let now = if case == "expired" { 40 } else { 5 };
000584 |             let result = s.handle(
000585 |                 &caller,
000586 |                 request(caller.role, apply_action(), 4),
000587 |                 now,
000588 |                 &system,
000589 |             );
000590 |             assert!(!result.ok, "{case}");
000591 |             assert_eq!(system.mutations.get(), 0, "{case}");
000592 |         }
000593 |     }
000594 |     #[test]
000595 |     fn authorized_apply_is_single_use_and_recovery_requires_new_scoped_proof() {
000596 |         let system = AuthorizationBoundary::default();
000597 |         let (mut s, p) = authorization_fixture(&system);
000598 |         let plan = s.sessions.get(&p.connection).unwrap().plan.clone().unwrap();
000599 |         *system.recovery.borrow_mut() = Some(plan);
000600 |         assert!(
000601 |             s.handle(&p, request(p.role, authorize_action(false), 2), 3, &system)
000602 |                 .ok
000603 |         );
000604 |         assert!(
000605 |             s.handle(&p, request(p.role, apply_action(), 3), 4, &system)
000606 |                 .ok
000607 |         );
000608 |         assert!(
000609 |             !s.handle(&p, request(p.role, apply_action(), 4), 5, &system)
000610 |                 .ok
000611 |         );
000612 |         let recover = || HelperAction::RecoverRepair {
000613 |             plan_id: "synthetic-session-token".into(),
000614 |         };
000615 |         assert!(!s.handle(&p, request(p.role, recover(), 5), 6, &system).ok);
000616 |         assert_eq!(system.mutations.get(), 1);
000617 |         // A second, fresh native credential; cached/replayed proof was rejected above.
000618 |         system.consumed.set(false);
000619 |         assert!(
000620 |             s.handle(&p, request(p.role, authorize_action(true), 6), 7, &system)
000621 |                 .ok
000622 |         );
000623 |         assert!(s.handle(&p, request(p.role, recover(), 7), 8, &system).ok);
000624 |         assert!(!s.handle(&p, request(p.role, recover(), 8), 9, &system).ok);
000625 |         assert_eq!(system.mutations.get(), 2);
000626 |     }

000768 |         struct NeverWrite(std::cell::Cell<bool>);
000769 |         impl SystemActions for NeverWrite {

000771 |                 Fake.random_token()
000772 |             }

000774 |                 Fake.read_state(service)
000775 |             }
000776 |             fn validate_policy(&self, policy: &RegionPolicy) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000777 |                 Fake.validate_policy(policy)

000780 |                 panic!("expired plan must not mutate")
000781 |             }

000783 |                 self.0.set(true);
000784 |                 Ok(())
000785 |             }
000786 |             fn guardian_status(&self, _: u32) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000787 |                 Ok(())

000001 | //! Root-only, fixed operation runner. Its inherited flock outlives the GUI and its own parent.
000002 | use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
000003 | use std::{
000004 |     fs::{self, File, OpenOptions},
000005 |     os::{
000006 |         fd::AsRawFd,
000007 |         unix::fs::{MetadataExt, OpenOptionsExt, PermissionsExt},
000008 |     },
000009 |     path::{Path, PathBuf},
000010 |     process::Command,
000011 | };
000012 | use switcher_domain::{
000013 |     Edition,
000014 |     system::{INSTALL_RECORD, SYSTEM_ROOT},
000015 | };
000016 | #[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
000017 | #[serde(tag = "operation", rename_all = "snake_case", deny_unknown_fields)]
000018 | pub enum RootOperation {
000019 |     InstallPackage {
000020 |         source: PathBuf,
000021 |         team: String,
000022 |         window: Option<RootWindow>,
000023 |         sha256: Option<String>,
000024 |     },
000025 |     ReplaceApp {
000026 |         source: PathBuf,
000027 |         edition: Edition,
000028 |         team: String,
000029 |         old: String,
000030 |         new: String,
000031 |         window: RootWindow,
000032 |         cdhash: String,
000033 |     },

000035 |         edition: Edition,
