000041 | #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
000042 | #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
000043 | pub struct RootWindow {
000044 |     pub issued: u64,
000045 |     pub expires: u64,
000046 |     pub utc: i64,
000047 |     pub continuous_ms: u64,
000048 |     pub boot_id: String,
000049 | }
000050 | impl RootWindow {
000051 |     fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000052 |         #[repr(C)]
000053 |         struct Timebase {
000054 |             n: u32,
000055 |             d: u32,
000056 |         }
000057 |         unsafe extern "C" {
000058 |             fn mach_continuous_time() -> u64;
000059 |             fn mach_timebase_info(i: *mut Timebase) -> i32;
000060 |         }
000061 |         let mut t = Timebase { n: 0, d: 0 };
000062 |         if unsafe { mach_timebase_info(&mut t) } != 0 || t.d == 0 {
000063 |             return Err("root_clock");
000064 |         }
000065 |         let continuous = (u128::from(unsafe { mach_continuous_time() }) * u128::from(t.n)
000066 |             / u128::from(t.d)
000067 |             / 1_000_000)
000068 |             .try_into()
000069 |             .map_err(|_| "root_clock")?;
000070 |         let utc = std::time::SystemTime::now()
000071 |             .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
000072 |             .map_err(|_| "root_clock")?
000073 |             .as_secs();
000074 |         self.validate_observation(utc, continuous, &boot_id()?)
000075 |     }
000076 |     fn validate_observation(
000077 |         &self,
000078 |         utc: u64,
000079 |         continuous: u64,
000080 |         boot: &str,
000081 |     ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000082 |         if boot != self.boot_id || continuous < self.continuous_ms {
000083 |             return Err("clock_recovery_required");
000084 |         }
000085 |         let elapsed = i128::from(continuous - self.continuous_ms);
000086 |         if ((i128::from(utc) - i128::from(self.utc)) * 1000 - elapsed).abs() > 300_000 {
000087 |             return Err("clock_recovery_required");
000088 |         }
000089 |         if utc < self.issued || utc >= self.expires {
000090 |             return Err("metadata_expired");
000091 |         }
000092 |         Ok(())
000093 |     }
000094 | }
000095 | fn ensure_claude_stopped(owner: u32) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000096 |     let out = Command::new("/bin/ps")
000097 |         .args(["-ww", "-axo", "uid=,comm="])
000098 |         .env_clear()
000099 |         .env("LC_ALL", "C")
000100 |         .output()
000101 |         .map_err(|_| "process_inventory")?;
000102 |     if !out.status.success() || out.stdout.len() > 1024 * 1024 {
000103 |         return Err("process_inventory");
000104 |     }
000105 |     let text = std::str::from_utf8(&out.stdout).map_err(|_| "process_inventory")?;
000106 |     for line in text.lines() {
000107 |         let line = line.trim_start();
000108 |         let at = line.find(char::is_whitespace).ok_or("process_inventory")?;
000109 |         let uid: u32 = line[..at].parse().map_err(|_| "process_inventory")?;
000110 |         let name = line[at..].trim();
000111 |         if uid == owner && (name.contains("/Claude.app/Contents/") || name == "Claude") {
000112 |             return Err("claude_running");
000113 |         }
000114 |     }
000115 |     Ok(())
000116 | }
000117 | pub struct RootLock(File);
000118 | impl RootLock {
000119 |     pub fn acquire() -> Result<Self, &'static str> {
000120 |         if unsafe { libc::geteuid() } != 0 {
000121 |             return Err("root_required");
000122 |         }
000123 |         safe_root(Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT))?;
000124 |         let file = OpenOptions::new()
000125 |             .read(true)
000126 |             .write(true)
000127 |             .create(true)
000128 |             .mode(0o600)
000129 |             .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK)
000130 |             .open(Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join("root-operation.lock"))
000131 |             .map_err(|_| "root_transaction_lock")?;
000132 |         let meta = file.metadata().map_err(|_| "root_transaction_lock")?;
000133 |         if !meta.is_file() || meta.uid() != 0 || meta.mode() & 0o077 != 0 || meta.nlink() != 1 {
000134 |             return Err("root_transaction_lock_type");
000135 |         }
000136 |         let lock = Self::lock(file)?;
000137 |         check_source_local_transaction(Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT))?;
000138 |         check_installer_barrier()?;
000139 |         Ok(lock)
000140 |     }
000141 |     fn lock(file: File) -> Result<Self, &'static str> {
000142 |         // Keep this descriptor through exec: installer/ditto retain the same open-file lock
000143 |         // even if the GUI and this runner both die. A successor must acquire this lock too.
000144 |         if unsafe { libc::flock(file.as_raw_fd(), libc::LOCK_EX | libc::LOCK_NB) } != 0 {
000145 |             return Err("root_transaction_busy");
000146 |         }
000147 |         if unsafe { libc::fcntl(file.as_raw_fd(), libc::F_SETFD, 0) } != 0 {
000148 |             return Err("root_transaction_fd");
000149 |         }
000150 |         Ok(Self(file))
000151 |     }
000152 |     pub fn held_fd(&self) -> i32 {
000153 |         self.0.as_raw_fd()
000154 |     }
000155 | }
000156 | pub(crate) fn boot_id() -> Result<String, &'static str> {
000157 |     let out = Command::new("/usr/sbin/sysctl")
000158 |         .args(["-n", "kern.bootsessionuuid"])
000159 |         .output()
000160 |         .map_err(|_| "root_boot_id")?;
000161 |     let id = String::from_utf8(out.stdout)
000162 |         .map_err(|_| "root_boot_id")?
000163 |         .trim()
000164 |         .to_owned();
000165 |     if !out.status.success()
000166 |         || id.len() != 36
000167 |         || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit() || b == b'-')
000168 |     {
000169 |         return Err("root_boot_id");
000170 |     }
000171 |     Ok(id)
000172 | }
000173 | /// Only the independent local root installer may recover or remove this journal.
000174 | /// Presence and metadata errors block ordinary root operations without reading untrusted contents.
000175 | pub fn source_local_pending() -> Result<bool, &'static str> {
000176 |     match check_source_local_transaction(Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT)) {
000177 |         Ok(()) => Ok(false),
000178 |         Err("source_local_recovery_required") => Ok(true),
000179 |         Err(error) => Err(error),
000180 |     }
000181 | }
000182 | fn check_source_local_transaction(root: &Path) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000183 |     match fs::symlink_metadata(root.join("source-local-transaction.json")) {
000184 |         Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(()),
000185 |         _ => Err("source_local_recovery_required"),
000186 |     }
000187 | }
000188 | fn barrier_path() -> PathBuf {
000189 |     Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join("component-installer-active")
000190 | }
000191 | fn check_installer_barrier() -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000192 |     use std::io::Read;
000193 |     let path = barrier_path();
000194 |     let file = match OpenOptions::new()
000195 |         .read(true)
000196 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK)
000197 |         .open(&path)
000198 |     {
000199 |         Ok(f) => f,
000200 |         Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(()),
000201 |         Err(_) => return Err("root_installer_active"),
000202 |     };
000203 |     let m = file.metadata().map_err(|_| "root_installer_active")?;
000204 |     if !m.is_file() || m.uid() != 0 || m.mode() & 0o022 != 0 || m.len() > 64 {
000205 |         return Err("root_installer_active");
000206 |     }
000207 |     let mut id = String::new();
000208 |     file.take(65)
000209 |         .read_to_string(&mut id)
000210 |         .map_err(|_| "root_installer_active")?;
000211 |     if id.trim() == boot_id()? {
000212 |         return Err("root_installer_active");
000213 |     }
000214 |     // A prior boot's installd job cannot still own the filesystem. Its root backup remains.
000215 |     fs::remove_file(path).map_err(|_| "root_installer_barrier_clear")
000216 | }
000217 | fn start_installer_barrier() -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000218 |     use std::io::Write;
000219 |     let mut file = OpenOptions::new()
000220 |         .write(true)
000221 |         .create_new(true)
000222 |         .mode(0o644)
000223 |         .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW)
000224 |         .open(barrier_path())
000225 |         .map_err(|_| "root_installer_barrier")?;
000226 |     file.write_all(boot_id()?.as_bytes())
000227 |         .and_then(|_| file.sync_all())
000228 |         .map_err(|_| "root_installer_barrier")?;
000229 |     File::open(SYSTEM_ROOT)
000230 |         .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000231 |         .map_err(|_| "root_installer_barrier")
000232 | }
000233 | pub(crate) fn safe_root(path: &Path) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000234 |     for parent in path.ancestors() {
000235 |         let m = fs::symlink_metadata(parent).map_err(|_| "root_directory")?;
000236 |         if !m.is_dir() || m.file_type().is_symlink() || m.uid() != 0 || m.mode() & 0o022 != 0 {
000237 |             return Err("root_directory_permissions");
000238 |         }
000239 |     }
000240 |     Ok(())
000241 | }
000242 | fn run(program: &str, args: &[&std::ffi::OsStr]) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000243 |     let status = Command::new(program)
000244 |         .args(args)
000245 |         .env_clear()
000246 |         .env("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin")
000247 |         .env("LC_ALL", "C")
000248 |         .status()
000249 |         .map_err(|_| "root_command_spawn")?;
000250 |     if status.success() {
000251 |         Ok(())
000252 |     } else {
000253 |         Err("root_command_failed")
000254 |     }
000255 | }
000256 | fn team_valid(team: &str) -> bool {
000257 |     team.len() == 10
000258 |         && team
000259 |             .bytes()
000260 |             .all(|b| b.is_ascii_uppercase() || b.is_ascii_digit())
000261 | }
000262 | fn version_valid(v: &str) -> bool {
000263 |     let p: Vec<_> = v.split('.').collect();
000264 |     p.len() == 3
000265 |         && p.iter()
000266 |             .all(|x| !x.is_empty() && x.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()))
000267 | }

000280 | fn bundle(edition: Edition) -> &'static str {
000281 |     match edition {
000282 |         Edition::Personal => "local.claudeswitcher.personal",
000283 |         Edition::Public => "local.claudeswitcher.public",
000284 |     }
000285 | }
000286 | fn app_signature_args(path: &Path, edition: Edition, team: &str) -> Vec<std::ffi::OsString> {
000287 |     let requirement = format!(
000288 |         "=anchor apple generic and certificate leaf[subject.OU] = \"{team}\" and identifier \"{}\"",
000289 |         bundle(edition)
000290 |     );
000291 |     [
000292 |         "--verify",
000293 |         "--deep",
000294 |         "--strict",
000295 |         "--test-requirement",
000296 |         &requirement,
000297 |     ]
000298 |     .into_iter()
000299 |     .map(std::ffi::OsString::from)
000300 |     .chain(std::iter::once(path.as_os_str().to_owned()))
000301 |     .collect()
000302 | }

000304 | #[test]
000305 | fn root_codesign_inline_requirement_argv() {
000306 |     let path = Path::new("/isolated path/切换器.app");
000307 |     for (edition, identifier) in [
000308 |         (Edition::Personal, "local.claudeswitcher.personal"),
000309 |         (Edition::Public, "local.claudeswitcher.public"),
000310 |     ] {
000311 |         assert_eq!(app_signature_args(path, edition, "ABCDEFGHIJ"), vec![
000312 |             std::ffi::OsString::from("--verify"), "--deep".into(), "--strict".into(), "--test-requirement".into(),
000313 |             format!("=anchor apple generic and certificate leaf[subject.OU] = \"ABCDEFGHIJ\" and identifier \"{identifier}\"").into(),
000314 |             path.as_os_str().to_owned(),
000315 |         ]);
000316 |     }

000318 | 
000319 | fn verify_app(
000320 |     path: &Path,
000321 |     edition: Edition,
000322 |     team: &str,
000323 |     version: Option<&str>,
000324 | ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000325 |     let m = fs::symlink_metadata(path).map_err(|_| "root_app_missing")?;
000326 |     if !m.is_dir() || m.file_type().is_symlink() {
000327 |         return Err("root_app_type");
000328 |     }
000329 |     let args = app_signature_args(path, edition, team);
000330 |     run(
000331 |         "/usr/bin/codesign",
000332 |         &args.iter().map(|arg| arg.as_os_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
000333 |     )?;
000334 |     if let Some(version) = version {
000335 |         let out = Command::new("/usr/bin/plutil")
000336 |             .args(["-extract", "CFBundleShortVersionString", "raw", "-o", "-"])
000337 |             .arg(path.join("Contents/Info.plist"))
000338 |             .output()
000339 |             .map_err(|_| "root_app_version")?;
000340 |         if !out.status.success() || String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim() != version {
000341 |             return Err("root_app_version");
000342 |         }
000343 |     }
000344 |     Ok(())
000345 | }
000346 | fn compare_cdhash(output: &[u8], expected: &str) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000347 |     if expected.len() != 40 || !expected.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
000348 |         return Err("root_app_cdhash");
000349 |     }
000350 |     let text = std::str::from_utf8(output).map_err(|_| "root_app_cdhash")?;
000351 |     let mut hashes = text.lines().filter_map(|line| line.strip_prefix("CDHash="));
000352 |     match (hashes.next(), hashes.next()) {
000353 |         (Some(hash), None) if hash.eq_ignore_ascii_case(expected) => Ok(()),
000354 |         _ => Err("root_app_cdhash"),
000355 |     }
000356 | }
000357 | fn verify_cdhash(path: &Path, expected: &str) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000358 |     let output = Command::new("/usr/bin/codesign")
000359 |         .args(["--display", "--verbose=4"])
000360 |         .arg(path)
000361 |         .env_clear()
000362 |         .env("LC_ALL", "C")
000363 |         .output()
000364 |         .map_err(|_| "root_app_cdhash")?;
000365 |     if !output.status.success() || output.stderr.len() > 65536 {
000366 |         return Err("root_app_cdhash");
000367 |     }
000368 |     compare_cdhash(&output.stderr, expected)
000369 | }
000370 | struct PrivateStage(PathBuf);
000371 | impl PrivateStage {
000372 |     fn new() -> Result<Self, &'static str> {
000373 |         use std::ffi::{CStr, CString};
000374 |         let path = Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join(".transaction-XXXXXX");
000375 |         let mut name = CString::new(path.as_os_str().as_encoded_bytes())
000376 |             .map_err(|_| "root_stage_path")?
000377 |             .into_bytes_with_nul();
000378 |         let result = unsafe { libc::mkdtemp(name.as_mut_ptr().cast()) };
000379 |         if result.is_null() {
000380 |             return Err("root_stage_create");
000381 |         }
000382 |         let path = PathBuf::from(
000383 |             unsafe { CStr::from_ptr(result) }
000384 |                 .to_str()
000385 |                 .map_err(|_| "root_stage_path")?,
000386 |         );
000387 |         fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700))
000388 |             .map_err(|_| "root_stage_mode")?;
000389 |         safe_root(&path)?;
000390 |         Ok(Self(path))
000391 |     }
000392 | }
000393 | impl Drop for PrivateStage {
000394 |     fn drop(&mut self) {
000395 |         let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
000396 |     }
000397 | }
000398 | /// Run only after Authorization Services granted an administrator operation.
000399 | /// Ordinary XPC callers cannot submit this enum or shell commands.
000400 | pub fn execute(op: RootOperation) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000401 |     let _lock = RootLock::acquire()?;
000402 |     if Path::new(SYSTEM_ROOT).join("repair-journal.json").exists() {
000403 |         return Err("repair_recovery_required");
000404 |     }
000405 |     let team = match &op {
000406 |         RootOperation::InstallPackage { team, .. }
000407 |         | RootOperation::ReplaceApp { team, .. }
000408 |         | RootOperation::RestoreApp { team, .. } => team,
000409 |     };
000410 |     if !team_valid(team) {
000411 |         return Err("root_team");
000412 |     }
000413 |     let mut owner = fs::metadata("/dev/console")
000414 |         .map_err(|_| "root_console_owner")?
000415 |         .uid();
000416 |     if Path::new(INSTALL_RECORD).exists() {
000417 |         let record = crate::platform::load_root_record(Path::new(INSTALL_RECORD))?;
000418 |         owner = record.owner_uid;
000419 |         if record.developer_team()? != team {
000420 |             return Err("root_team_changed");
000421 |         }
000422 |     }
000423 |     match op {
000424 |         RootOperation::InstallPackage {
000425 |             source,
000426 |             team,
000427 |             window,
000428 |             sha256,
000429 |         } => {
000430 |             if !source.is_absolute() {
000431 |                 return Err("root_source");
000432 |             }
000433 |             let stage = PrivateStage::new()?;
000434 |             let pkg = stage.0.join("components.pkg");
000435 |             let input = OpenOptions::new()
000436 |                 .read(true)
000437 |                 .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK)
000438 |                 .open(&source)
000439 |                 .map_err(|_| "root_package_source")?;
000440 |             if !input
000441 |                 .metadata()
000442 |                 .map_err(|_| "root_package_source")?
000443 |                 .is_file()
000444 |             {
000445 |                 return Err("root_package_type");
000446 |             }
000447 |             let mut output = OpenOptions::new()
000448 |                 .write(true)
000449 |                 .create_new(true)
000450 |                 .mode(0o600)
000451 |                 .open(&pkg)
000452 |                 .map_err(|_| "root_package_stage")?;
000453 |             std::io::copy(
000454 |                 &mut std::io::Read::take(input, 1024 * 1024 * 1024 + 1),
000455 |                 &mut output,
000456 |             )
000457 |             .map_err(|_| "root_package_copy")?;
000458 |             if output.metadata().map_err(|_| "root_package_copy")?.len() > 1024 * 1024 * 1024 {
000459 |                 return Err("root_package_size");
000460 |             }
000461 |             output.sync_all().map_err(|_| "root_package_sync")?;
000462 |             if let Some(expected) = sha256 {
000463 |                 if expected.len() != 64 || !expected.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
000464 |                     return Err("root_package_hash");
000465 |                 }
000466 |                 let actual = Command::new("/usr/bin/shasum")
000467 |                     .args(["-a", "256"])
000468 |                     .arg(&pkg)
000469 |                     .output()
000470 |                     .map_err(|_| "root_package_hash")?;
000471 |                 if !actual.status.success()
000472 |                     || String::from_utf8_lossy(&actual.stdout)
000473 |                         .split_whitespace()
000474 |                         .next()
000475 |                         != Some(expected.as_str())
000476 |                 {
000477 |                     return Err("root_package_hash");
000478 |                 }
000479 |             }
000480 |             let proof = Command::new("/usr/sbin/pkgutil")
000481 |                 .arg("--check-signature")
000482 |                 .arg(&pkg)
000483 |                 .env("LC_ALL", "C")
000484 |                 .output()
000485 |                 .map_err(|_| "root_package_signature")?;
000486 |             if !proof.status.success()
000487 |                 || !String::from_utf8_lossy(&proof.stdout).lines().any(|l| {
000488 |                     l.contains("Developer ID Installer:")
000489 |                         && l.trim().ends_with(&format!("({team})"))
000490 |                 })
000491 |             {
000492 |                 return Err("root_package_signature");
000493 |             }
000494 |             run(
000495 |                 "/usr/sbin/spctl",
000496 |                 &[
000497 |                     "--assess".as_ref(),
000498 |                     "--type".as_ref(),
000499 |                     "install".as_ref(),
000500 |                     pkg.as_os_str(),
000501 |                 ],
000502 |             )?;
000503 |             if let Some(window) = &window {
000504 |                 window.validate()?;
000505 |             }
000506 |             ensure_claude_stopped(owner)?;
000507 |             start_installer_barrier()?;
000508 |             let result = run(
000509 |                 "/usr/sbin/installer",
000510 |                 &[
000511 |                     "-pkg".as_ref(),
000512 |                     pkg.as_os_str(),
000513 |                     "-target".as_ref(),
000514 |                     "/".as_ref(),
000515 |                 ],
000516 |             );
000517 |             // A returned installer client confirms its request completed, even on failure.
000518 |             // If this runner dies first, the signed package postinstall clears the barrier.
000519 |             let cleared = match fs::remove_file(barrier_path()) {
000520 |                 Ok(()) => Ok(()),
000521 |                 Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(()),
000522 |                 Err(_) => Err("root_installer_barrier_clear"),
000523 |             };
000524 |             result.and(cleared)
000525 |         }
000526 |         RootOperation::ReplaceApp {
000527 |             source,
000528 |             edition,
000529 |             team,
000530 |             old,
000531 |             new,
000532 |             window,
000533 |             cdhash,
000534 |         } => {
000535 |             if !version_valid(&old) || !version_valid(&new) || !source.is_absolute() {
000536 |                 return Err("root_app_parameters");
000537 |             }
000538 |             let stage = PrivateStage::new()?;
000539 |             let incoming = stage.0.join("incoming.app");
000540 |             let dest = app_path(edition);
000541 |             let backup = backup_path(edition);
000542 |             if fs::metadata("/Applications")
000543 |                 .map_err(|_| "applications")?
000544 |                 .dev()
000545 |                 != fs::metadata(&stage.0).map_err(|_| "root_stage")?.dev()
000546 |             {
000547 |                 return Err("app_same_volume_required");
000548 |             }
000549 |             verify_app(&dest, edition, &team, Some(&old))?;
000550 |             let identity = fs::symlink_metadata(&dest).map_err(|_| "root_app_metadata")?;
000551 |             run(
000552 |                 "/usr/bin/ditto",
000553 |                 &[source.as_os_str(), incoming.as_os_str()],
000554 |             )?;
000555 |             run(
000556 |                 "/usr/sbin/chown",
000557 |                 &["-R".as_ref(), "root:wheel".as_ref(), incoming.as_os_str()],
000558 |             )?;
000559 |             run(
000560 |                 "/bin/chmod",
000561 |                 &["-R".as_ref(), "go-w".as_ref(), incoming.as_os_str()],
000562 |             )?;
000563 |             verify_app(&incoming, edition, &team, Some(&new))?;
000564 |             verify_cdhash(&incoming, &cdhash)?;
000565 |             run(
000566 |                 "/usr/sbin/spctl",
000567 |                 &[
000568 |                     "--assess".as_ref(),
000569 |                     "--type".as_ref(),
000570 |                     "execute".as_ref(),
000571 |                     incoming.as_os_str(),
000572 |                 ],
000573 |             )?;
000574 |             window.validate()?;
000575 |             ensure_claude_stopped(owner)?;
000576 |             if backup.exists() {
000577 |                 verify_app(&backup, edition, &team, None)?;
000578 |                 fs::remove_dir_all(&backup).map_err(|_| "app_old_backup")?;
000579 |             }
000580 |             fs::rename(&dest, &backup).map_err(|_| "app_backup_rename")?;
000581 |             let moved = fs::symlink_metadata(&backup).map_err(|_| "app_backup_metadata")?;
000582 |             if moved.file_type().is_symlink()
000583 |                 || (moved.dev(), moved.ino()) != (identity.dev(), identity.ino())
000584 |             {
000585 |                 return Err("app_identity_changed");
000586 |             }
000587 |             fs::File::open(SYSTEM_ROOT)
000588 |                 .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000589 |                 .map_err(|_| "app_backup_sync")?;
000590 |             exclusive_rename(&incoming, &dest)?;
000591 |             File::open("/Applications")
000592 |                 .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000593 |                 .map_err(|_| "app_install_sync")
000594 |         }
000595 |         RootOperation::RestoreApp {
000596 |             edition,
000597 |             team,
000598 |             old,
000599 |             new,
000600 |         } => {
000601 |             if !version_valid(&old) || !version_valid(&new) {
000602 |                 return Err("root_app_parameters");
000603 |             }
000604 |             let dest = app_path(edition);
000605 |             let backup = backup_path(edition);
000606 |             ensure_claude_stopped(owner)?;
000607 |             if verify_app(&dest, edition, &team, Some(&old)).is_ok() {
000608 |                 return Ok(());
000609 |             }
000610 |             verify_app(&backup, edition, &team, Some(&old))?;
000611 |             if dest.exists() {
000612 |                 verify_app(&dest, edition, &team, Some(&new))?;
000613 |                 fs::remove_dir_all(&dest).map_err(|_| "app_recovery_remove")?;
000614 |             }
000615 |             exclusive_rename(&backup, &dest)?;
000616 |             File::open("/Applications")
000617 |                 .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
000618 |                 .map_err(|_| "app_recovery_sync")
000619 |         }
000620 |     }
000621 | }
000622 | #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
000623 | fn exclusive_rename(from: &Path, to: &Path) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000624 |     use std::ffi::CString;
000625 |     let from = CString::new(from.as_os_str().as_encoded_bytes()).map_err(|_| "app_path")?;
000626 |     let to = CString::new(to.as_os_str().as_encoded_bytes()).map_err(|_| "app_path")?;
000627 |     if unsafe { libc::renamex_np(from.as_ptr(), to.as_ptr(), libc::RENAME_EXCL) } == 0 {
000628 |         Ok(())
000629 |     } else {
000630 |         Err("app_destination_changed")
000631 |     }
000632 | }
000633 | #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
000634 | fn exclusive_rename(_: &Path, _: &Path) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000635 |     Err("macos_required")
000636 | }

000649 |     #[test]
000650 |     fn source_local_pending_survives_previous_boot_barrier_removal() {
000651 |         let root = source_local_fixture("cross-boot");
000652 |         let journal = root.join("source-local-transaction.json");
000653 |         let contents = br#"{"stage":"replacing","boot_id":"previous-boot"}"#;
000654 |         fs::write(&journal, contents).unwrap();
000655 |         let barrier = root.join("component-installer-active");
000656 |         fs::write(&barrier, b"previous-boot").unwrap();
000657 |         assert_eq!(
000658 |             check_source_local_transaction(&root),
000659 |             Err("source_local_recovery_required")
000660 |         );
000661 |         // Model the next boot removing its obsolete temporary installer barrier.
000662 |         fs::remove_file(&barrier).unwrap();
000663 |         assert_eq!(
000664 |             check_source_local_transaction(&root),
000665 |             Err("source_local_recovery_required")
000666 |         );
000667 |         assert_eq!(fs::read(&journal).unwrap(), contents);
000668 |         fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
000669 |     }
000670 |     #[test]

000672 |         use std::os::unix::fs::symlink;
000673 |         let root = source_local_fixture("bad-journal");
000674 |         let journal = root.join("source-local-transaction.json");
000675 |         for bytes in [b"invalid json".as_slice(), b"", b"{\"committed\":true}"] {
000676 |             fs::write(&journal, bytes).unwrap();
000677 |             assert_eq!(
000678 |                 check_source_local_transaction(&root),
000679 |                 Err("source_local_recovery_required")
000680 |             );
000681 |         }
000682 |         fs::remove_file(&journal).unwrap();
000683 |         fs::create_dir(&journal).unwrap();
000684 |         assert_eq!(
000685 |             check_source_local_transaction(&root),
000686 |             Err("source_local_recovery_required")
000687 |         );
000688 |         fs::remove_dir(&journal).unwrap();
000689 |         symlink(root.join("missing-journal-target"), &journal).unwrap();
000690 |         assert_eq!(
000691 |             check_source_local_transaction(&root),
000692 |             Err("source_local_recovery_required")
000693 |         );
000694 |         fs::remove_file(&journal).unwrap();
000695 |         let raw = std::ffi::CString::new(journal.as_os_str().as_encoded_bytes()).unwrap();
000696 |         assert_eq!(unsafe { libc::mkfifo(raw.as_ptr(), 0o600) }, 0);
000697 |         assert_eq!(
000698 |             check_source_local_transaction(&root),
000699 |             Err("source_local_recovery_required")
000700 |         );
000701 |         fs::remove_file(&journal).unwrap();
000702 |         let not_directory = root.join("invalid-parent");
000703 |         fs::write(&not_directory, b"file").unwrap();
000704 |         assert_eq!(
000705 |             check_source_local_transaction(&not_directory),
000706 |             Err("source_local_recovery_required")
000707 |         );
000708 |         fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
000709 |     }

000743 |         let path = std::env::temp_dir()
000744 |             .canonicalize()
000745 |             .unwrap()
000746 |             .join(format!("switcher-root-lock-{}", std::process::id()));
000747 |         let file = OpenOptions::new()
000748 |             .read(true)

000751 |             .mode(0o600)
000752 |             .open(&path)
000753 |             .unwrap();
000754 |         let lock = RootLock::lock(file).unwrap();
000755 |         assert_eq!(
000756 |             unsafe { libc::fcntl(lock.held_fd(), libc::F_GETFD) } & libc::FD_CLOEXEC,
000757 |             0
000758 |         );
000759 |         let mut child = Command::new("/bin/sleep").arg("0.3").spawn().unwrap();
000760 |         drop(lock);
000761 |         let competing = OpenOptions::new()
000762 |             .read(true)
000763 |             .write(true)
000764 |             .open(&path)
000765 |             .unwrap();
000766 |         assert_eq!(
000767 |             RootLock::lock(competing).err(),
000768 |             Some("root_transaction_busy")
000769 |         );
000770 |         child.wait().unwrap();
000771 |         let after = OpenOptions::new()
000772 |             .read(true)
000773 |             .write(true)
000774 |             .open(&path)
000775 |             .unwrap();
000776 |         assert!(RootLock::lock(after).is_ok());
000777 |         fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
000778 |     }

000060 | unsafe fn requirement(text: &str) -> Result<Obj, &'static str> {
000061 |     let text = CString::new(text).map_err(|_| "signature_requirement")?;
000062 |     let string =
000063 |         unsafe { CFStringCreateWithCString(std::ptr::null_mut(), text.as_ptr(), 0x08000100) };
000064 |     if string.is_null() {
000065 |         return Err("signature_requirement");
000066 |     }
000067 |     let mut requirement = std::ptr::null_mut();
000068 |     let status = unsafe { SecRequirementCreateWithString(string, 0, &mut requirement) };
000069 |     unsafe { CFRelease(string) };
000070 |     if status != 0 || requirement.is_null() {
000071 |         Err("signature_requirement")
000072 |     } else {
000073 |         Ok(requirement)
000074 |     }
000075 | }
000076 | fn version_parts(text: &str) -> Option<[u32; 3]> {
000077 |     let parts: Vec<_> = text.split('.').collect();
000078 |     if parts.len() != 3 {
000079 |         return None;
000080 |     }
000081 |     let mut out = [0; 3];
000082 |     for (i, part) in parts.iter().enumerate() {
000083 |         if part.is_empty() || !part.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
000084 |             return None;
000085 |         }
000086 |         out[i] = part.parse().ok()?;
000087 |     }
000088 |     Some(out)
000089 | }
000090 | unsafe fn minimum_version(code: Obj, minimum: &str) -> bool {
000091 |     let Some(minimum) = version_parts(minimum) else {
000092 |         return false;
000093 |     };
000094 |     unsafe {
000095 |         let mut info = std::ptr::null_mut();
000096 |         if SecCodeCopySigningInformation(code, 2, &mut info) != 0 || info.is_null() {
000097 |             return false;
000098 |         }
000099 |         let plist = CFDictionaryGetValue(info, kSecCodeInfoPList);
000100 |         let key = CFStringCreateWithCString(
000101 |             std::ptr::null_mut(),
000102 |             c"CFBundleShortVersionString".as_ptr(),
000103 |             0x08000100,
000104 |         );
000105 |         let value = if plist.is_null() || CFGetTypeID(plist) != CFDictionaryGetTypeID() {
000106 |             std::ptr::null_mut()
000107 |         } else {
000108 |             CFDictionaryGetValue(plist, key)
000109 |         };
000110 |         let mut buffer = [0i8; 64];
000111 |         let copied = !value.is_null()
000112 |             && CFGetTypeID(value) == CFStringGetTypeID()
000113 |             && CFStringGetCString(
000114 |                 value,
000115 |                 buffer.as_mut_ptr(),
000116 |                 buffer.len() as isize,
000117 |                 0x08000100,
000118 |             );
000119 |         let actual = if copied {
000120 |             std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(buffer.as_ptr())
000121 |                 .to_str()
000122 |                 .ok()
000123 |                 .and_then(version_parts)
000124 |         } else {
000125 |             None
000126 |         };
000127 |         CFRelease(key);
000128 |         CFRelease(info);
000129 |         actual.is_some_and(|version| version >= minimum)
000130 |     }
000131 | }
000132 | unsafe fn authenticate(
000133 |     message: Obj,
000134 |     connection: Obj,
000135 |     record: &InstallRecord,
000136 |     role: ClientRole,
000137 | ) -> Result<VerifiedPeer, &'static str> {
000138 |     let uid = unsafe { xpc_connection_get_euid(connection) };
000139 |     if uid != record.owner_uid {
000140 |         return Err("owner_uid");
000141 |     }
000142 |     let expression = record.client_requirement(role)?;
000143 |     let mut code = std::ptr::null_mut();
000144 |     if unsafe { SecCodeCreateWithXPCMessage(message, 0, &mut code) } != 0 || code.is_null() {
000145 |         return Err("message_audit_identity");
000146 |     }
000147 |     let req = unsafe { requirement(&expression) };
000148 |     let valid = match req {
000149 |         Ok(req) => {
000150 |             let status = unsafe { SecCodeCheckValidity(code, 0, req) };
000151 |             unsafe { CFRelease(req) };
000152 |             status == 0
000153 |         }
000154 |         Err(_) => false,
000155 |     };
000156 |     let version_ok = unsafe { minimum_version(code, &record.minimum_app_version) };
000157 |     unsafe { CFRelease(code) };
000158 |     if !version_ok {
000159 |         return Err("client_version");
000160 |     }
000161 |     if !valid {
000162 |         return Err("signature_identity");
000163 |     }
000164 |     Ok(VerifiedPeer {
000165 |         uid,
000166 |         role,
000167 |         connection: connection as usize,
000168 |     })
000169 | }
000170 | unsafe fn reply(connection: Obj, message: Obj, response: HelperResponse) {
000171 |     let output = unsafe { xpc_dictionary_create_reply(message) };
000172 |     if output.is_null() {
000173 |         return;
000174 |     }
000175 |     if let Ok(bytes) = serde_json::to_vec(&response) {
000176 |         unsafe {
000177 |             xpc_dictionary_set_data(output, c"body".as_ptr(), bytes.as_ptr().cast(), bytes.len());
000178 |             xpc_connection_send_message(connection, output);
000179 |         }
000180 |     }
000181 |     unsafe { xpc_release(output) };
000182 | }
000183 | /// Runs only the fixed launchd Mach service. It never registers or installs it.
000184 | pub fn serve(record: InstallRecord) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
000185 |     if unsafe { libc::geteuid() } != 0 {
000186 |         return Err("helper_requires_root");
000187 |     }
000188 |     record.validate()?;
000189 |     let peer_requirement = CString::new(crate::policy::allowed_peer_requirement(&record)?)
000190 |         .map_err(|_| "signature_requirement")?;
000191 |     let service = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Service::new(record.clone())?));
000192 |     let listener = unsafe {
000193 |         xpc_connection_create_mach_service(
000194 |             c"local.claudeswitcher.helper".as_ptr(),
000195 |             std::ptr::null_mut(),
000196 |             1,
000197 |         )
000198 |     };
000199 |     if listener.is_null() {
000200 |         return Err("xpc_listener_create");
000201 |     }
000202 |     let listener_handler = block2::RcBlock::new(move |connection: Obj| {
000203 |         if unsafe { xpc_get_type(connection) }
000204 |             != std::ptr::addr_of!(_xpc_type_connection).cast_mut().cast()
000205 |         {
000206 |             return;
000207 |         }
000208 |         if unsafe { xpc_connection_get_euid(connection) } != record.owner_uid {
000209 |             unsafe { xpc_connection_cancel(connection) };
000210 |             return;
000211 |         }
000212 |         // The system gates the peer before connection activation. Message checks remain supplemental.
000213 |         if unsafe {
000214 |             xpc_connection_set_peer_code_signing_requirement(connection, peer_requirement.as_ptr())
000215 |         } != 0
000216 |         {
000217 |             unsafe { xpc_connection_cancel(connection) };
000218 |             return;
000219 |         }
000220 |         let service = service.clone();
000221 |         let record = record.clone();
000222 |         let identity = connection as usize;
000223 |         let handler = block2::RcBlock::new(move |message: Obj| {
000224 |             let connection = identity as Obj;
000225 |             if unsafe { xpc_get_type(message) }
000226 |                 != std::ptr::addr_of!(_xpc_type_dictionary).cast_mut().cast()
000227 |             {
000228 |                 if let Ok(mut service) = service.lock() {
000229 |                     service.disconnect(identity);
000230 |                 }
000231 |                 return;
000232 |             }
000233 |             let mut len = 0usize;
000234 |             let bytes = unsafe { xpc_dictionary_get_data(message, c"body".as_ptr(), &mut len) };
000235 |             let response = (|| {
000236 |                 if bytes.is_null() || len > 16384 {
000237 |                     return Err("message_size");
000238 |                 }
000239 |                 let request: HelperRequest = serde_json::from_slice(unsafe {
000240 |                     std::slice::from_raw_parts(bytes.cast(), len)
000241 |                 })
000242 |                 .map_err(|_| "message_schema")?;
000243 |                 let peer = unsafe { authenticate(message, connection, &record, request.role) }?;
000244 |                 let now = SystemTime::now()
000245 |                     .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
000246 |                     .map_err(|_| "clock_unavailable")?
000247 |                     .as_secs();
000248 |                 if matches!(
000249 |                     request.operation,
000250 |                     HelperAction::ReconcileManagedTimezone { .. }
000251 |                 ) {
000252 |                     crate::platform::require_idle_timezone_guard(std::path::Path::new(
000253 |                         SYSTEM_ROOT,
000254 |                     ))?;
000255 |                 }
000256 |                 let _root_operation = if crate::root_operation::source_local_pending()?
000257 |                     && crate::installation_health::allows_pending_action(&request.operation)
000258 |                 {
000259 |                     None
000260 |                 } else {
000261 |                     Some(crate::root_operation::RootLock::acquire()?)
000262 |                 };
000263 |                 Ok(service
000264 |                     .lock()
000265 |                     .map_err(|_| "service_unavailable")?
000266 |                     .handle(&peer, request, now, &Platform))
000267 |             })()
000268 |             .unwrap_or_else(|code| HelperResponse::status(code, false));
000269 |             unsafe { reply(connection, message, response) };
000270 |         });
000271 |         unsafe {
000272 |             xpc_connection_set_event_handler(connection, &handler);
000273 |             xpc_connection_activate(connection)
000274 |         };
000275 |     });
000276 |     unsafe {
000277 |         xpc_connection_set_event_handler(listener, &listener_handler);
000278 |         xpc_connection_activate(listener);
000279 |         dispatch_main()
000280 |     }
000281 | }
000282 | /// Read-only native probe. A synthetic dictionary must never authenticate as an XPC sender.
000283 | pub fn signature_probe() -> (bool, bool, bool) {
000284 |     unsafe {
000285 |         let mut code = std::ptr::null_mut();
000286 |         let self_valid = SecCodeCopySelf(0, &mut code) == 0
000287 |             && !code.is_null()
000288 |             && SecCodeCheckValidity(code, 0, std::ptr::null_mut()) == 0;
000289 |         let apple_valid = match requirement("anchor apple generic") {
000290 |             Ok(req) => {
000291 |                 let valid = !code.is_null() && SecCodeCheckValidity(code, 0, req) == 0;
000292 |                 CFRelease(req);
000293 |                 valid
000294 |             }
000295 |             Err(_) => false,
000296 |         };
000297 |         if !code.is_null() {
000298 |             CFRelease(code);
000299 |         }
000300 |         let message = xpc_dictionary_create(std::ptr::null(), std::ptr::null(), 0);
000301 |         let mut forged = std::ptr::null_mut();
000302 |         let forged_valid = SecCodeCreateWithXPCMessage(message, 0, &mut forged) == 0;
000303 |         if !forged.is_null() {
000304 |             CFRelease(forged);
000305 |         }
000306 |         xpc_release(message);
000307 |         (self_valid, apple_valid, forged_valid)
000308 |     }
000309 | }

000323 | #[link(name = "System")]
000324 | unsafe extern "C" {
000325 |     fn xpc_connection_set_peer_code_signing_requirement(
000326 |         connection: Obj,
000327 |         requirement: *const c_char,
000328 |     ) -> i32;
000329 |     fn xpc_connection_send_message_with_reply(
000330 |         connection: Obj,

000336 | /// A live connection owns its session. Tokens cannot be reused on another connection.
000337 | pub struct NativeClient {
000338 |     connection: usize,
000339 |     role: ClientRole,
000340 |     session: Option<String>,
000341 |     sequence: u64,
000342 | }
000343 | fn request_budget(operation: &HelperAction) -> u64 {
000344 |     match operation {
000345 |         HelperAction::ApplyRepair { .. } | HelperAction::RecoverRepair { .. } => 150,
000346 |         HelperAction::RepairStatus { .. } | HelperAction::ReconcileManagedTimezone { .. } => 20,
000347 |         _ => 5,
000348 |     }
000349 | }
000350 | impl NativeClient {
000351 |     pub fn connect(record: &InstallRecord, role: ClientRole) -> Result<Self, &'static str> {
000352 |         record.validate()?;
000353 |         record.client_requirement(role)?;
000354 |         if unsafe { libc::geteuid() } != record.owner_uid {
000355 |             return Err("owner_uid");
000356 |         }
000357 |         let connection = unsafe {
000358 |             xpc_connection_create_mach_service(
000359 |                 c"local.claudeswitcher.helper".as_ptr(),
000360 |                 std::ptr::null_mut(),
000361 |                 2,
000362 |             )
000363 |         };
000364 |         if connection.is_null() {
000365 |             return Err("helper_unavailable");
000366 |         }
000367 |         let expression =
000368 |             CString::new(record.helper_requirement()?).map_err(|_| "signature_requirement")?;
000369 |         if unsafe {
000370 |             xpc_connection_set_peer_code_signing_requirement(connection, expression.as_ptr())
000371 |         } != 0
000372 |         {
000373 |             unsafe {
000374 |                 xpc_connection_cancel(connection);
000375 |                 xpc_release(connection);
000376 |             }
000377 |             return Err("helper_signature_requirement");
000378 |         }
000379 |         let handler = block2::RcBlock::new(|_: Obj| {});
000380 |         unsafe {
000381 |             xpc_connection_set_event_handler(connection, &handler);
000382 |             xpc_connection_activate(connection)
000383 |         };
000384 |         let mut client = Self {
000385 |             connection: connection as usize,
000386 |             role,
000387 |             session: None,
000388 |             sequence: 0,
000389 |         };
000390 |         let response = client.call(HelperAction::BeginSession)?;
000391 |         if !response.ok {
000392 |             return Err("helper_session_rejected");
000393 |         }
000394 |         client.session = response.session;
000395 |         if client.session.is_none() {
000396 |             return Err("helper_session_missing");
000397 |         }
000398 |         Ok(client)
000399 |     }
000400 |     pub fn call(&mut self, operation: HelperAction) -> Result<HelperResponse, &'static str> {
000401 |         // Mutation can use 25 bounded five-second commands including rollback/state reads.
000402 |         // A timeout reports an unknown outcome; cancelling this connection does not cancel root.
000403 |         let budget = request_budget(&operation);
000404 |         let begin = matches!(operation, HelperAction::BeginSession);
000405 |         if !begin {
000406 |             self.sequence = self.sequence.checked_add(1).ok_or("sequence_exhausted")?;
000407 |         }
000408 |         let request = HelperRequest {
000409 |             role: self.role,
000410 |             session: self.session.clone(),
000411 |             sequence: self.sequence,
000412 |             operation,
000413 |         };
000414 |         let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&request).map_err(|_| "request_schema")?;
000415 |         let message = unsafe { xpc_dictionary_create(std::ptr::null(), std::ptr::null(), 0) };
000416 |         if message.is_null() {
000417 |             return Err("xpc_allocation");
000418 |         }
000419 |         unsafe {
000420 |             xpc_dictionary_set_data(
000421 |                 message,
000422 |                 c"body".as_ptr(),
000423 |                 bytes.as_ptr().cast(),
000424 |                 bytes.len(),
000425 |             )
000426 |         };
000427 |         let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::sync_channel(1);
000428 |         let handler = block2::RcBlock::new(move |reply: Obj| {
000429 |             let result = (|| {
000430 |                 if unsafe { xpc_get_type(reply) }
000431 |                     != std::ptr::addr_of!(_xpc_type_dictionary).cast_mut().cast()
000432 |                 {
000433 |                     return Err("helper_unavailable");
000434 |                 }
000435 |                 let mut len = 0;
000436 |                 let bytes = unsafe { xpc_dictionary_get_data(reply, c"body".as_ptr(), &mut len) };
000437 |                 if bytes.is_null() || len > 16384 {
000438 |                     return Err("helper_response_size");
000439 |                 }
000440 |                 serde_json::from_slice(unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(bytes.cast(), len) })
000441 |                     .map_err(|_| "helper_response_schema")
000442 |             })();
000443 |             let _ = tx.try_send(result);
000444 |         });
000445 |         unsafe {
000446 |             xpc_connection_send_message_with_reply(
000447 |                 self.connection as Obj,
000448 |                 message,
000449 |                 std::ptr::null_mut(),
000450 |                 &handler,
000451 |             );
000452 |             xpc_release(message);
000453 |         }
000454 |         rx.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(budget))
000455 |             .map_err(|_| "helper_outcome_unknown")?
000456 |     }
000457 | }
000458 | impl Drop for NativeClient {
000459 |     fn drop(&mut self) {
000460 |         unsafe {
000461 |             xpc_connection_cancel(self.connection as Obj);
000462 |             xpc_release(self.connection as Obj);
000463 |         }
000464 |     }
000465 | }
